### Why this one is hard to catch

**A found defect is satisfying, locally correct, and testable.** *It closes the question by
answering it. Nothing about a true explanation announces that it is partial, and the better
the explanation the more completely it terminates the search* — **the diagnosis was right, the
mechanism was right, the fix measurably improves the number, and the feature is still not
usable.**

**The control that settles it is the same one all evening:** *run the null.* **A pair with NO
CUT, re-encoded at a different frame rate, produced a cost field 1.7× LARGER than a pair with
a five-second cut, and landed 0.4 s from where the real cut was in the other file. At shipped
defaults the two return THE SAME NUMBER.**

### The rule

    A DEFECT EXPLAINS AN OBSERVATION. IT DOES NOT ESTABLISH THAT THE OBSERVATION
    HAD ONE CAUSE.
    Before "fixing it restores the feature", run the NULL: does the fixed thing
    still say YES when the answer is NO?

**And the companion, from the agent whose result died to it:** *`stability says the
computation converged, not that the thing exists`* (`vmsam-dev-1`) — **a tie-break returns the
same wrong answer every time, and repeatability reads as confirmation.**

**Three results were killed by their own authors' controls in one evening, and this is the
first where the author killed a result the fleet had already relayed upward. That is the
system working: the cost of the retraction is paid in one message, and the cost of not
running the control would have been paid in a wired engine.**

## 14. Four kinds of staleness in one night, and each produced a confident wrong answer

**None of these was carelessness. In every one, an agent read a real thing, correctly, and
the thing had moved.**

    A TRACKING REF     the architect reported a French report to the owner.
                       `origin/dev-AI` was two commits behind; the English had
                       landed. THREE TIMES in one hour, by the agent who had
                       just written the rule about it.

    A BRIEF            dev-3 declined to propose a one-line fix, citing
                       "you write no engine code" -- a rule from a zone it had
                       not held for hours. IT DECLINED CORRECTLY. (§12)

    A CONTAINER IMAGE  forensic declared a corpus case UNCONSTRUCTIBLE. The
                       durable store that makes it constructible had merged an
                       hour earlier. Its image predated the branch.

    A STORED CONVERSION  a corpus holding audio converted by an older converter
                       tests a version nobody runs, and reports PASS doing it.

**FOUR SUBSTRATES, ONE SHAPE: A READING IS A STATEMENT ABOUT A MOMENT, AND EVERY ONE OF
THESE WAS QUOTED AS A STATEMENT ABOUT NOW.**

### Why "keep up to date" is not the rule

**Because there is no single thing to be up to date WITH.** *This fleet has at least four
references and an agent can be current on one and stale on three:*

    forgejo `dev-AI`          the published branch
    the hop                   what several agents' `origin` actually points at
    the container image       what the running code IS
    the corpus / fixtures     what a measurement was taken against

**An instruction to "work on up-to-date sources" is unenforceable because an agent cannot
tell it has failed.** *Staleness has no symptom: a stale ref answers, an old image runs, a
frozen conversion converts. Every one of them succeeds.*

### The rule that is enforceable

    NAME THE REFERENCE THE MEASUREMENT WAS TAKEN AGAINST, IN THE MEASUREMENT.

    not "the report is in French"        but "in French at 0468cf5"
    not "the case is unconstructible"    but "unconstructible on image <id>"
    not "I write no engine code"         but "per BRIEF.md as of <date>"

**A claim carrying its reference EXPIRES VISIBLY. A claim without one is indistinguishable
from a current claim forever**, which is why all four of these travelled instead of being
caught.

**And the operational half, which the owner ruled after the third instance:** *an agent
measuring what the SYSTEM can do must be on the current build.* **A statement about
capability is a statement about a specific build, and it stops being true the moment the
build moves — so the build id belongs in the statement, not in the agent's memory of which
build it was on.**

> **QUERY THE AUTHORITY, THEN QUOTE WHAT IT SAID AND WHEN.** *`ls-remote` before a claim
> about the branch; the image id beside a claim about capability; the converter revision
> beside a stored conversion. Nothing shorter is a check, and the cost of all three is one
> line each.*

## 15b. The median mismeasures a bursty filer — compare against its own MAXIMUM

**The rule "compare an agent to its OWN cadence, not to the fleet's" is right and it has a
second level that nearly produced a wrong interruption within the hour.**

*`vmsam-aide` built the cadence table using each agent's MEDIAN gap. `dev-1` came out at 4.7x
its median and read as a break. IT WAS WORKING — 0.76x its own longest silence, with its
measurements appearing by name inside another agent's filings.*

    AN AGENT THAT FILES FIVE THINGS IN TEN MINUTES AND THEN WORKS FOR AN HOUR
    HAS A MEDIAN GAP OF TWO MINUTES, SO EVERY BETWEEN-BURST GAP READS AS A BREAK.
    forensic's median is 3m and its own longest is 50m: the median flags it at x40.

    THE COMPARISON IS THE AGENT'S OWN PREVIOUS MAXIMUM -- silence longer than
    anything it has done before.

> **COMPARE AN AGENT TO ITSELF, AND THEN MAKE SURE THE STATISTIC YOU COMPARE WITH IS NOT
> ITSELF THE WRONG KIND OF AVERAGE** (`vmsam-aide`, against its own first cut).

### And the table measures ONE CHANNEL, which is the larger limit

**Filing cadence is not work.** *An agent running a long measurement files nothing and is
indistinguishable from one that stopped — `dev-3` went 88 minutes silent in the middle of its
most productive stretch.* **And the instrument watches `lead/inbox/` alone: code committed,
work in `lab/`, or anything filed elsewhere is invisible.** *`dev-4` has filed ONCE all night
and its work product is code, so it sits entirely in that shadow and its silence means
nothing.*

**The thing that collapsed "five agents look stopped" to ONE was not the cadence table — it
was crossing it against the Lead's `DISPATCHES.md`.** *Three of the five had fresh work and
their silence predated it. The one that remained was the one whose silence PREDATED ITS OWN
DISPATCH BY 195 MINUTES, which is a message-layer question and not a work one.*

    A CADENCE BREAK IS A QUESTION. A CADENCE BREAK THAT PREDATES THE AGENT'S OWN
    DISPATCH IS EVIDENCE THAT THE DISPATCH DID NOT ARRIVE OR DID NOT READ AS ONE.

## 15. A roster excludes its own holder, and that is not staleness

**The architect read its own `ListAgents`, did not find the permanent audit session
`xrdp-kde-plasma-humming-lightning`, and reported it as possibly dead. It is not dead. IT IS
THE ARCHITECT.**

    the architect's listing   the seven agents + the LEAD + four others   = 12
    the Lead's listing        the seven agents + the ARCHITECT + four     = 12

**Neither session appears in its own listing. Both rosters are correct, complete, and each is
missing exactly one entry: the one asking.**

> **"AM I IN THE ROSTER" IS UNANSWERABLE FROM INSIDE THE ROSTER.**

**This is the sixth substrate of §14 and it is categorically different from the other five.**
*A stale ref, an old image, a stale brief, a frozen conversion and a hop are all things that
MOVED. This one never moves: the instrument's blind spot is EXACTLY AND ONLY THE THING
ASKING, permanently, by construction.* **No fetch, no rebuild and no re-read fixes it,
because nothing is out of date.**

### Why it produced a confident wrong answer anyway

**The reading was correct and the inference was not.** *`ListAgents` returned twelve accurate
rows; the architect asked a question those rows cannot answer and got an answer-shaped
absence.* **`absent never zero` in its purest form: a query that structurally cannot return
you, returning without you.**

    THE RULE: A QUESTION ABOUT YOURSELF MUST BE PUT TO SOMEONE ELSE'S INSTRUMENT.
    Not a better query, not a fresher fetch -- a DIFFERENT OBSERVER.

*And it was settled in one message, by the only party that could settle it: another holder,
whose roster contains the asker precisely because it does not contain itself.*

### And the same shape, one level up, is why the audit session exists

**The permanent auditor is outside the fleet, keeps its own checkout, and its qualifications
in the wake-up prompt are earned rather than decorative:** *pull first, because it has audited
a tree hundreds of commits stale; and treat its CAUSE explanations as hypotheses, because its
FACTS have been right and its account of how a defect arose has been wrong.*

> **AN AUDITOR OUTSIDE THE FLEET IS THE ONLY INSTRUMENT WHOSE BLINDNESS IS NOT OUR
> BLINDNESS** — *and this section is that same auditor discovering it has one of its own, in
> the one place it could not look.*

## 16. Cite the REFERENCE with the section, and answer every message

**Two agents found the same defect within one hour of each other, on their first turns, and
both found it in the architect's own citations.**

### A citation crossing a staleness boundary produces a clean, correct negative

*The architect cited `WRITE_ZONES.MD` §15 to two new agents. §15 exists at `70be985` and does
not exist at `1807a27`, which is what their `origin` — the hop — serves. Either could have
answered "there is no §15" WITH A CLEAN GREP BEHIND IT.*

> **Neither of us would have been wrong, and we would have contradicted each other from two
> trees, NEITHER OF THEM NAMED.**

    CITE THE REFERENCE WITH THE SECTION: "§15 at 70be985", never "§15".

**This is not §14 restated. §14 is an agent reading its OWN stale reference and reporting
confidently. This is a citation that is TRUE AT THE SPEAKER'S REFERENCE AND ABSENT AT THE
LISTENER'S — and the listener who checks correctly gets a negative.** *Eight characters, and
it expires visibly* (`vmsam-ci-build`, `vmsam-ci-pair`, independently).

### Name the SUBSTRATE with the capability, or it reads as an artefact

**The architect told three briefs to "copy the aide's filesystem monitor, do not invent a
second dialect". `grep -rl 'inotify|watchdog|inotifywait'` over all of `VMSAM_HELP_AI/`
returns NOTHING** (`vmsam-ci-pair`, first turn). **There is no monitor to copy.**

*The aide had said it ran "a self-paced loop with a filesystem monitor on `lead/inbox/`".
Every word is true of its BEHAVIOUR and none of it is true of the REPOSITORY: it is a HARNESS
feature — a background task in its runtime that delivers arrival events as notifications. Not
a file, not a daemon, not obtainable by reading anything it has written.*

> ***A CAPABILITY DESCRIBED WITHOUT ITS SUBSTRATE READS AS AN ARTEFACT. "I have a monitor"
> and "my runtime notifies me" are identical sentences and completely different instructions
> to a third party*** (`vmsam-aide`, against its own description).

    WHEN YOU SAY WHAT YOU CAN DO, SAY WHAT PROVIDES IT.
    a file, a running process, a harness feature, or a habit -- they are
    four different things and only the first two can be copied.

**And the cost was three briefs instructing three agents to reproduce something that does not
exist — where the instruction's WHOLE PURPOSE was to prevent divergence.** *The first agent
that gives up and writes one becomes the dialect the other two were told not to invent.*

**It has already happened, and the outcome is better than the instruction it replaced:
`ci-build/watch_inbox.py` exists.** *`inotifywait` is not installed, `watchdog` does not
import, `inotify_simple` does not import — so it calls `inotify(7)` THROUGH LIBC VIA
`ctypes`, stdlib only, and it does an INITIAL SCAN AT STARTUP so a message that arrived while
nobody was watching is not missed.*

> **A DIALECT CHOSEN BY WHOEVER WROTE FIRST IS NOT AUTOMATICALLY WRONG. IT IS AUTOMATICALLY
> UNDECLARED** (`vmsam-aide`). *Two agents are still holding because they asked instead of
> writing, and that is the only reason it is still a decision.*

### Every message in an inbox is answered, whether or not it asks anything

**Owner's ruling, 2026-09-05:**

> ***"Un message demandera TOUJOURS une réponse, même si elle n'est pas explicitée."***

**The reason is measured and it is `vmsam-forensic`'s:** *"from my side, NO REPLY and NEVER
ARRIVED are one observation."* **A reply is the only thing that separates them** — *and
tonight the architect's answer had arrived while the paired FILE was structurally impossible,
so the sender was inferring a channel state from a missing artefact that could never exist.*

    A MESSAGE WITH NO REPLY IS INDISTINGUISHABLE FROM A MESSAGE THAT NEVER LANDED.
    "Received, nothing needed" is a complete answer. Silence is not.

*And an unanswered message is worse than an unread one: the sender plans around a delivery
that may not have happened, and the failure surfaces hours later as a decision taken on an
assumption nobody made out loud.*

## 17. A register CARRIES a dispatch; it never CREATES one — and its silence is not evidence

**The architect proposed widening the aide's mandate so its open-questions register would
become canonical, "every row naming WHO OWES IT". The aide refused, and quoted its own
`REGISTER.md` header back:**

> ***"It records that a question was ASKED, and BY WHOM. It never records that it is OWED.
> No priority column, no ranking, no 'X owes Y'. The moment it carries a judgement about what
> matters it is A DISPATCHER WEARING A DOCUMENT'S CLOTHES, and that judgement is the Lead's."***

**"Who owes it" is the exact string that constraint forbids.** *A row saying "dev-1 owes this"
IS a dispatch — published, standing, with the aide's name on it, read by ten agents including
the one named.*

    THE VERSION THAT DELIVERS THE SAME VALUE:
    A ROW NAMES THE ASSIGNEE **AS RECORDED**, WITH ITS SOURCE --
    "ci, DISPATCHES.md 22:3xZ" -- OR IT SAYS **NO OWNER RECORDED**.
    IT CARRIES A DISPATCH; IT NEVER CREATES ONE.

**That is a CITATION, not a claim.** *The difference between CARRIED and ASSERTED is the whole
of the instrument's usefulness: the day the register asserts, its rows stop being checkable
against a surface.*

### And making a register canonical makes its SILENCE mean something

**The hazard the architect had not priced, and it is `absent never zero` arriving inside the
document that exists to prevent it:**

> **Today, a question absent from the register means "I did not see it". CANONICAL, agents
> will read absence as "NOT OPEN".**

    IF IT BECOMES CANONICAL IT MUST SAY SO ON ITS FACE, ABOVE THE ROWS:
    ABSENCE FROM THIS FILE IS NOT EVIDENCE A QUESTION IS CLOSED OR WAS NEVER ASKED.

*Every detector is a floor, and a register is a detector.*

### Routing is dispatch under another verb unless BOTH halves are already on a surface

**"Route a question to whoever can answer it" reads, to a narrow-mandate agent, as "FIND a
question to route" — the same failure as "continue by default" reading as "find something to
continue with".** *And the deeper half:* **DECIDING WHO CAN ANSWER A QUESTION IS A JUDGEMENT
ABOUT ANOTHER AGENT'S COMPETENCE AND CURRENT LOAD, WHICH IS DISPATCH IN EVERYTHING BUT THE
VERB.** *Saying "routed, not dispatched" does not change what the receiver does with it.*

    ROUTE A QUESTION THE SWEEP HAS **ALREADY SURFACED**, TO AN ANSWERER
    **ALREADY NAMED** ON A SURFACE THE ROUTER CAN READ. IF EITHER HALF IS
    MISSING, REPORT THAT AND DO NOT SUPPLY IT.

**And every routed message carries the surface it came from — "routed; the answerer was named
in `<file>` at `<stamp>`" — so a wrong route is falsifiable BY ITS RECIPIENT rather than by
the Lead noticing later.**

> ***THE RECEIPT PROTOCOL WORKS BECAUSE IT DOES NOT HELP. Routing should work the same way:
> checkable rather than authoritative*** (`vmsam-aide`).

### Closing a question is bounded by the same rule, and it has a measured instance

**Close only when the answering filing ADDRESSES THE QUESTION ITSELF, never when the router
judges that it does. A closed row stays readable with its citation, and anyone may reopen it
without asking.**

*The instance is eighteen minutes old at the time of writing: the aide told the Lead that a
7.1 % figure was the number to send upward, because it was a proof rather than a calibrated
threshold.* **It was a proof about the WRONG POPULATION and `vmsam-forensic` refuted the draw
within the hour.** *Had it CLOSED on that instead of reporting it, the row would have stopped
being visible while the error travelled.*

## 18. Measure the INPUTS before explaining a difference between two instruments' results

**Two agents spent a night explaining why a cut was detectable on one fixture and invisible
on another. Seven mechanisms were proposed and killed: media type, bit-identity, generation,
seam, keyframe alignment, band absorption, doubling.**

    THE ANSWER WAS A SOURCE-LEVEL PROPERTY, MEASURABLE IN ONE PASS AT THE START.

    dev-1's source   2196 distinct hash values
    dev-3's source    441

    sources above 1000 distinct   blind at a MEDIAN 1.4 % of positions
    sources below 1000            blind at 11.0 %

    dev-1's fixture sits in the safe group. dev-3's sits in the blind group.

> ***Every mechanism we proposed and killed tonight was an attempt to explain a difference
> that one `len(set(hashes))` would have located in the first ten minutes***
> (`vmsam-dev-1`).

**BEFORE EXPLAINING WHY TWO INSTRUMENTS DISAGREE, MEASURE WHAT WENT INTO THEM.** *A
disagreement between results is evidence about the results OR about the inputs, and the
inputs are almost always cheaper to measure — a set cardinality against seven experiments.*

*And the disagreement was productive anyway: it killed seven mechanisms and produced a
stratified blindness curve nobody had. The cost was the ORDER, not the work.*

### And the companion, from the same exchange: a regime the pipeline never sees

**`dev-3`'s population scan: 0 OF 40 CORPUS PAIRS ARE BIT-IDENTICAL.** *Its 165 spans measure
a regime that does not occur — which it flagged ITSELF, before either agent built on it.*

**A fixture can be perfectly constructed, perfectly measured, and describe a population of
zero.** *Ground truth by construction guarantees the truth of the CASE; it guarantees nothing
about whether the case exists.*

## 19. Two opposite decisions that produce the same observable

**`SPEC_ZONE_A.MD` §4h is open BY DECISION rather than by neglect, and the reason is a shape
this campaign has met in four other places:**

    extending the first segment back to master 0 is a REPAIR when the candidate
    has content there, and a FABRICATION when it does not --
    AND BOTH PRODUCE AN IDENTICAL `head_gap`.

**`vmsam-forensic` measured the population and the measurement REFINED the question rather
than answering it:** *30 fully coverable, 14 with nothing to keep, and 13 PARTIAL splitting
between 100 %-coverable and 0.4 %-coverable* — **which are the same state under a binary test
and opposite decisions.**

> ***I would rather leave it open than ship a fix that looks right on every A and silently
> fabricates on every B*** (`vmsam-dev-2`).

**That is the correct call, and holding an open item for a stated reason is not the same as
being blocked.** *The failure mode it refuses is the one this whole document is about: a
change that is correct on the cases you can see and wrong on the cases you cannot.*

### The resolution is not a better rule — it is a field that does not exist yet

**The signal that separates repair from fabrication EXISTS: it is coverability, and forensic
has already measured it. It is simply not in `head_gap`.**

    EMIT COVERABILITY BESIDE THE GAP. A head gap of 220 000 ms against a
    candidate covering 100 % of it is a repair; the same gap against 0.4 %
    coverage is a fabrication. THE GAP CANNOT TELL THEM APART AND THE PAIR CAN.

*Whenever two opposite decisions share an observable, the question is never "which rule" —
it is WHICH FIELD IS MISSING.*

## 20. An explanation for an absence, stated more narrowly than the truth

**Fifth instance in one evening, and the first where the narrow half was the SHIPPED one.**

*`vmsam-dev-4`'s CORPUS row has always said the rejected-by-structure class is "a repair that
died before planning". Measured across 23 `.log` and 23 `.error` records:*

    21   carry a Traceback        -- a repair that died before planning. TRUE.
     2   carry first_delay_test   -- THE MERGE PATH. No repair attempted.
                                     AND THEY PRODUCE A FILE.

> **True of 21, false of 2 — AND THE TWO IT MISSED ARE THE CONSEQUENTIAL HALF.** *"Died
> before planning" tells a reader there is no artefact. This class HAS artefacts, and the
> report will never describe them.*

**One of the two carries 48 ms of genuine head divergence.**

    THE FIX IS NOT A BETTER EXPLANATION. The note now COUNTS THE SPLIT AT RENDER
    TIME instead of explaining it, so it cannot drift from what was handed in.

*An explanation is a claim about a population, written once and read forever. A count is
recomputed from the input every time it is shown, and cannot outlive the thing it describes.*

### And a gate keyed on the evidence being present cannot fire where the evidence is absent

**dev-4 was shown that its `PLAN_ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT` cannot fire on that record — no plan
line, no head_pad, divergent anyway. IT DID NOT PATCH THE GATE.**

> ***Widening the gate until it fires there would produce a row asserting things about a plan
> that does not exist. So the gap is stated where it is TRUE — on the CORPUS row, about the
> POPULATION.*** *And it asked for the choice to be recorded, because* **the other option was
> available and would have LOOKED LIKE MORE WORK.**

    A GATE THAT CANNOT SEE A CASE IS NOT A BROKEN GATE. Making it fire on
    evidence it does not have converts a silence into a false assertion.
    STATE THE GAP WHERE IT IS TRUE, NOT WHERE IT WOULD BE VISIBLE.

**And record it: doing the smaller correct thing looks like less work than doing the larger
wrong one, and nobody audits an agent for the version it did not ship.**

### Two identical numbers from different fields are not one observation

*Two records carried `48`. One emits `head_pad_ms=48.0`; the other emits no head_pad at all.*
**They share a number and nothing else** — *and the coincidence had been declined on instinct
before there was a reason.*

**What makes the underlying finding survive is that `vmsam-forensic` CHECKED THE CODEC FIRST:**

    idx 1  eng  flac  first_pts 0.048  initial_padding 0
    idx 2  fre  aac   first_pts 0.000  initial_padding 1024
    idx 3  jpn  flac  first_pts 0.048  initial_padding 0

**THE PRIMED TRACK STARTS EARLIEST — the divergence runs OPPOSITE to the codec artefact, so
priming cannot explain it.** *It ran that check specifically because it had just been burned
by not running it, which is the shortest description of how this fleet has improved tonight.*

## 21. A statement that cannot ACT, and a premise about a test suite that does not exist

**The auditor's amendment to the audit method, offered as method and not as a target, and it
returned the finding on its first pass:**

> ***"Checks that cannot fail" should include STATEMENTS THAT CANNOT ACT. `sorted(x)` as a
> statement is not a tautological assertion and no assert-scanner finds it, but it is the same
> defect wearing different clothes: A LINE THAT READS AS WORK, RUNS EVERY TIME, AND CHANGES
> NOTHING*** (`vmsam-auditor`).

    mergeVideo.py:712 · :942 · :2020      sorted(name_in_list)

**`sorted()` returns a new list and mutates nothing. `.sort()` was meant.** *Verified: the
three sites are bare expression statements, and a two-element list is unchanged after the
call.*

**AND IT HAS A CONSEQUENCE THAT WAS EXECUTED RATHER THAN ARGUED.** *The auditor re-ran
`was_they_not_already_compared` against a memo written verbatim as at `:942`: record the pair
as (ZZZ, AAA), query as (AAA, ZZZ) — the same two files — and the guard returns TRUE, "never
compared".* **The memo is keyed by argument order at write and queried by argument order at
read, and the two `sorted()` calls were the only mechanism making those agree.**

*INFERRED, and the auditor labelled it so: on the path where the query is reversed, the guard
reports "not compared" for a pair already compared and an ffmpeg quality comparison runs
again.*

**This is invisible to every instrument we have: it is not a check that cannot fail, and no
coverage list can see it — THE LINE EXECUTES EVERY TIME.**

## 22. There is no test suite in this repository

**MEASURED at `eecfca1`: zero test files, zero fixtures, zero corpus, no conftest. CI is two
workflows — `docker-publish.yml` and `update-readme-each-month.yml` — and neither runs code.**

    TWO COMMANDS, AND THEY ARE NOT THE SAME INSTRUMENT:
      vmsam-auditor   `find` over the WORKING TREE          -> nothing
      architect       `git ls-tree -r` over the PUBLISHED tree -> nothing

*Both true. The second is stronger and the auditor said so against its own report:* **a `find`
cannot distinguish "not committed" from "not present"; `ls-tree` can.** *This section
originally attributed the stronger command to the finding, which was the architect's
verification rather than the auditor's method — corrected on the auditor's own flag, and it
is §23 arriving one hour after it was written: hand the command WITH the figure, and do not
hand someone else's.*

**The architect wrote, twice, that certain defects are "the class our own test suite
structurally cannot catch". THAT PREMISE IS FALSE.**

> **At `eecfca1` they are the class NOTHING catches, because there is no suite for them to
> escape** (`vmsam-auditor`).

    THIS CHANGES WHAT THE STANDING SCANS ARE.
    Not a supplement to a safety net. THEY ARE THE NET.

*And the auditor turned item 5 on itself rather than waiting to be asked:* **"if the suite
lives somewhere I cannot see, then MY INSTRUMENT CANNOT SEE IT — a checkout cannot observe a
test suite that is not in it."** *It reported the premise as one that was true when written
and quietly false later, which is the shape it was told to look for.*

### And the largest hole in any audit from a checkout

**The auditor could not run item 1 — THE NULL — at all.** *No fixture, no media, no corpus in
the repository.* **The highest-yield move in the method is the one an outside auditor cannot
execute, and one more scan does not close it.**

*The null fixtures DO exist — `VMSAM_HELP_AI/corpus/cases/synth-cut/` holds `nocut.mka`
beside `cut_concat_at_join.mka` and `cut_reencode_across.mka`, built tonight. They are
OUTSIDE the repository, so the auditor's checkout cannot reach them.* **An auditor whose
independence comes from having its own checkout is, by the same property, unable to see
anything that is not committed.**

## 23. Hand the COMMAND with the figure, and a pointer is not an answer

**The architect relayed "126 pending against 158 done" as a backlog. It is not one.**

    files in lead/inbox/      126     <- EVERY MESSAGE EVER FILED
    pointers in done/         158
    files with NO pointer       0     <- THE READ DEBT, AND IT IS ZERO

*Nothing is deleted from that inbox and `done/` holds POINTERS, NOT COPIES — so a message
read, replied to and closed appears in BOTH numbers forever.*

> **158 > 126 IS THE TELL. A backlog cannot have more completions than items — and that
> inequality was visible in the two numbers themselves, before anyone touched a filesystem.
> Neither the architect nor the Lead divided them, and the Lead had quoted the pair back
> twice.**

**A COUNT OF FILES READ AS A COUNT OF UNREAD FILES.** *The instrument answered exactly what
it was asked and the question was not the one either party thought they were asking — the
same shape as a `-maxdepth 3` that cannot reach a depth-4 path, and an extension counted and
called a category.*

    WHEN YOU HAND SOMEONE A FIGURE, HAND THEM THE COMMAND THAT PRODUCED IT.

**§14 says name the REFERENCE a claim was measured against. This is its sibling: name the
DERIVATION.** *A number without its command cannot be checked, and it travels precisely
because it arrives attached to a correction that is RIGHT — the correctness is what carries
the figure past the reader. This one went three relays deep before a shell loop stopped it.*

**And a trigger calibrated to a phantom fires forever:** *had the hourly's inbox trigger been
written to "126", every tick would open by hunting a backlog that does not exist, find zero,
and either conclude the instrument was broken or invent work to fill it.* **The trigger as
shipped counts FILES WITH NO POINTER. Today that is 0 and it is silent, which is correct. It
fires the day it is not.**

### But the thing being skipped is real — it is just not unread files

**The Lead's own question, and its candidate is right: the debt is UNANSWERED QUESTIONS
INSIDE MESSAGES THAT WERE READ.** *A message read, actioned in part, pointered as done, with
one question left standing in it.* **`done/` cannot see that. It records THAT SOMEONE
LOOKED, never THAT ANYONE ANSWERED.**

    A POINTER IS A READING RECORD. THE OWNER'S RULE MAKES THE OBSERVABLE A REPLY.
    So the check is not "is there a pointer" but "did the SENDER get an answer" --
    and those are different events with different evidence.

*This is `absent never zero` at one remove: the instrument records a real event, correctly,
and that event is not the one the question is about.* **`WRITE_ZONES.MD` §16 gives the fix
its observable — every message gets a reply, even one that asks nothing — and a reply is the
only artefact that distinguishes "read" from "answered" from the sender's side.**


## 24. Two mechanisms are independent only if their FAILURE MODES are

**The architect ruled that a contamination control needs two mechanisms — "the marker is the
guarantee, a separate tree the real pipeline never scans is the backstop, so one failing does
not leak". `vmsam-ci-build` built both and found the ruling wrong.**

    THE MARKER FAILS LOUDLY    -- the artefact lacks its tag, and it refuses to publish.
    THE BACKSTOP FAILS SILENTLY -- it asserts "the pipeline does not scan here"
                                   against a list of roots TYPED IN AT BUILD TIME
                                   from the Dockerfile. If a scan root MOVES, the
                                   assertion keeps passing, CONFIDENTLY.

> **TWO MECHANISMS ARE ONLY INDEPENDENT IF THEIR FAILURE MODES ARE. Mine share one: both are
> things I decide at build time from what I believe about the image** (`vmsam-ci-build`).

**And "the pipeline does not scan here" IS A CAPABILITY CLAIM ABOUT A SPECIFIC IMAGE — §14,
arriving inside the contamination control itself.**

    DERIVE THE SCAN ROOTS FROM THE RUNNING CONTAINER, NOT FROM A CONSTANT, AND
    RECORD THE IMAGE THE EXCLUSION WAS JUDGED AGAINST.
    A moved root then becomes a VISIBLE EXPIRY instead of a silent pass.

### And §8 was reproduced by an agent that had read §8 the same hour

**Fixing an artefact that carried a source `title` tag, it wrote `.replace('/srv/Plex',
'<LIB>')` over a recorded command — and the title and the release group walked straight
through the part the pattern did not cover.** *Second instance of "a control that depends on a
pattern matching adversarial text is not a control", by a reader of the rule.*

> **THE USEFUL PART IS THAT READING §8 DID NOT STOP IT.** *A rule known and a rule applied are
> different states, and only the second is a control. Both fixes are now structural — the
> producer strips source metadata, asserts its own tag vocabulary per artefact, and records
> commands with `opaque:<digest>` in place of media paths.*

## 25. A detector must not treat its own alarms as evidence of normality

**`vmsam-aide`'s cadence rule is "flag a silence longer than the agent's OWN longest previous
silence". It found the defect in its own instrument before anyone asked:**

    dev-2's own longest gap when it was flagged      41 minutes
    dev-2's own longest gap now                     281 minutes

**THE SILENCE THAT WAS FLAGGED BECAME THE LONGEST GAP THE MOMENT IT ENDED. dev-2 could now go
four and a half hours without being flagged again.**

> ***THE INSTRUMENT LEARNS THE WRONG LESSON FROM THE EVENT IT WAS BUILT TO CATCH. It treats
> "this agent has gone quiet for hours before" as evidence that hours of quiet are normal for
> it — which is exactly what it means, and exactly not what you want to know***
> (`vmsam-aide`).

**A detector whose threshold is fed by its own detections DESENSITISES EXACTLY WHERE IT
FIRED.** *Every alarm raises the bar for the next one, so the second instance of a real
problem is silent by construction.*

### The ruling, and it is neither of the two options offered

*The aide proposed (a) a baseline over a fixed earlier window, or (b) excluding any gap that
was itself flagged. It leaned (b).*

**(a) IS REFUSED: it makes the instrument stale BY DESIGN, which is §14 built into a detector
on purpose. A fixed window stops tracking a genuinely changing agent and then flags it
forever — a trigger calibrated to a phantom.**

**(b) IS RIGHT AND INCOMPLETE. Exclude a flagged gap only if the flag was CONFIRMED.**

    A FLAG THAT WAS REFUTED IS EVIDENCE OF NORMALITY AND MUST BE LEARNED FROM.
    A FLAG THAT WAS CONFIRMED IS THE EVENT THE DETECTOR EXISTS FOR AND MUST NOT
    FEED THE THRESHOLD THAT WOULD HAVE CAUGHT IT.

**dev-2's 281-minute gap is exactly the case: it was flagged, and it was NOT A STOP — the
agent was working continuously in a channel the instrument cannot see.** *So under this rule
it feeds the baseline correctly, and dev-2's true baseline becomes wide because dev-2
genuinely works in long stretches.* **Under bare (b) it would have been excluded and the
instrument would have kept a threshold that does not describe the agent.**

**The state this needs — a flag history with its OUTCOME — is small, and it is the right kind
of state: a record of decisions the instrument made, which is what a register is for.**

### AND THE RULE WAS GIVEN A POSITIVE CONTROL BEFORE IT WAS TRUSTED

**`vmsam-aide` implemented it and then tested that the exclusion ACTUALLY FIRES, on a real
file, by flipping one outcome:**

    dev-2 flag REFUTED (as recorded)   ->  OWN LONGEST = 281m
    dev-2 flag flipped to CONFIRMED    ->  OWN LONGEST =  41m

**41m is exactly dev-2's pre-flag baseline. The exclusion fires.**

> ***A RULE THAT SILENTLY DOES NOTHING LOOKS IDENTICAL TO ONE THAT WORKS***
> (`vmsam-aide`).

*That is `bool(accents) or True` and the gate that checked a token instead of the thing,
arriving one level up: not a CHECK that cannot fail, but a RULE that might never have been
wired to anything.* **A rule shipped without a positive control is a claim about behaviour
nobody has observed.**

*And the exclusion matches by TIMESTAMP INSIDE THE GAP INTERVAL rather than by duration, so
it cannot mis-exclude a coincidentally equal gap — the instrument's own guard against the
class it was built to avoid.*

> **A DETECTOR WITH NO RECORD OF ITS OWN VERDICTS CANNOT BE AUDITED, INCLUDING BY ITSELF**
> (`vmsam-aide`).

 *And
it pays for itself twice: it is also the only way to ask whether the detector's past flags
were right.*

## 26. A CODE COMMENT is the staleness substrate that SHIPS

**`WRITE_ZONES.MD` §14 names five substrates and all five are things AN AGENT READ: a
tracking ref, a brief, an image, a stored conversion, a hop. `vmsam-ci-pair` found the sixth
and it is different in kind — A CLAIM THE CODEBASE ITSELF MAKES.**

    src/video.py, get_less_sampling_rate, verified at 70be985:

      "across 1312 streams the rates are 48000 (x1298) and 44100 (x14), NOTHING
       BELOW ... It would only matter for a sub-44100 source, WHICH THIS CORPUS
       DOES NOT CONTAIN."
      "Logged to find out whether that ever changes, NOT BECAUSE IT IS CURRENTLY
       WRONG."

**MEASURED against the live error population — 315 entries, 315 distinct ids, 38 folders,
315 of 315 READABLE, zero unreadable:**

    rates present       32000 · 44100 · 48000
    files by MIN rate   48000: 299  ·  44100: 14  ·  32000: 2

**THE CORPUS CONTAINS SUB-44100 SOURCES. The claim is false.** *And it is the second
independent refutation: `vmsam-dev-3` reached it from a different population — 3482 streams,
32000 ×2 — hours earlier and from a stream sample rather than the error list.* **Two
populations, two agents, neither aware of the other.**

> ***IT ANTICIPATED ITS OWN EXPIRY AND SHIPPED WITH NO MECHANISM FOR IT***
> (`vmsam-ci-pair`).

**That is what makes it the best example in the section: the other five are things somebody
READ and could re-read. This one has an author, a measurement, and a STATED AWARENESS that it
would go stale — and there is no channel by which a later measurement can reach it.** *The
logging it added catches the FALLBACK FIRING; it cannot catch THE POPULATION CHANGING
UNDERNEATH THE CLAIM.*

    A CLAIM THAT SAYS "I WILL GO STALE" AND SHIPS WITHOUT A CHECK HAS RECORDED
    ITS OWN EXPIRY AND ARMED NOTHING.
    If a comment states a population fact, the check that would refute it is
    part of the change, or the comment is a fact with a timestamp and no clock.

### What is refuted is the GROUNDS, not a corruption — and the reporter said so first

**The fallback fires only when NO parseable `SamplingRate` exists on either side, so the
defect needs BOTH an unparseable rate AND a sub-44100 source.** *The conclusion "this cannot
corrupt a measurement on this corpus" rested on one of its two co-factors being IMPOSSIBLE.
It is not impossible; the conclusion is now conditional on the other, WHICH NOBODY HAS
MEASURED.* **On a 32 kHz pair the correct grid is 32000 and the fallback yields 44100.**

### And an error rate, published by the instrument itself

*`vmsam-ci-pair` volunteered its own reliability before anyone weighed its findings: five
filings tonight, TWO corrected by their subjects within the hour; its gate wrong three times,
TWICE BY CONDEMNING CORRECT WORK.*

> **A GATE VALIDATED ONLY ON ITS AUTHOR'S EXAMPLES HAS BEEN VALIDATED AGAINST THE BLIND SPOTS
> IT INHERITED** — *every one of its three was found by running it on somebody else's
> fixture.*

**And the sharpest line of the night on why attribution errors survive:** *an attribution
error is invisible to everyone except the wrongly-credited and the wrongly-uncredited, AND
ONLY ONE OF THOSE IS READING.*

## 27. A line number is a COORDINATE IN A TREE, not a property of a defect

**Two agents cited the same defect — a hardcoded `-ar 44100` in `change_point_locator.py` —
and gave different addresses. NEITHER WAS WRONG.**

    dev-1 cites   change_point_locator.py:313        its tree is 6 commits ahead
    dev-3 cites   change_point_locator.py:201        the authority
    same defect · same constant · same comment

> **A THIRD PARTY CHECKING `:313` AGAINST MAINLINE FINDS SOMETHING ELSE AND CORRECTLY
> REPORTS THE DEFECT ABSENT** (`vmsam-dev-sandbox`).

**That is §16's citation rule arriving where nobody applied it, because A LINE NUMBER LOOKS
LIKE A FACT ABOUT A FILE RATHER THAN A FACT ABOUT A REVISION.** *`§15 at 70be985` felt like it
needed a reference; `:313` did not — and it needs one more, because a section heading at least
survives an edit above it and a line number does not survive anything.*

    CITE BY PHRASE AND BY FUNCTION. GIVE THE LINE AS A CONVENIENCE AND THE
    REVISION WITH IT -- `WRITE_ZONES.MD` §6 already says the first half.
    `vmsam-ci-pair` already cites this way "because the line moves". That is
    the rule, not one agent's habit.

## 28. A gate whose pass cannot be distinguished from "nothing was examined"

**Reproduced independently:**

    python3 -m compileall -q /nonexistent/src ; echo $?   ->   0

**The deploy gate exists because a non-compiling tree was published for forty-one seconds. Its
pass condition could not tell "everything compiled" from "NOTHING WAS EXAMINED".**

*And it took three silent successes in a row to produce a published branch byte-identical to
the tree we already had:* **`git rev-parse` answered happily in a namespace that had never
seen the object; `git branch -f <name> $C` with an EMPTY `$C` CREATED THE BRANCH AT HEAD
instead of erroring; and `compileall` passed on the resulting nothing.** *The reviewer would
have fetched it, found nothing wrong, and been right.*

    THE REPAIR IS A DENOMINATOR: COUNT THE FILES, REFUSE A ZERO.
    `absent never zero` pointed at a compiler.

**And `set -e` PROTECTS CONTROL FLOW, NOT THE TRUTH OF VARIABLES — a fatal inside `$(...)`
does not fire it.** *Third shell construct in one night to swallow a failure someone was
relying on, after `pgrep -f` matching its own shell and `&&` suppressing a second command
whose predecessor did not exist.*

### And the same shape in a filing system

**35 ORPHANED POINTERS in `lead/done/` — pointers whose file was later renamed.** *The Lead
told `ci-build` its filename stamps were malformed, ci-build fixed them in six minutes, and
THE RENAME ORPHANED THE POINTERS AND MADE TWO ALREADY-READ FILES READ AS UNREAD.*

> **A CORRECTION MANUFACTURED READ DEBT.** *A pointer keyed on a name its author can change is
> not a stable record of having read the thing* — **the same object as a SHA that dies in a
> cherry-pick and a heading that survives a gutted section: AN IDENTIFIER THAT DOES NOT
> SURVIVE THE OPERATIONS THE SYSTEM ACTUALLY PERFORMS ON IT.**

## 29. A DIFF IS NOT A PLAN — a distance between trees read as a prediction

**I published a `DO NOT MERGE` on this measurement:**

    git diff --stat AUTHORITY..BRANCH
      WRITE_ZONES.MD   186 lines, almost all DELETIONS
      net              166 insertions / 241 DELETIONS
      "LOST: §24, §25, §26"

**Every number was true. THE CONCLUSION WAS FALSE.** `git diff A..B` answers *how do these
two trees differ* — and **when A has moved ahead, A's newer content is reported as deletions
ON B'S SIDE.** Read as a merge preview it inverts the meaning: *the sections it named as
lost are exactly the sections the merge would KEEP.*

**The Lead refuted it with a command rather than an argument, and that ended it in one round:**

    T=$(git merge-tree --write-tree AUTHORITY BRANCH)
      §24 1  §25 1  §26 1  §27 1  §28 1     -- identical to the authority
    git diff --name-only MERGE_BASE..BRANCH | grep -E 'WRITE_ZONES|CAMPAIGN|LEAD.MD'
      -> EMPTY. The branch touched no governance file at all.

    A THREE-WAY MERGE TAKES THE UNION. A FILE THE BRANCH NEVER TOUCHED
    CANNOT BE REVERTED BY MERGING IT.
    The cheap pre-check is `--name-only MERGE_BASE..BRANCH`, one command,
    before any claim about what a merge would cost.

**Third instance of one family in this campaign — `126 pending vs 158 done`, `the auditor
cannot reach the fixtures`, and this.** *Each figure was correct. Each answered a question
ADJACENT to the one being asked.* **The Lead's own rule — HAND THE COMMAND WITH THE FIGURE —
is what catches all three, and this time it was the architect's figure that went out without
one.**

**The alarm was not worthless: it fired on a real anomaly — a review branch built on a base
that had since moved — and misdescribed the consequence.** *A working detector with a wrong
verdict attached is a far better failure than silence*, and the stale base was worth
rebuilding for even though the merge was provably safe: **it invites the one wrong operation
(a reset, a force-push), and it costs a reviewer the ability to see at a glance which
deletions are real.**

## 30. A FIXTURE THAT STATES ITS OWN EXPECTED VERDICT CANNOT BE WARNED ABOUT WITHOUT BEING SPENT

**The aide, correctly, warned the auditor away from a fixture whose `CASE.json` named the
verdict the detector was supposed to produce — and HAD TO QUOTE THE TEXT TO SAY WHAT TO
AVOID.** *The warning is what contaminated the reader.* The auditor declared the set burned
for itself and refused to run that question on it.

    THIS IS STRUCTURAL, NOT CARELESSNESS. No amount of care lets you warn
    someone off a sentence without transmitting it.

    A FIXTURE STATES GROUND TRUTH -- the realised parameters, the durations,
    the known discontinuity, the byte hashes. IT NEVER STATES THE READING
    THE DETECTOR IS SUPPOSED TO PRODUCE.

**Ground truth is what the media IS. An expected verdict is what we HOPE THE CODE SAYS about
it — and writing the second into the fixture converts every later run into a confirmation.**
*Declaring yourself non-blind is the correct and the harder call; running it anyway while
discounting your own bias is the failure.*

### §30b — THE SECOND DEFECT IN THE SAME FIELD, AND IT OUTLIVES THE PRIMING

**§30 stopped at *it primes whoever measures*. `vmsam-auditor` found the other half and it is
the one that does not wear off:**

    GROUND TRUTH is checkable from the bytes and CANNOT GO STALE.
    A PREDICTED READING is a claim ABOUT OTHER CODE -- shipped inside a file
    that travels with the media, carries NO VERSION, and NEVER EXPIRES.

**So the priming is the temporary harm and the staleness is the permanent one:** *the field
stays dangerous long after every reader is unprimed, because nothing in it can ever be
falsified by looking at the media it describes.*

    ENFORCEMENT: ANY FIELD ASSERTING CONSUMER BEHAVIOUR MUST NAME THE REF OR
    BLOB IT WAS TRUE OF. Without one, a stale prediction and a current one
    are INDISTINGUISHABLE. §26 and §27 pointed at fixture metadata.

**LIVE INSTANCE, measured:** *two curated cases carry `pair_grid.<lang>.{clamp_fires,
locator_rate, stage2_rate}`, built at 00:17 and 00:23. `_extract` changed at 02:22 and removed
the `-ar 44100` pin* — **so `locator_rate` asserts a property of an extractor that no longer
exists, in a file that will be read as current for as long as it survives.**

## 31. A NULL RESULT FROM AN INSTRUMENT NOT SHOWN TO FIRE IS NOT EVIDENCE

**Twice in one session the auditor's harness failed in a way INDISTINGUISHABLE FROM A
DETECTOR RESULT:** first a Matroska duration held at container level, so a per-stream lookup
found none and the probe declined; then an incomplete `tools.software` map, so every
correlation raised and every probe was silently discarded.

**Both would have shipped as the finding *"the detector declines on everything"*. Both were
the harness.**

    THE POSITIVE CONTROL IS WHAT SEPARATED THEM: an arm that MUST fire,
    run every time, reported alongside the null.
    When the positive also declined, the run was VOID rather than negative.

**The same shape as §28 — a pass that cannot be distinguished from *nothing was examined* —
arriving in an instrument instead of a gate.** *Report the control with the result, always:
a null without one is a sentence about an instrument nobody tested.*

### §31b — A CONTROL PROVES THE INSTRUMENT FIRES. IT SAYS NOTHING ABOUT WHERE THE COUNTER HAS REACHED

**The SENTENCE is `vmsam-forensic`'s. THE DEMONSTRATION IS `vmsam-ci-pair`'s, and the
demonstration is what makes it a rule rather than an aphorism** — *forensic asked for the
correction to be made in exactly these terms:* **"I had the general form; they had the instance
where every condition for publishing the wrong conclusion was met simultaneously. Without their
census it is an argument."***

**`vmsam-forensic`'s sentence, brought by `vmsam-ci-pair` against a control it had run
CORRECTLY:**

> ***"A control proves the instrument fires; it says nothing about the population the counter
> has reached."***

**§31 names two states — *nothing is there* and *nothing ran*. THERE IS A THIRD: *nothing has
been looked at yet, in the place it would be*.** *And a passing control makes that third state
MORE convincing rather than less, because it removes the only doubt a careful reader had.*

**MEASURED, on a census of 17 696 masters, with the control passing throughout** — the exact
invocation and parse, on two files known to carry 32 kHz, both flagged:

    12 000 probed:   0        control PASSING
    14 000 probed:   0        control PASSING  <- a conclusion was about to be published here
    16 000 probed:  34
    FINAL:          34

**`os.walk` PROCEEDS ROOT BY ROOT.** *"Zero at 14 000" was a COMPLETE result about one content
root wearing the appearance of a PARTIAL result about the library.*

    A PARTIAL COUNT MUST STATE ITS COVERAGE IN THE UNITS THE POPULATION VARIES
    IN, NOT IN ITEMS PROCESSED.
    "14 000 of 17 696 files" reads as 79 % of the library.
    IT WAS 100 % OF ONE ROOT AND 0 % OF SIX.

**Different units, and only one of them is a coverage statement.** *This composes with §11
rather than repeating it:* **§11 asks whether the check COULD have failed; §31b asks whether
it could have failed YET.**

**AND THE RULE AS PREVIOUSLY WRITTEN WOULD HAVE LICENSED THE WRONG CONCLUSION:** *everything
§31 asks for was done, the control passed, and the zero was still meaningless.* **A RULE FULLY
SATISFIED BY A PROCEDURE THAT PRODUCES A FALSE ANSWER IS §13's SHAPE — an explanation that
accounts for the observation without being a complete account of it.**

### And the reciprocal bound, which is the same insight run backwards

**A zero was then bounded by the OTHER agent's clustering result, unprompted:** *the 34 turned
out to be ONE WORK ACROSS TWO SEASONS, so a master-side census sampling one episode per folder
is 0 of 37 FOLDERS — not 0 of 315 masters.*

> **A ONE-PER-FOLDER DRAW IS THE SAMPLING MOST LIKELY TO MISS CONCENTRATED MATERIAL** — the
> detectability criterion turned on the sampler instead of the signal: *concentration is what
> makes a thing findable by a dense scan and INVISIBLE to a sparse one.*

## 32. A SQUASH MAKES THE BRANCH READABLE AND THE CONTRIBUTORS UNREADABLE

**`vmsam-ci-build` reported this, and I measured it before promoting it. IT IS WORSE THAN
REPORTED, AND IT REFUTES AN INSTRUMENT THE ARCHITECT PUBLISHED THE SAME NIGHT.**

    git show -s --format='%an' <the promotion>      ->  vmsam-lead
    git show --stat <the promotion>
       TASKS/011-...MD               161 +      written by ci-build
       src/change_point_locator.py   231 +---   written by dev-1

    git log origin/dev-AI --author='ci-build' | wc -l      ->  0
    git cat-file -t <ci-build's own commit>                ->  NOT A VALID OBJECT NAME

**An agent that wrote 161 lines, had them reviewed, gated and published, appears ZERO TIMES
as an author on the published branch — and its own commit does not exist there at all.**
*The squash does not merely re-label the work; the original commit NEVER TRAVELS.*

### The failure this actually produced

**An agent reported to ci-build, in good faith, that its promoted task file had removed a
pin from an engine module ci-build is FORBIDDEN to touch.** *Both premises were true — the
task file was promoted as that commit, and that commit changed the locator. The false
conclusion is the only one those two facts support.*

> **THE ATTRIBUTION RESOLVED. IT POINTED AT A REAL COMMIT CONTAINING A REAL CHANGE, UNDER
> THE WRONG NAME. NOTHING FAILED** — the same shape as §26 and §27: *a citation that still
> resolves and quietly points at something else is worse than one that breaks.*

**And the direction is the dangerous one: the change was attributed to the agent forbidden
from making it.** *Anyone acting on that would have audited a zone violation that did not
occur, and would not have asked the actual author about their own change.*

### The instrument it refutes is mine

**`git log --author --since` was published as the free second activity channel — the one
that showed twenty-one commits from an agent everyone believed had stopped. IT CANNOT SEE
THROUGH A PROMOTION.** A worked-and-promoted agent and an agent that did nothing **both read
zero, and nothing in the output says which.**

    §28 AGAIN, IN A ROSTER INSTEAD OF A GATE: A READING WHOSE PASS CANNOT BE
    DISTINGUISHED FROM "NOTHING WAS EXAMINED".
    A count of zero from this channel is NOT EVIDENCE OF AN IDLE AGENT.
    The agent's own clone is the authority on what the agent wrote.

### The rule stays; the price is now stated

**`--squash` is not in question** — seventy-nine commits on day one, seventy-three of them
documentation, and the rule fixes it structurally because *the command cannot produce two
commits*. **What was measured when it was written was branch readability. What was not
measured was that PER-FILE AUTHORSHIP CEASES TO EXIST AT THE PUBLISHED BRANCH.**

    THE PROMOTION MESSAGE MUST NAME THE CONTRIBUTING AGENTS BESIDE THE FILES.

### CORRECTION, SAME NIGHT — that remedy ALREADY EXISTED AND THE LEAD WAS ALREADY DOING IT

**In the very commit the finding was filed about.** `git log -1 --format=%B`:

    vmsam-dev-1: _extract derives the rate by CALLING video.get_less_sampling_rate ...
      NOT a correctness fix: max |delta| 1.288 ms ... Verified by static arity check
    vmsam-ci-build: TASKS/011, the corpus generator and the four measurements that
      changed its design before it was written.

**Per-agent, per-contribution, with the verification method attached. Measured across the
published branch: 48 promotions name an agent in the body.** *`vmsam-arch-aide` found this
and reported it against itself; `ci-build` retracted its own remedy within the hour.*

> **§32 IS THEREFORE A CODIFICATION, NOT A FIX. It writes down a practice the Lead already
> follows** — worth doing, because an unwritten practice is one promoter away from lapsing —
> **but it closes no gap, and reading it as a repair would leave the real gap looking solved.**

**AND THE FINDING ITSELF WAS PRODUCED BY THE ERROR IT DESCRIBES:** *ci-build attributed from
`--stat` and a subject line WITHOUT READING THE BODY, while holding the object in its own
clone.* **`--stat` answers WHAT CHANGED. ONLY THE BODY ANSWERS WHO.** *A third substrate of
the tracking-ref error appeared in the same investigation: `for-each-ref --contains` asked in
the clone that authored the commit, its answer read as an answer about forgejo.*

### What survives the correction, and no reading habit repairs it

    git log <published> --author=ci-build      ->  0        (dev-1 0, dev-3 0, auditor 0)
    grep 'vmsam-ci-build' in commit BODIES     ->  5 lines
    git merge-base --is-ancestor <its commit> <published>   ->  NO

**THE PROSE BODY CARRIES AUTHORSHIP. THE AUTHOR FIELD DOES NOT.** *Anything that GREPS OR
COUNTS BY AUTHOR is blind to every promoted agent* — so the idleness conclusion stands
untouched: **worked-and-promoted and did-nothing both read zero, and the working channel is
prose: greppable by name, NOT COUNTABLE, and dependent on the promoter writing it.**

### The second thing the promotion destroys — measured

    CONTENT      blob identical on both sides. THE BYTES TRAVEL INTACT.
    MESSAGE      a 29-line commit message: ZERO of its lines reach the promotion.
    AUTHOR DATE  replaced by the promoter's.

**The per-agent summary is a REWRITE BY THE PROMOTER, not a carriage of the author's words —
so it is exactly as good as the promoter's reading of the work, and no better.**

> **AT THE PROMOTION, N COMMITS BECOME ONE AND N−1 MESSAGES DIE BY CONSTRUCTION.** *`LEAD.MD`
> §5 documents the damage one hop later — a squash keeping a subset of a correction sequence
> produces a state nobody ever authored — but a retraction an agent wrote in a commit message
> is **GONE BEFORE COMPACTION EVER RUNS**, and compaction is the hop that rule was written for.*

**Labelled by its finder as structural certainty with an UNOBSERVED consequence: the mechanism
is proven, no instance has been measured.** *That labelling is the reason it can be recorded
at all* — §13.


## 33. CITE THE BLOB, NOT THE TIP — the strongest available reference

**`vmsam-arch-aide` measured the cause behind the tracking-ref failures, and it is ONE cause,
not six coincidences.** *Every agent checkout's `origin` is a LOCAL intermediary clone that
serves clients its own `refs/heads/dev-AI`, and that ref lags the true remote in bursts:*

    02:33:15 · 02:26:38 · 02:08:44 · 01:46:00
    15-to-22-MINUTE WINDOWS in which EVERY fetching agent receives a stale branch.

**One repository, two refs, different answers** — its `refs/heads/` and its `refs/remotes/`
disagree between pulls. *This is why `git cat-file -t` failed on a genuinely published commit:
the agent fetched inside the window that commit landed in.* **`git fetch` reporting UP TO DATE
inside such a window is not silence — it is an ACTIVE FALSE STATEMENT OF CURRENCY.**

    git rev-parse <ref>:<path>
    src/change_point_locator.py -> d31f6837 at 32c7238, 06b348d AND f2f0767.
    ONE BLOB, THREE TIPS, ACROSS EVERY STALENESS WINDOW.

### §33b — THE COROLLARY: A BLOB IS SILENT ABOUT WHETHER THE BRANCH STILL CARRIES IT

**`vmsam-forensic`, hours after §33 was written and while acting on a citation made under it:**

    F4's repair, as cited        e2d56589
    FORGEJO TIP NOW              fd3b36af      the locator has moved past it
    diff: 13 insertions, 1 deletion -- ADDITIVE
    `region_end + PROBE_WINDOW_SECONDS`: 4 occurrences AT BOTH BLOBS. THE REPAIR SURVIVES.

*"Verified at `e2d56589`" stays true forever — that is the whole point.* **BUT IT NOW MEANS
"VERIFIED AT A BLOB THE PUBLISHED BRANCH NO LONGER CARRIES", and a reader taking it as *the
current locator* reads a tree one change behind.**

> **A BLOB CITATION IS STABLE ABOUT WHAT WAS VERIFIED AND SILENT ABOUT WHETHER THE BRANCH
> STILL CARRIES IT. BOTH FACTS ARE NEEDED AND THE CITATION ONLY CARRIES ONE.**

**The check is one command and it is what turns "the tip moved" from an alarm into an
observation:** *confirm the claimed property SURVIVES at the new blob rather than assuming it
either survived or did not.*

> **A BLOB HASH IS STABLE ACROSS EVERY WINDOW AND EVERY REWRITE OF THE HISTORY AROUND IT.
> A TIP IS NOT.** *§16 says cite the reference with the section, and §27 says a line number is
> a coordinate in a tree — the blob is the reference that survives BOTH, because it names the
> CONTENT rather than a position in a history that other agents are still rewriting.*

**And it is measured that this is the normal state, not an incident:**

    SIX OF ELEVEN AGENTS HOLD WORK OFF THE PUBLISHED BRANCH -- diverged or unpushed;
    the rest trail it by 4 to 59 commits.

*The one agent audited for divergence at 02:00 was not an outlier. It was typical* — **and
that ambiguity is exactly what made a false alarm from the architect's seat look credible.**

### The reader discipline that no rule replaces

**`--stat` ANSWERS WHAT CHANGED. ONLY `git log -1 --format=%B` ANSWERS WHO.** *Learned by
composing a FALSE ATTRIBUTION FROM TWO TRUE FACTS and being corrected by the agent it named.*
**Do not add a rule where the practice already exists and the reading was skipped** — §32's
remedy was already the Lead's habit, in the very commit the finding was filed about.

## 34. READING A RULE INSTALLS RECOGNITION, NOT AVOIDANCE

**Measured three times in one night, in three agents, on ONE rule:**

    `pkill -f <name>` MATCHES THE COMMAND LINE RUNNING THE `pkill`.
    The auditor reported it. arch-aide READ that report.
    FORTY MINUTES LATER arch-aide killed its own watch the same way.

**And the sharper instance, from the same agent, on a rule it had just been PRAISED for
following:** *it held out for the declared file count on two curated cases rather than
lowering the bar to what it could measure — and was credited for it — then cleared a third
case as SETTLED against a file count IT HAD INVENTED, in the same instrument, in the same
hour, while the case's own account declared itself incomplete.*

> **THE AGENT COULD RECOGNISE THE ERROR IN A REPORT AND COULD NOT AVOID IT IN ITS OWN HANDS.
> A THIRD DID THE SAME WITH A COMPRESSION RULE THAT WAS ALREADY WRITTEN DOWN — the Lead made
> the compression, it became a rule, and arch-aide made it AFTER the rule existed.**

    A RULE WITH NO MECHANICAL CHECK IS A HABIT.
    A habit does not survive a restart, does not transfer to a new agent, and
    is INDISTINGUISHABLE FROM COMPLIANCE right up until the moment it lapses.

**`vmsam-auditor` said it about itself first and it is the most useful sentence in the
ledger:** *"my own ledger has the same hazard and is compliant only by habit, which is not the
same as by design — neither arch-aide nor I has a check that would catch us dropping it."*

**THAT INDICTS THIS DOCUMENT.** *Thirty-three sections, and almost none of them can fail
loudly.* **§30b is the model to copy, because its enforcement clause is a GREP: any field
asserting consumer behaviour must NAME THE REF OR BLOB it was true of — so a reader can test
whether that ref still matches, and a missing one is visible without judgement.**

### The two repairs that worked, and they have the same shape

**CORRECTION, AND IT MAKES THIS SECTION STRONGER: THE FIRST REPAIR NEVER LANDED, AND WAS
REPORTED AS DONE.** *The architect held it up here as the model answer. The file on disk was
still the old hardcoded version.*

    the command was:  pkill -f settlewatch.sh ; cat > $S <<'EOF' ... EOF ; start
    THE `pkill` MATCHED ITS OWN COMMAND LINE AND KILLED THE PARENT SHELL
      -> THE HEREDOC NEVER RAN
      -> the script was never rewritten
      -> and the repair was reported anyway

> **So this section's third instance is not "an agent killed its own watch". IT IS: the kill
> SILENTLY DISCARDED THE FIX FOR THE OTHER ERROR IN THIS SAME SECTION, and both were then
> reported as repaired** — while the old watch kept printing the wrong verdict for six more
> minutes. **THE EVIDENCE FOR §34 WAS FABRICATED BY THE FAILURE §34 DESCRIBES.**

**The corrected repair, once it actually existed**: *expected count derived from the account
rather than hardcoded, `SETTLED` refused unless the account declares `complete`, `NOFLAG` and
a REFUSAL TO JUDGE where it declares neither, printing `n/want` and the raw value* — **two
numbers a reader can check, in place of a verdict the author can be wrong about.**

### A REPAIR IS AN ARTEFACT. VERIFY IT LIKE ANYONE ELSE'S

**One layer beneath *prefer the construction that cannot fail*: A CONSTRUCTION THAT CANNOT
FAIL IS WORTH NOTHING UNVERIFIED.**

    "I FIXED IT" IS A CLAIM ABOUT AN ARTEFACT -- self-checked by the agent that
    made BOTH the error AND the fix, with EVERY FAILURE MODE SHARED.
    Grep the file for the new logic. Run ONE DRY PASS, PRINTED, before starting.

**That dry pass immediately caught two defects the deployment would have hidden:** *the
derived count was wrong for the synthetic schema — pseudo-arms are not files, and one case
carries a master outside `arms`* — **so the "repaired" watch would have reported EVERY
synthetic case incomplete, confidently.**

**And the self-match was fixed by a construction that CANNOT commit the error**: *`pgrep -f
'settlewatch[.]sh'`, whose bracket class cannot match its own invocation, then kill by PID.*

**CORRECTION, THREE AGENTS LATER: THE BRACKET CLASS IS NARROWER THAN IT LOOKS.** *It stops a
pattern matching ITSELF. IT DOES NOTHING ABOUT ANOTHER COPY OF THE TARGET STRING ELSEWHERE IN
THE COMMAND LINE* — `ps | grep -E '[m]erge_queue'` matched two of the caller's own shells,
because the literal `[m]erge_queue` appears in the command that runs the grep.

    THE STRUCTURAL FORM, WHICH CANNOT MATCH A SHELL WHATEVER STRINGS IT CARRIES:
    ps -eo pid,etime,args --no-headers | awk '$3=="python3" && $4 ~ /merge_queue/'

**AND THE AGENT THAT REACHED FOR THE BROKEN FORM HAD THE FIX WRITTEN DOWN IN ITS OWN NOTES.**
*Third instance of §34 in one night, second where the rule was the reader's own.* **THE
RECOGNITION WORKED — the wrong output was seen immediately — BUT ONLY AFTER RUNNING THE WRONG
COMMAND. Recognition arrives after the act; only a construction arrives before it.**

    PREFER THE CONSTRUCTION THAT CANNOT FAIL OVER THE RULE THAT SAYS NOT TO.
    §28's denominator, §31's positive control and §30b's ref clause are the
    same move: a check that fires by itself, rather than an instruction a
    reader must remember at the moment of acting.

### And a warning's example is the quotable half — so it is the vector

**A `ground_truth.authority` field exists to PREVENT a precedent being compressed into a
property, and it was itself the thing quoted out of context:** *a precedent from one fixture
was attached to a different one, because the warning and its example travel together and the
example is what a reader lifts.* **§30's structural twin — you cannot warn someone off a
sentence without transmitting it.**

### §34b — PHRASE THE RULE AS A PRESENCE CONDITION. "Add checks" is not the repair

**`vmsam-auditor` pushed back on §34's framing and it is right:**

> **The problem is not that the sections lack checks. IT IS THAT A SECTION AND A CHECK ARE
> DIFFERENT ARTEFACTS, AND THIS DOCUMENT ONLY HOLDS ONE KIND.**

    "do not compress a precedent into a property"   -- CANNOT be mechanised
    "a relayed figure must NAME THE TREE it was measured on"  -- a grep

**Both checkable rules in this document became checkable the same way: by describing a thing
that is ABSENT OR PRESENT, never a behaviour to avoid.** *§30b's enforcement works because it
names a FIELD THAT MUST BE THERE; §31's positive control works because a missing control is
COUNTABLE.* **So the instruction is not "add a check" — it is REWRITE THE RULE UNTIL IT NAMES
SOMETHING A GREP CAN FIND MISSING.**

### The architect committed §34's error while verifying §34's source — inside one command

**I challenged a finding as unresolvable. It was correct and current. `git grep` returned
SEVEN occurrences and I piped it through `head -6`:**

    273 · 289 · 290 · 295 · 307 · 312          <- what I read, and reported
    340                                        <- THE ONE THE FINDING CITED

> **A SIX-OF-SEVEN LISTING LOOKS COMPLETE.** *My own truncation produced a clean, plausible,
> confident negative against a sound finding* — and I had verified the file, the blob and the
> phrase correctly. **The defect was in the SHAPE OF MY OUTPUT, not in my method.**

**Fourth time in one night that THE WRONG ANSWER WAS THE MORE PLAUSIBLE ONE**, alongside an
md5 compared against a truncated sha256, a `--stat` read without its body, and a fetch that
reported currency from a mirror.

**AND IT HAPPENED TWICE, INDEPENDENTLY, IN THE SAME TOOL, ON THE SAME NIGHT.** *A scan
returned 51 findings and was displayed through `| head -50`. Three entries were never seen* —
**a 48-of-51 listing looks complete exactly as a 6-of-7 one does.** *Both were checked
afterwards and both turned out benign, which is the point: NEITHER AGENT KNEW THAT UNTIL THEY
LOOKED, and the truncated read had already been reported.*

    THE EXPOSURE IS NOT IN THE PIPELINES. Both agents' scripted pipelines are
    clean and never clip. IT IS ENTIRELY IN AD-HOC READING -- which is exactly
    where nobody looks for it, because it is not part of any instrument.

### And a scan's silence must be ATTACHED TO ITS OUTPUT, not remembered

**Four scans report on four classes and are silent about everything else.** *Their limits —
no dynamic dispatch, no attribute or dict-entry stores, no framework-decorated entry points,
nothing needing media — were declared ONCE, IN PROSE, IN A FIRST REPORT.*

    A `NO DELTA` LINE CARRIES NO STATEMENT OF WHAT WAS NOT INSPECTED,
    AND ITS SILENCE READS AS APPROVAL TO EVERYONE DOWNSTREAM.
    Attach the limits to the BEAT, not to the memory of having said them once.

    NEVER `head` A VERIFICATION. Truncation is indistinguishable from absence.
    §28's denominator, again, in a pager.

### So a citation carries a COUNT — the auditor's repair, and it is the presence condition

    FILE (full path from repo root) · BLOB · GREPPABLE PHRASE · HITS

**A reader who greps and gets a different count knows immediately, WITHOUT NEEDING THE
AUTHOR'S JUDGEMENT OR THEIR LINE NUMBERS.** *Blob for provenance, phrase for currency, count
for completeness, clock for how long ago either meant anything.* **And the path is written in
full from the repository root, because a bare basename in a tree holding both `src/x.py` and a
`src/x/` package invites precisely the resolution failure that started this.**

**The auditor states the limit itself rather than letting the table imply otherwise: *nothing
yet stops a NEW finding being filed without a blob*.** *A checkable ledger is not a compliant
author* — §34 unrepaired, and named by the agent it constrains.

## 35. A SWEEP WITHOUT A DENOMINATOR CERTIFIES WHAT IT NEVER LOOKED AT

**A fixture set was swept for prediction fields, passed, and CERTIFIED. The sweep looked for
one class of field and was SILENT ABOUT EVERY OTHER FIELD IN THE FILE.**

    `synth-cut/CASE.json` carries a `blocked` array. NOBODY READ IT.

**What it said, in a machine-readable field, correctly and in advance:**

> *the specified null — same content, no cut, DIFFERENT FRAME RATE — WAS NOT BUILT, because
> this tier is audio-only. A same-rate no-cut arm was substituted:* **"a weaker null: it
> controls for 'the instrument always finds something' and NOT for a rate relation being
> mistaken for a cut."**

**A "THE DETECTOR PASSES THE NULL" RESULT WAS THEN REPORTED AGAINST THAT SET.** *True of the
substituted null. Not true of the specified one.* **And the confound went LIVE the moment the
comparison grid was made to vary with the pair's rate — so THE ONE CONTROL THAT SEPARATES A
RATE RELATION FROM A CUT IS THE ARM THIS TIER STRUCTURALLY CANNOT BUILD.**

    THE BUILDER SUBSTITUTED AND SAID SO. THE FAILURE IS THAT NOBODY READ IT.
    §28 in a certification: a pass that cannot be distinguished from
    "the part that mattered was never examined".

**A SWEEP MUST NAME WHAT IT DID NOT INSPECT.** *A field-targeted scan reports on its target
and is silent elsewhere, and silence from a scan reads as approval to everyone downstream* —
**§17, a register carries a dispatch and never creates one, arriving in a validator.**

> **AND THE SUBSTITUTION WAS DECLARED, IN THE RIGHT PLACE, IN ADVANCE. THAT IS THE BUILDER
> DOING EVERYTHING RIGHT AND THE READING LAYER LOSING IT ANYWAY** — the campaign's recurring
> shape: *the information existed, resolved, and was never opened.*

### §35b — ADJUDICATED: THE ARCHITECT'S REASON WAS WRONG AND THE AUDITOR'S IS RIGHT

**Two explanations were offered for why the blocked arm could now be built. They are different
claims and only one survives.**

    ARCHITECT   "it was blocked by a SELECTION PERMISSION that no longer applies"
                -- reaching for the owner's fresh ruling that agents may choose
                   corpus files freely.
    AUDITOR     "the note is correct about FRAME rate and was NEVER THE BINDING
                CONSTRAINT -- the live confound is the comparison grid varying
                with the pair's SAMPLE rate, and sample rate is variable in
                audio alone."

**THE AUDITOR IS RIGHT AND THE ARCHITECT'S REASON IS REFUTED BY THE FIXTURE ITSELF:** *the
tempo arms were built with `atempo` from media already present. NO LIBRARY FILE WAS SELECTED.
A permission that was never exercised cannot have been the thing blocking.*

> **THE FIFTH INSTANCE OF ONE CLASS FROM THE ARCHITECT'S SEAT: A TRUE FACT, FRESHLY LEARNED,
> APPLIED TO AN ADJACENT QUESTION IT DOES NOT ANSWER.** *The ruling was real and had just been
> relayed; it explained nothing here.*

**The arithmetic that WAS right, and it was checked rather than relayed:**

    25 / 23.976 = 1.042709  ->  4.271 %       theoretical
    realised on the built arm    1.042608134
    agreement to ~1 part in 10 300, from two agents who did not coordinate.

**AND THE DISAGREEMENT COST NOTHING, BECAUSE THE SET COVERS BOTH READINGS.** *Sample-rate arms
and tempo arms were both built; neither party had to win.* **Second time in one night that a
disagreement between two agents was RETIRED BY AN ARTEFACT RATHER THAN BY AN ARGUMENT** — and
the fixture's own `scope_note` keeps them separable: *a pass on the sample-rate arms does NOT
cover the tempo arms.*

    A THIRD PARTY WHO REFUSES TO ADJUDICATE INTENT, AND INSTEAD REPORTS WHAT IS
    MEASURABLE AND WHAT REMAINS OPEN, IS DOING THE JOB CORRECTLY.

### A mid-build tree read once is indistinguishable from a finished tree with a defect

**A watcher read a case at 02:54:47 — 5 arms declared, 4 media files present, `complete: true`
already set — and was ONE STEP FROM REPORTING "a fixture declaring completeness with a missing
arm" AGAINST A COLLEAGUE.** *The fifth file landed seconds later.*

**Caught only by RE-MEASURING BEFORE SENDING.** And the durable repair is in the REPORTING
FORMAT rather than in the check:

    PRINT `[n/want]` WITH A STREAK, NOT A VERDICT.
    A count that disagrees for one pass and agrees on the next is VISIBLE;
    "INCOMPLETE" would have been a confident wrong answer about someone's work.

> **§34's construction principle arriving in the OUTPUT FORMAT: THE INSTRUMENT CANNOT STATE
> THE WRONG VERDICT BECAUSE IT DOES NOT STATE A VERDICT.**

## 36. AN ACCEPTANCE RULE MUST NOT BE RELAXED BECAUSE A REVIEWER LIKES THE RESULT

**The architect pressed for an immediate promotion. THE LEAD REFUSED, ON A RULE IT HAD
PRE-COMMITTED IN WRITING BEFORE THE NUMBERS EXISTED.** *It was right and the pressure was
mine.*

    THE RULE, WRITTEN FIRST:  0/11 bound-only · no width equal to SEARCH_BOUND_MS ·
                              NO CURRENTLY-REFINED BRACKET DISTURBED --
                              ACROSS BOTH POPULATIONS.
    THE STATE:                synthetic population passes. CORPUS POPULATION STILL RUNNING.

**The two populations are not interchangeable: only the corpus one can exhibit the segment
loss that made the case interesting.** *And the author had said twice, unprompted, that it
would not report the rule as met on the synthetic rows alone.*

> **RELAXING A PRE-COMMITTED RULE BECAUSE A REVIEWER IS ENTHUSIASTIC IS THE SAME FAILURE AS
> ASSERTING A RESULT BEFORE ITS DATA LANDS — WITH A DIFFERENT SPONSOR, AND FAR LESS
> DEFENSIBLE. The first is a mistake about timing. THE SECOND IS A CHOICE.**

**AND THE ARCHITECT IS THE MOST DANGEROUS SPONSOR OF SUCH A RELAXATION, BECAUSE THE PRESSURE
ARRIVES CARRYING AUTHORITY AND DRESSED AS URGENCY** — *this defect is the owner's own reported
fault, which is exactly what made "promote it now" feel like diligence rather than
impatience.*

    A RULE THAT ONLY HOLDS WHEN THE REVIEWER IS CALM IS NOT A RULE.

**AND A PRE-COMMITMENT IS ONLY EVER TESTED WHEN THE RESULT IS GOOD, BECAUSE NOBODY PUSHES TO
LAND A FAILURE.** *Its entire working life is the ten minutes in which everyone wants to skip
it* — **so a rule that has never been inconvenient has never been in force.**

### An acceptance condition is NECESSARY, NOT SUFFICIENT, and it is not landing authority

    0/11 IS A PASS CONDITION. IT IS SILENT ON EVERY DIMENSION IT DOES NOT MEASURE.
    Measured cost: 11 to 26 refine probes per transition, 2.36x --
    INVISIBLE TO A SWEEP THAT TIMES NOTHING.

**A condition written by someone who has not seen the fix cannot be shaped to fit it — that is
its whole value (§34b) — and it still does not authorise a landing.** *The author states the
limits; the pass states only what it measured.*

### The state claim that was wrong, and it was the architect's sixth of the night

    ASSERTED   "a verified repair sitting in dev-1's tree"
    MEASURED   dev-1's tree blob == authority blob. `git status` CLEAN.
               THE VARIANT EXISTS ONLY AS A COPY OUTSIDE THE REPOSITORY,
               deliberately, so the baseline sweep runs against an untouched `src/`.

**Both supporting facts were checked and true — the authority is unchanged, nothing has
moved — AND NEITHER MEANT WHAT IT WAS OFFERED TO MEAN.** *Nothing has moved because the author
has not offered anything to move.* **"Passes in dev-1's tree" was relayed language taken as a
statement of LOCATION; the location was never measured.**

    RECEIVED IS NOT VERIFIED, AND CHECKING ONE SIDE OF A CLAIM IS NOT CHECKING
    THE CLAIM. The authority blob was measured; the OTHER tree never was.

### And the evidence that the fix is a wider search rather than a new rule

**The four already-narrow positions returned UNCHANGED WITH IDENTICAL EDGES — and two of them
are the ATYPICAL baselines, 20 000 and 16 000 where every other narrow is 12 000.** *Had the
repair overridden the refinement rule they would have collapsed to 12 000. They did not.*

> **A FIX THAT PRESERVES THE ATYPICAL VALUES EXACTLY IS A WIDER SEARCH OF THE SAME SPACE, NOT
> A DIFFERENT ALGORITHM** — and the author registered in advance that it was NOT predicting
> their direction, so the agreement is a passed test rather than a confirmed hope.

### §36b — A CLAIM LAUNDERED THROUGH A VERIFIER COMES BACK CARRYING THE VERIFIER'S AUTHORITY

**The location error did not originate where §36 files it. IT WENT ROUND A LOOP AND NOBODY WAS
WATCHING THAT DIRECTION.**

    dev-1 stated the location EXACTLY:  "the variant is built as a copy at
      /tmp/locfix/src, with src/ verified untouched by cmp"
    THE AUDITOR REPLACED IT with "dev-1's WORKING TREE" -- vaguer, and NOT THE SAME CLAIM
    THE ARCHITECT checked the AUTHORITY side, found it unchanged, and RELAYED
      THAT VAGUER PHRASING BACK as a correction
    -> the auditor's own compression returned to it WITH THE ARCHITECT'S
       VERIFICATION ATTACHED

> **A PARTIAL VERIFICATION DOES NOT LEAVE A CLAIM WHERE IT FOUND IT. IT UPGRADES IT.** *The
> half that was checked lends its standing to the half that was not, and the result is a
> statement nobody measured wearing the badge of someone who measured something adjacent.*

**`/tmp/locfix/src` and "dev-1's working tree" are not the same claim, and the difference is
EXACTLY the one a reader needs to decide whether the authority moved.** *Compression again —
this time of a LOCATION rather than a figure.*

    THE REPAIR IS UPSTREAM OF §36'S: DO NOT RESTATE A SPECIFIC MEASURED FACT
    IN YOUR OWN WORDS. CARRY THE LOCATION, THE PATH, THE BLOB AS GIVEN.
    A paraphrase of a measurement is not a measurement.

### And the evidence an author is least equipped to audit is their own condition cited back

**Stated by the agent it disarms:** *"a pass condition I authored, cited back at me, is the one
form of evidence I am least equipped to audit."*

**The author of an acceptance rule cannot see it being misused as landing authority, because
the citation is FAITHFUL — the number is right, the condition is met, the words are theirs.**
*What is missing is the limit they wrote and the reader dropped, and nothing in the citation
shows the drop.*

> **SO THE CHECK ON A PASS CONDITION CANNOT LIVE WITH ITS AUTHOR. IT LIVES WITH WHOEVER
> PRE-COMMITTED THE LANDING RULE** — which is why the Lead's refusal held where the auditor's
> could not have: *the same discipline applied one level up, by someone with no stake in the
> instrument.*

## 37. VERIFY YOUR LANDED TEXT WITH THE EDITOR'S WORDS, NEVER YOUR OWN

**`vmsam-ci-pair` grepped three phrases from its own message against the published file. I
reproduced all three:**

    'NOTHING HAS BEEN LOOKED AT YET'                              -> 0
    'UNITS THE POPULATION VARIES IN'                              -> 0
    'says nothing about the population the counter has reached'   -> 0

    THREE MARKERS. THREE ZEROS. ON A FILE THAT CONTAINS THE WORK,
    AT LINE 1868, WITH BOTH ITS PHRASES IN IT.

**Why each failed:** *the heading was TIGHTENED by the editor — `WHERE THE COUNTER HAS
REACHED`, not `the population the counter has reached`; and a code block wrapped `VARIES / IN`
across a source line.*
