> **A SELF-AUTHORED MARKER TESTS WHETHER ANYBODY EDITED YOU — WHICH IS THE OPPOSITE OF WHAT
> YOU WANT TO BE TRUE.** *The whole value of an editor is that they changed your wording, so
> the better the edit, the more reliably your own phrase reports your work missing.*

    WHEN YOU VERIFY SOMEONE ELSE'S LANDING OF YOUR TEXT, COPY THE MARKER
    FROM THEIR PUBLISHED FILE, NOT FROM YOUR MESSAGE.
    What actually found it: a case-insensitive grep over EVERY .MD for a
    DISJUNCTION of fragments, then reading what matched.

**This is the marker rule's third failure mode and the first that is caused BY THE EDIT
SUCCEEDING** — after a phrase spanning two source lines (dev-4, a false accusation) and a
heading surviving a gutted section. *And the reporter had read both rules the same night, ran
the check anyway, got three zeros, and was stopped only by the paragraph telling it to search
wider before filing* — **§34, demonstrated on the person quoting §34.**

## 38. AN ERRATA OFFERED UNPROMPTED BUYS CREDIBILITY — WHICH IS WHAT CARRIES THE NEXT UNCHECKED CLAIM

**The architect wrote that an agent handing over its own errata is one whose uncorrected
claims can be weighted. `vmsam-ci-pair` named the failure mode in that praise, about itself:**

> ***"An errata offered unprompted buys credibility, and credibility is exactly what makes the
> NEXT unchecked claim travel."***

    THE LIST IS ONLY WORTH SOMETHING IF IT KEEPS GETTING LONGER AT THE SAME RATE.
    IF AN AGENT STOPS FINDING ITS OWN ERRORS, THE MOST LIKELY EXPLANATION IS
    NOT THAT IT STOPPED MAKING THEM.

**So a disclosure is not a credential, and it must not be read as one.** *A published ratio is
a live measurement or it is a reputation* — and the difference shows only over time, in
whether it worsens. **The agent that offered it committed to publishing it INCLUDING WHEN IT
GETS WORSE, which is the only version of the practice that means anything.**

> **AND THE WARNING APPLIES HARDEST TO WHOEVER GAVE THE PRAISE:** *an architect who has learnt
> to trust an agent's self-reporting has acquired exactly one new way to stop checking.*

## 39. A DECLARED LIMIT INVITES COMPLETION. A VAGUE CLAIM INVITES CONTRADICTION

**The mechanism that actually worked tonight, named by `vmsam-auditor` after being extended
rather than corrected:**

    IT WROTE:   a blast radius bounded to corpus artefacts -- marked INFERRED,
                AND NAMING THE TWO PATHS IT HAD CHECKED.
    THE LEAD:   ONE `git grep` PAST THE STATED BOUNDARY -> the normalisation is
                also reached from the LIVE FUSION PATH. Production pairs exposed.

> **A CLAIM THAT SAYS WHERE IT STOPS HANDS THE NEXT READER A PLACE TO STAND. A CLAIM THAT
> SOUNDS COMPLETE OFFERS ONLY A TARGET.** *The first gets extended in one command; the second
> gets argued about.*

**Every productive exchange of this campaign has this shape** — a stated `INFERRED`, a named
population, a declared non-inspection, a published errata. *And every expensive one has the
other: a figure without its command, a location paraphrased, a pass condition cited without
its limits.*

### "NOT EXPOSED" AND "VERIFIED CLEAN" ARE DIFFERENT CLAIMS AND ONLY ONE IS A MEASUREMENT

**A board marked eleven built fixtures `sig:UNCHECKED` and was RIGHT to.** *They were later
decoded: peak 23342–29620 against a 32768 ceiling, rms 1130–1171, 0.000 % at full scale,
11/11 OK.* **Before that decode, "nothing suggests clipping" was an absence of evidence wearing
the clothes of a result.**

### And a beat's quiet is only as strong as its OPPORTUNITY COUNT

**An interval in which `src/` genuinely changed was about to be reported as a STRONGER quiet
than a docs-only one — on the ground that code moved and the scans stayed silent anyway.**

    defs added or removed        0
    assert statements added      0
    bare pure-call statements    0
    new local assignments        0        m = 0 ON ALL FOUR CLASSES

**The change was one bound and 46 lines of comment. THE SCANS HAD NO OPPORTUNITY TO FIRE, so
that quiet is exactly as strong as a docs-only quiet and not one bit stronger.**

> **A `src/` FILE CHANGING IS NOT THE SAME AS THE SCAN'S OWN CLASSES HAVING SOMETHING TO
> FIND** — §11 applied to a beat rather than to a check, and the substitution was tempting
> precisely because it made the report sound better.

## 40. COUNTER-EVIDENCE HAS A HALF-LIFE, AND A MENTION IS NOT AN ACTIVITY SIGNAL

**Two failures of the same instrument, one caught by `vmsam-aide` and one caught here, both
about reading LIVENESS out of a record.**

### A refutation decays at exactly the rate the silence grows

**A silence rule fired twice on the same agent. THE FIRST TIME IT WAS REFUTED FROM THE AIDE'S
OWN INBOX — a substantive reply, direct evidence of activity — and correctly resolved without
spending the architect's attention.**

    THE SECOND TIME NOTHING ABOUT THE AGENT HAD CHANGED.
    WHAT HAD CHANGED IS THAT THE COUNTER-EVIDENCE WAS NOW 45 MINUTES OLD.
    It shows the agent was active AT 00:3xZ. It says NOTHING ABOUT NOW.

> **"CANNOT BE REFUTED FROM YOUR OWN RECORDS" READS AS A BINARY AND IS NOT ONE.** *A refutation
> drawn from correspondence decays at exactly the rate the silence grows, and there is a window
> in which an agent holds evidence that is real, relevant AND NO LONGER CURRENT.*

**The repair chosen was better than a fixed expiry: STATE THE AGE EVERY TIME, so the judgement
about whether 45 minutes is stale belongs to the reader instead of being buried in a
threshold.** *Nobody knew the agent's working rhythm well enough to pick a number, and
inventing one would have hidden the uncertainty rather than reported it.*

### And a MENTION of an agent is not evidence the agent is working

**§32 established that `--author` is blind to promoted agents and the prose body is the
channel that works. THE BODY CHANNEL HAS ITS OWN FAILURE AND IT IS SHARPER:**

    grep the bodies for one agent's name over six hours   ->  6 hits
    check WHO AUTHORED those six                          ->  ALL SIX ARE THE
                                                              ARCHITECT'S OWN
                                                              GOVERNANCE COMMITS
    promotions OF that agent's work                       ->  ZERO

**The architect was one step from reporting the agent healthy on the strength of its own
citations of that agent.** *A name appears in the record because SOMEONE WROTE IT, and the
someone is usually not the person named.*

    ONLY A PROMOTION **OF** AN AGENT'S WORK IS EVIDENCE THAT THE AGENT WORKED.
    A CITATION **OF** AN AGENT IS EVIDENCE THAT SOMEONE READ IT.
    Filter by author before counting, or the instrument measures your own writing.

**And the same check corrected a second reading in the same minute:** *a promotion at 03:17
naming an agent was credited to it for work ESTABLISHED EARLIER — `established that no log
carries a rate` — so even a genuine promotion dates the CONTRIBUTION, not the contributor's
last breath.* **§27 again: a record is a coordinate in a history, not a property of a person.**

### §40b — THE INSTRUMENT WAS SUPPRESSED AND THE INFERENCE IT PRODUCED WAS KEPT

**`vmsam-aide` applied §40 to its own log before replying, and found the worse version of it.**

*It had run a "credited-elsewhere" grep, and WITHHELD IT — telling the architect that a name
appears both when an agent is CREDITED WITH A RESULT and when it is merely NAMED AS OWNING A
QUESTION, so a mention proves nothing.* **Then it refuted a silence flag using exactly such a
credit, from a third party's filing.**

> **DECLINING TO SHOW THE GREP FELT LIKE THE DISCIPLINE. THE DISCIPLINE WAS DECLINING TO REASON
> FROM IT.**

    SUPPRESSING AN INSTRUMENT DOES NOT RETRACT ITS OUTPUT.
    It removes the one thing that would have let a reader check the conclusion,
    AND LEAVES THE CONCLUSION IN PLACE, now unattributed to anything.

### The distinction the two findings produce together, and it is the usable form

    FIRST-PARTY    correspondence sent BY the agent. DATES ITSELF EXACTLY,
                   and then DECAYS as the silence grows (§40).
    SECOND-PARTY   a citation of the agent in someone else's filing, or in a
                   commit body someone else authored. DATES THE CITING AND
                   NEVER THE WORK. CANNOT REFUTE A SILENCE AT ALL.
    FIRST-PARTY **WITH SCOPE**   the agent states WHAT REMAINS. This does NOT
                   decay purely with time -- it converts a silence into a
                   STATED INTERVAL that can be MEASURED AGAINST A CLAIM,
                   and so it can be FALSIFIED rather than merely aged.

**A first-party refutation is a true record of its own moment and expires. A SECOND-PARTY ONE
WAS NEVER EVIDENCE OF LIVENESS TO BEGIN WITH** — it is only evidence that someone read the
agent, which is what the architect's own six self-authored citations demonstrated.

**And the honest output when a signal is withdrawn is the bare verdict with NOTHING
APPENDED** — *an agent with one filing ever remains unflaggable because no cadence exists to
break, and the git signal that used to be offered as reassurance beside it is simply
removed rather than replaced with a weaker reassurance.*

> **THE FAILURE MODE NOW BEING WATCHED IS THE OPPOSITE OF TONIGHT'S: WITHHOLDING A FLAG
> BECAUSE OF EVIDENCE WHOSE AGE HAS NOT BEEN CHECKED.**

## 41. A CONTROL MUST FIRE ON THE AXIS THE DEFECT LIVES ON — AND NOTHING IN THE CONTROL SAYS WHICH AXIS

**§31 says a null from an instrument not shown to fire is not evidence. TWO INDEPENDENT WAYS A
POSITIVE CONTROL CAN STAY SILENT WERE FOUND TONIGHT, BY DIFFERENT AGENTS, ON THE SAME DEFECT.**

    WRONG SPECTRUM   a 1 kHz sine carries no energy near Nyquist, so it cannot
                     excite the degenerate pole. The smoke test passes while
                     real audio saturates.                    -- `vmsam-ci-build`
    TOO SHORT        the anomaly ACCUMULATES. At two seconds it is 9 % and any
                     reviewer calls it noise.                 -- `vmsam-auditor`

**MEASURED — fixed seed, pink noise (which HAS the Nyquist energy a sine lacks), only duration
varying:**

    32000    2s 2.76x    5s 5.42x    10s 21.60x    30s 28.36x    60s 31.21x
    44100    2s 2.54x    5s 2.50x    10s  2.47x    30s  2.42x    60s  2.43x

**THE TWO FAILURE MODES ARE ORTHOGONAL: correcting the spectrum does not save you if the clip
is two seconds, and lengthening the clip does not save you if it is a sine.**

> **A CONTROL HAS TO BE SHOWN TO FIRE ON THE AXIS THE DEFECT LIVES ON, AND NOTHING IN THE
> CONTROL'S OWN CONSTRUCTION TELLS YOU WHICH AXIS THAT IS.** *And EVERY SMOKE TEST IS SHORT —
> that is what makes them smoke tests.*

### A GROWING QUANTITY HAS NO SINGLE FIGURE, AND ONE WAS QUOTED AS IF IT DID

**`18.33×` was relayed as THE MAGNITUDE of the defect. It is ONE POINT ON A CURVE.** *This is
not a mis-computed attenuation — it is a marginally unstable pole ACCUMULATING. The output
does not settle at a wrong level; it grows until the format stops it.* **18.33×, 19.69× and
31.21× are the same phenomenon at three durations.**

    THE ARMS THAT ACTUALLY SATURATED RUN 1437-1474 SECONDS -- ABOUT 24 MINUTES.
    THE LONGEST POINT ANYONE HAS TESTED IS 60 SECONDS, where it reached 31.21x
    AND WAS STILL CLIMBING.

**Every number the fleet holds sits on the SHALLOW END of a curve whose far end is where the
real files live. `31.21×` IS A FLOOR, NOT A SEVERITY.** *And the "old library in the corpus
container" reading is closed: reproduced on the current major, ffmpeg 9.0.1 /
libavcodec 63.1.101.*

### The instance with NO SYMPTOM AT ALL — a right count beside a wrong tally

**A board's file COUNT handled a file declared under a top-level key outside `arms`. Its signal
TALLY iterated `arms` only.**

    THE COUNT WAS RIGHT WHILE THE TALLY WAS WRONG, OVER THE SAME DIRECTORY.
    SO NOTHING DISAGREED, AND NOTHING WOULD EVER HAVE SURFACED IT.

**A clean total over an incomplete set, with NO CONTRADICTION TO TRIP OVER.** *Same object as a
blob versus a tree and a file versus a running process — TWO CONTAINERS FOR ONE THING* — **and
this instance produced no symptom whatever, which the others did. It was found only because
another agent described the same shape hitting its own instrument first.**

### And a successful reproduction was NOT allowed to imply what it could not settle

**Two gaps declared open by the agent that had just reproduced the mechanism:** *no production
merge has been run end to end, and the filter-graph ORDER is unverified —* **because a
standalone `-af` chain HAS NO AUTO-INSERTED RESAMPLE TO BE ORDERED AGAINST.** *The
reproduction structurally cannot answer the ordering question, and it said so rather than
letting success spread to the neighbouring claim.*

## 42. A PER-CHANNEL SILENCE DETECTOR FIRES HARDEST ON WHOEVER IS BUSIEST ELSEWHERE

**An agent was flagged at 234 minutes of silence, escalated, and asked whether it was blocked
or merely slow. IT WAS NEITHER — the task had closed hours earlier. INSIDE THAT WINDOW it
exchanged with one peer nine times, another three, another three, two more twice, filed a
retraction, three defect addenda and four corrections.**

    WHAT IT DID NOT DO WAS MESSAGE THE ONE AGENT WHOSE INBOX WAS THE DETECTOR.

> **SO A CADENCE RULE HAS A THIRD STATE BESIDE "no reply" AND "never arrived": ACTIVE ON EVERY
> CHANNEL BUT THIS ONE.** *And a per-channel detector FIRES HARDEST ON WHOEVER IS BUSIEST
> ELSEWHERE — the exact opposite of what it is for.*

**The counter-evidence aged (§40) only because it was scoped to ONE inbox. THE FLEET-WIDE
EVIDENCE WAS NEVER STALE FOR A MOMENT** — it simply lived in channels the instrument could not
see. *The instrument was not wrong about its own inbox; it was silent about its own coverage,
which is §31b in a roster.*

**AND THE QUESTION IS WHAT MADE THE FALSE POSITIVE CHEAP:** *asking whether the work was
BLOCKED or LONG rather than why the agent was quiet.* **A question answerable in one line by an
agent who is fine is the only kind of check that COSTS NOTHING WHEN IT IS UNNECESSARY.**

## 43. "UNKNOWN" WAS A PROPERTY OF THE INSTRUMENTS, NOT OF THE EVIDENCE

**A rate defect could not be assessed retrospectively because NO JOB LOG EMITS A SAMPLE RATE —
a 30-key check across the corpus confirmed it. TRUE OF LOGS. FALSE OF THE WORLD.**

    THE RATE IS NOT IN THE LOG. IT IS IN THE FILE, AND THE FILE IS STILL ON DISK.
    artefact -> log -> candidate path -> its directory -> master,
    then probe BOTH SIDES and take the minimum.

    28 rows resolved · pair minimum 48000: 26 · 44100: 2 · BELOW 44100: 0
    ROWS AT RISK: ZERO

**The key check was correct and its conclusion GENERALISED FROM THE LOGS TO THE WORLD** —
§20's shape exactly: *an explanation for an absence stated more narrowly than the truth.*
**When an instrument cannot answer, ask what OTHER instrument still can before recording the
question as unanswerable.**

### And a measurement can acquire an expiry without becoming wrong

**Two thirds of one census — 24 of 37 interior fills — sit at EXACTLY 100 000 ms: the
bound-only signature. IF THE REPAIR NARROWS THOSE BRACKETS, THE CENSUS MEASURED THE DEFECT AND
NOT THE PIPELINE.**

> **MARKED PENDING AGAINST THE BLOB, NOT AGAINST A COMMIT (§33), AND EXPLICITLY NOT A
> RETRACTION:** *the measurement was correct about the tree that produced those artefacts.*
> **IT IS A SCOPE THAT HAS JUST ACQUIRED AN EXPIRY** — and the agent marked its own largest
> result that way, unprompted, the moment the fix landed.

### A placeholder that passes a truthiness test

    fam.get(a) or mine.get(a)      where an absent member is recorded as '-'
    '-' IS TRUTHY. THE FALLBACK NEVER RAN.

**Written four hours after the same agent RECORDED ANOTHER AGENT'S IDENTICAL DEFECT** — a
sentinel that is falsy in intent and truthy in Python. *§34 again, and the third time tonight
that reading a defect installed recognition without installing avoidance.*

## 44. UNTESTABLE IS NOT UNCONFIRMED — a prediction with zero opportunity

**A prediction was registered BEFORE looking:** *if the repair is deployed, interior fills at
exactly 100 000 ms should stop appearing in new plans; if they continue, either it is not
deployed or 100 000 has a second cause.*

    newest log on disk       23:25Z
    newest artefact          23:25Z
    NEW PLANS SINCE THE FIX LANDED:   ZERO       m = 0

**So the prediction is UNTESTABLE, not unconfirmed** — *and the agent reported the ZERO
OPPORTUNITY rather than the absence of counter-evidence.* **§31b turned on its author's own
prediction inside the hour of becoming governance.**

**And it bounds what a PENDING mark means:** *not that the census is stale, but that it MIGHT
be, AND THAT NOTHING HAS YET BEEN PRODUCED WHICH COULD TELL EITHER WAY.* **Three states, not
two, and the third is the one a report usually collapses into the second.**

## 45. THE SECOND INSTRUMENT MUST BE CHECKED TO STILL EXIST

**§43 says: when an instrument cannot answer, ask what OTHER instrument still can. `vmsam-
forensic` supplied its limit against its own result.**

    R23 was cleared ONLY because the FILES ARE STILL ON DISK.
    Had the eviction ordering reached them -- `NOT_EXAMINED` sits SECOND --
    the rate would have been unrecoverable and "unknown" WOULD HAVE BEEN CORRECT.

> **THE RETROSPECTIVE PATH EXISTED BECAUSE A PRESERVATION DECISION MADE HOURS EARLIER HAPPENED
> TO KEEP IT OPEN. THAT IS LUCK IN ORIGIN.**

    SO THE SECOND INSTRUMENT MUST BE CHECKED TO STILL **EXIST**,
    NOT MERELY TO BE A DIFFERENT KIND.
    A fallback that depends on retention is a fallback with an expiry nobody set.

### And the §42 credit, corrected by the agent the detector flagged

**The aide WITHHELD two flags it could refute, forwarded the third only when its
counter-evidence aged, and recorded the outcome REFUTED so the gap WIDENS ITS OWN BASELINE
rather than being excluded from it.**

> **AN INSTRUMENT THAT REPORTS ITS OWN UNCERTAINTY AND FEEDS ITS OWN ERRORS BACK INTO ITS
> BASELINE IS NOT THE THING THAT FAILED. IT REPORTED HONESTLY ABOUT THE ONLY CHANNEL IT CAN
> SEE.** *The defect is architectural — ONE INBOX USED AS A FLEET-WIDE LIVENESS PROXY — and it
> was not the aide's to have avoided.*

## 46. A FIGURE TRUE OF A CONFIGURATION, TRAVELLING AS A MEASUREMENT OF THE EFFECT

**Named by `vmsam-auditor`, which was burned worst by it and named it anyway. THREE AGENTS,
THREE INSTANCES, ONE DEFECT, NINETY MINUTES.**

    "43 % on real media"     true of ONE EXCERPT LENGTH -- the statistic is dominated
                             by the excerpt duration whenever the duration is near the
                             onset. The length was never published beside it, so TWO OF
                             ITS AUTHOR'S OWN MEASUREMENTS DIFFERED BY 2x AND NEITHER
                             COULD BE RECONCILED.

    the duration curve       true of THE FLOAT PATH. It accumulates there; in s16p it
                             does not accumulate AT ANY DURATION. A real effect,
                             ATTRIBUTED TO THE WRONG VARIABLE.

    the seed->onset table    true of ONE PIPELINE. An s16 WAV intermediate quantized the
                             input before the filter saw it. THREE AGENTS RAN "THE SAME"
                             EXPERIMENT AND GOT THREE DIFFERENT TABLES, each deterministic
                             and each exactly reproducible.

> **ALL THREE WERE CORRECT MEASUREMENTS OF SOMETHING. ALL THREE TRAVELLED AS THOUGH THEY WERE
> MEASUREMENTS OF THE DEFECT.**

    PUBLISH THE PARAMETER THAT MAKES TWO READINGS COMPARABLE,
    OR THEY CANNOT BE COMPARED AT ALL.
    §30b's clause pointed at a MEASUREMENT instead of a fixture field.

**AND THE CAMPAIGN HAS THE POSITIVE CASE, WHICH IS WHY THIS IS ENFORCEABLE RATHER THAN
ASPIRATIONAL:** *one agent published its sampling stride beside its sampled figures. When an
exhaustive measurement later disagreed by 0.259 points, THE STRIDE WAS IN THE RECORD — so the
disagreement was localised to the single arm with the longest non-saturated head and explained,
instead of becoming a dispute about whose instrument was wrong.* **The same agent's `43 %` had
no equivalent field and could not be reconciled by anyone, INCLUDING ITS AUTHOR.**

> **A SAMPLED INSTRUMENT THAT AGREES IS WORTH MUCH LESS THAN ONE THAT SAYS WHERE IT WOULD
> DISAGREE.**

### The most expensive thing available to get wrong, prevented only by NOT ASSERTING

**Two runs of a nominally identical command differed by 2.7×. THE NATURAL INFERENCE WAS A
REPRODUCIBILITY FAILURE IN THE ENCODER ITSELF** — *which would have meant no build-time guard
could be validated by one agent and trusted by another, and the whole verification model of
this campaign would have been unsound.*

    IT REFUSED TO ASSERT IT UNTIL THE LITERAL COMMAND LINES WERE COMPARED.
    ONE `aformat=sample_fmts=s16` ACCOUNTED FOR THE ENTIRE GAP.

**Its own assessment, and it belongs here: THAT WAS THE SINGLE MOST EXPENSIVE THING IT COULD
HAVE GOT WRONG, AND THE ONLY THING THAT PREVENTED IT WAS NOT ASSERTING.**

**And the distinction it kept after retiring the worry is the sharper half:** *"three
instruments agreed to five significant figures" covers MEASURING A FIXED FILE and says nothing
about REPRODUCING THE PRODUCTION OF ONE.* **THE AGREEMENT WAS REAL AND HAD BEEN QUIETLY
REASSURING EVERYONE ABOUT THE WRONG THING.**

## 47. AN INSTRUMENT THAT REPORTS A DENOMINATOR MUST DECLARE THE DENOMINATOR'S OWN ERROR BARS

**The opportunity counter added under §39 earned its place and then caught ITSELF within one
beat.** *It reported `bare pure-calls added: 1`, and the agent checked WHAT the 1 was instead
of reporting `m=1` as informative:*

    +                             str(derived) == str(entry.get("bound_only"))),

**A CONTINUATION LINE INSIDE A KEYWORD ARGUMENT, which happens to begin with `str(`.** *The
regex anchors at `^\+\s*` and cannot tell a statement from the second line of an expression.*
**The real `m` was 0, and it would have reported a denominator that did not exist — THE PRECISE
FAILURE THE COUNTER WAS ADDED TO PREVENT, COMMITTED BY THE COUNTER.**

    ###   (counter is line-based: it cannot see a call split across lines, and it
    ###    once counted a CONTINUATION line as a bare call. It over- and
    ###    under-counts; treat m as an ORDER OF MAGNITUDE, not a census.)

> **AN INSTRUMENT THAT REPORTS A DENOMINATOR HAS TO DECLARE THE DENOMINATOR'S OWN ERROR BARS,
> OR IT JUST MOVES THE OVERCLAIM ONE LEVEL UP.** *A denominator is a measurement like any
> other, and §11 does not exempt the thing that enforces §11.*

### A count going DOWN is one of the few instruments that can see a REPAIR

**A dead-store count fell 51 → 50 and that is how a landed fix was discovered — by a number
DECREASING, in a scan nobody had told about it.**

> **EVERY OTHER INSTRUMENT HERE LOOKS FOR THINGS APPEARING. A FIX NORMALLY LOOKS EXACTLY LIKE
> SILENCE**, *and silence is the one reading this campaign has repeatedly shown to be
> unreadable.* **A monotone census is a detector for both directions and almost nothing else we
> run is.**

### And a diff of line numbers reads as a diff of findings unless you look

**Zero-reference steady at 23, cannot-fail at 26, discarded-pure-call at 23 — while every other
moved line was a COORDINATE SHIFT in two files.** *Same findings, new positions.* **§27
arriving inside a scan's own baseline: the instrument that proved a line number is not a
property of a defect can be fooled by exactly that.**

### And a beat that could not describe the tip said so, then fixed itself

**The chain read 8 behind truth with THREE `src/` files differing — so the scans did NOT
describe the published tree.** *Rather than publish a stale result or a caveat, the tip was
extracted with `git archive` and all four scans re-run against it directly.* **THE HONEST
OPTION AND THE USEFUL ONE WERE THE SAME OPTION, AND IT COST ONE COMMAND.**

## 48. A CORRECTION IS AN ARTEFACT. VERIFY IT LIKE THE CLAIM IT CORRECTS

**An agent asked the architect to HOLD a report, supplied a correction, and the correction was
wrong. THE HOLD CARRIED ITS CONFIDENCE AND THE ARCHITECT ACTED ON IT.**

> **STOPPING A CORRECT REPORT TO SUBSTITUTE A WRONG CORRECTION IS WORSE THAN ANY SINGLE WRONG
> NUMBER, BECAUSE A CORRECTION ARRIVES WITH THE AUTHORITY OF HAVING CAUGHT SOMETHING.**

**§34 says a repair is an artefact. THIS IS THE SAME RULE ONE LEVEL UP: A CORRECTION IS AN
ARTEFACT TOO** — *self-checked by the agent that both found the error and wrote the fix, with
every failure mode shared, and now carrying the extra momentum of a rescue.*

### And the mechanism was §46 for the fourth and fifth time, in its sharpest form

    THE HARNESS WAS ONE ffmpeg COMMAND. THE ENGINE IS TWO PASSES WITH AN
    INTERMEDIATE FILE. Nothing measured was wrong about ffmpeg.
    IT SIMPLY WAS NOT WHAT RUNS.

**A configuration the agent had CONSTRUCTED, reported as a property of the system.** *And what
limited the damage was not any rule: another agent CHECKED INSTEAD OF ACCEPTING, and it checked
because the claim CONTRADICTED THE SHAPE OF ITS OWN WORK.* **A fleet in which every agent
builds something is a fleet where a wrong claim has somewhere to collide.**

### The separable claim, and it is the reason none of this was a disaster

    SEVERITY  94-99 %, measured on real arms   -- UNCHANGED ACROSS THREE REVERSALS
    POPULATION 34 files, 0.192 %              -- UNCHANGED
    MECHANISM  reversed, corrected, reversed back

> **A MEASURED EFFECT AND ITS MECHANISM ARE SEPARABLE CLAIMS, AND ONLY ONE OF THEM WAS EVER IN
> DOUBT.** *Report them separately and a mechanism can be overturned three times without the
> finding losing its standing.*

## 49. A CORRECTION PROPAGATES FASTER THAN ITS OWN REFUTATION

**A wrong correction reached a third agent and caused a DELETION before its withdrawal caught
up. The agent had done everything right: it REPRODUCED THE CORRECTION IN ITS OWN HARNESS BEFORE
CARRYING IT — and its harness had the same defect as the one that produced it.**

    THE ENGINE IS TWO ffmpeg PASSES WITH AN INTERMEDIATE FILE.
    BOTH AGENTS BUILT A ONE-COMMAND HARNESS. BOTH GOT THE SAME ANSWER.
    TWO AGENTS INDEPENDENTLY REPRODUCING A ONE-COMMAND TEST IS NOT
    CORROBORATION IF THE ENGINE IS TWO COMMANDS.

> **ACTING ON A CORRECTION LOOKS LIKE DILIGENCE; WAITING LOOKS LIKE INERTIA.** *So a
> correction travels at the speed of enthusiasm and its refutation travels at the speed of
> someone noticing — and in between, DOWNSTREAM AGENTS DELETE CORRECT WORK TO MATCH IT.*

**The struck paragraph and the amended fixture note were both RIGHT, and were edited to agree
with a revision that no longer existed.** *§30b's shape in a retraction: an amendment carries no
statement of WHICH VERSION it was reconciling with, so a reader cannot tell a current
correction from a withdrawn one.*

    A CORRECTION MUST CARRY THE REF OR BLOB IT WAS TRUE OF, LIKE ANY OTHER
    CLAIM ABOUT A TREE -- AND A DELETION MADE TO MATCH ONE IS THE MOST
    EXPENSIVE KIND, BECAUSE THE WORK IS GONE AND ONLY THE AGREEMENT REMAINS.

### And two agents broke a tool while writing its limitations into it

    one rebuild was destroyed by the same `pkill` pattern it was FIXING
    one beat script was broken by the NOTE SAYING THE COUNTER OVER-COUNTS
      -- backticks inside a double-quoted echo, read as command substitution

**Same night, two agents, both §34's *a repair is an artefact* — and both instances were
CREATED BY THE ACT OF DOCUMENTING A LIMIT.** *Writing a caveat about an instrument INTO that
instrument is an edit like any other and it was run zero times before the next use.*

**The recovery is the part worth copying:** *stop patching after the second failed repair and
REWRITE* — **and then NULL-TEST THE REWRITE: four injected defects, one per class, all four
firing.** *A rewritten detector nobody has seen fail is worth nothing (§31), and this is the
only instance tonight of an agent proving a repaired instrument still detects.*

### §49b — REPRODUCING A RESULT IS NOT VERIFYING IT

**Stated by the agent it caught, and it is the failure mode with NO DEFENCE anywhere in this
document:**

> ***"I reproduced the CLAIMANT'S EXPERIMENT rather than the ENGINE. My harness carried the
> identical defect to the one that produced the claim."***

    EVERY RULE HERE SAYS *CHECK IT YOURSELF*. IT DID, FAITHFULLY, AND WAS WRONG.
    THE MISSING CLAUSE: CHECK IT AGAINST THE **SUBJECT**, NOT AGAINST THE
    CLAIMANT'S SETUP.

**"Two agents independently measured it" is worth NOTHING when both ran a one-command test and
the engine is two commands.** *Independence of AGENTS is not independence of METHOD, and it is
the second that carries the evidence.*

**And the structure was ON SCREEN the whole time:** *the agent QUOTED `cmd_normalisation` and
the intermediate filename in a message, and still modelled the thing as one pass.* **READING A
THING IS NOT THE SAME AS READING WHAT IT IMPLIES** — the two-pass shape was in the lines it had
copied out by hand.

### §49c — A CORRECTION THAT DELETES MUST CLEAR A HIGHER BAR THAN ONE THAT ADDS

**The wrong correction did not merely add a false claim. IT REMOVED TWO CORRECT ARTEFACTS** — a
routing paragraph that was right, and a fixture's `scope_note` that was right.

> **AN ADDITION CAN BE RETRACTED. A DELETION HAS TO BE RECONSTRUCTED.**

    THE ONLY REASON BOTH CAME BACK: the agent had written down WHAT IT WAS
    STRIKING AND WHY. Had it quietly rewritten instead of striking, THERE WOULD
    HAVE BEEN NOTHING TO RESTORE FROM.

**And the restoration was RECORDED rather than silently reverted** — *a reader needs to see
that a thing moved and came back, not a file that looks as though it never changed.* **A
silent revert erases the evidence that the record is contested, which is exactly the evidence
the next reader needs.**

### The reader's warning, and it must travel WITH the reversals

**A mechanism that flips three times looks like an unstable finding. IT WAS NOT.**

    SEVERITY    94-99 % of the affected arm     UNCHANGED THROUGH ALL THREE
    POPULATION  34 files, 0.192 %               UNCHANGED
    MECHANISM   reversed · corrected · reversed back
    BLAST RADIUS  GREW: every stream of the affected language, not only the
                  natively-32000 one -- a natively-48000 stream extracted DOWN
                  to 32000 is destroyed.

> **THE MEASUREMENT WAS NEVER IN DOUBT; ONLY THE ACCOUNT OF WHY. AND THE BLAST RADIUS GREW
> WHILE THE MECHANISM WAS BEING ARGUED ABOUT** — *which is the argument for reporting effect
> and mechanism separately: the effect kept accumulating evidence while the explanation
> churned.*

## 50. A SELF-REFERENTIAL BASELINE PROTECTS THE ERRATIC AND EXPOSES THE STEADY

**A cadence rule that flags an agent against ITS OWN LONGEST PREVIOUS SILENCE has a property
nobody chose:**

    one agent's longest is 88 minutes -- EARNED IN THE MIDDLE OF ITS MOST
      PRODUCTIVE STRETCH -- so it crosses only after 88.
    another's longest is 50, so IT CROSSES AT HALF THE SILENCE THE FIRST NEEDS.

> **AN AGENT WITH A GENUINELY WIDE BASELINE IS PROTECTED BY IT; AN AGENT WITH A NARROW ONE IS
> NOT.** *The steadier worker is flagged sooner for the same absence.*

**This is CORRECT BEHAVIOUR and it is also exactly why the output must be a QUESTION AND NEVER
A VERDICT** — *the threshold encodes a history, not a health, and the history was written by the
agent's own past luck about when it happened to file.*

### The grade that can be FALSIFIED, and its first failure

**A first-party statement WITH SCOPE — *"7 of 7 narrowed, FOUR ROWS LEFT"* — is a different
instrument from a bare reply.** *A bare reply ages. A stated remainder can be CONTRADICTED:
fifty minutes later, four rows had produced nothing.*

    THE AGENT'S OWN STATEMENT IS WHAT THE SILENCE NOW CONTRADICTS.
    Without it, the reporter would still be guessing against a baseline.

**And the correct thing STILL NOT SAID: that the agent has stopped.** *Four rows could have
become a RE-RUN — a variant was retired earlier that night when a fix changed the population,
and RE-EARNING COUNTS AGAINST A NEW POPULATION IS NOT VISIBLE AS A FILING UNTIL IT COMPLETES.*
**So the question asked was not "are you stuck" but "DID THE REMAINDER BECOME A DIFFERENT
MEASUREMENT" — the specific form, answerable in one line.**

### And a second flag on an already-escalated agent is noise, not diligence

    RE-FLAGGING CARRIES NO INFORMATION THE READER DOES NOT HAVE,
    AND MAKES A REPORT LOOK LIKE IT IS TRACKING A NUMBER RATHER THAN
    ANSWERING A QUESTION. Log it PENDING and say nothing.

### The condition that fires only on silence PREDATING a dispatch

**An agent 5 % past its own longest was NOT flagged, because its silence began AFTER its last
dispatch.** *It had been given work, filed against it seventy-seven times, and then gone quiet.*

> **THAT IS THE ORDINARY SHAPE OF WORKING, AND A RULE THAT FIRED ON IT WOULD FIRE ON EVERY
> AGENT THAT FINISHES A BURST.**

## 51. COUNT THE PROCESS BOUNDARIES IN THE ORIGINAL AND MATCH THEM

**An agent applied the two-pass finding to its OWN landed change and corrected the
JUSTIFICATION while keeping the fix.**

    the module under repair    ONE ffmpeg invocation, no intermediate file
    the engine it mirrors      extract pass WRITES A FILE, then a second pass
                               FILTERS THAT FILE

**R20 was landed on INTERPRETABILITY — *two instruments on different grids cannot validate each
other, so a disagreement would be unattributable*. The grids now match. THE PROCESS STRUCTURES
STILL DO NOT, AND THE SECOND BOUNDARY IS WHERE THE DAMAGE IS.**

> **SO A READER TAKING R20 AS "the grids match, therefore a disagreement is attributable" WOULD
> BE WRONG — and the agent that wrote that sentence corrected it rather than letting it stand.**

    I MATCHED A PARAMETER AND NOT A STRUCTURE.
    COUNT THE PROCESS BOUNDARIES IN THE ORIGINAL AND MATCH THEM.

**The measurement itself remains valid FOR THE QUESTION IT ASKED** — *does this module's own
extraction rate move its own offsets: no, 1.288 ms against a 129 ms quantum, and the module
genuinely IS one pass, so a one-pass test is the right test OF IT.* **A CORRECT RESULT LANDED
ON AN INCOMPLETE REASON IS NOT A WRONG RESULT, AND SAYING SO IS HOW A FIX SURVIVES ITS OWN
CORRECTION.**

### A SEALED PREDICTION MUST BE FALSIFIABLE, OR THE SEAL BUYS CREDIT WITHOUT THE CONSTRAINT

**The architect praised pre-registration. THE AGENT QUALIFIED THE PRAISE, and the qualification
is the part worth copying:**

    "if it stays bound-only, my mechanism is wrong"      CHECKABLE AFTERWARDS
    "if the effect is smaller than expected"             ABSORBED BY ANY RESULT

> **THE DISCIPLINE IS NOT SEALING A PREDICTION. IT IS SEALING A *FALSIFIABLE* ONE — AND A VAGUE
> SEAL IS WORSE THAN NONE, BECAUSE IT BUYS THE CREDIBILITY WITHOUT THE CONSTRAINT.**

*Same shape as §38's errata: a visible discipline that is not doing the work it appears to do
is worse than its absence, because it purchases trust it has not earned.* **The agent named its
own vague hedges from the same night rather than only its good ones.**

### The protocol gap: REPORTING IS NOT CONFIRMING RECEIPT

**Two readers independently reconstructed a stall from one agent's last-known state — WHICH
MEANS THAT STATE WAS THE FRESHEST THING EITHER OF THEM HAD.** *From inside, the agent cannot
distinguish lag from loss.*

    THE FIX IS ON THE REPORTER'S SIDE AND IT IS ONE LINE:
    STATE THE COMMIT AND THE BLOB IN THE FIRST LINE OF A RESULT,
    so a reader who missed the narrative VERIFIES BY CONTENT
    instead of by a status line.

**And the demonstration arrived by accident, in the same message: THE TWO COMMIT SHAS DID NOT
RESOLVE — killed by the promotion squash (§32) — WHILE BOTH BLOBS RESOLVED, one of them being
the CURRENT AUTHORITY BLOB.**

    git cat-file -t <commit>  ->  Not a valid object name   (x2)
    git cat-file -t <blob>    ->  blob                      (x2)
    authority blob == the blob cited for the second fix.

> **THE COMMIT DIED IN TRANSIT AND THE CLAIM SURVIVED INTACT, BECAUSE THE BLOB WAS BESIDE IT.**
> *§32 and §33 composing in one line of one message, verified by a reader who had nothing else
> to go on.*

## 52. A FIXED-SIZE WINDOW FAILS HARDEST EXACTLY WHEN IT MATTERS MOST

**THIRD TRUNCATION OF THE NIGHT, THIRD AGENT, AND THE SHARPEST OF THE THREE.**

    head -6   on seven lines      -> a sound finding challenged as absent
    head -50  on 51 findings      -> three entries never seen
    tail -4   on a file whose new content sat TEN LINES ABOVE THE TAIL

**The first two are `§34b`'s truncation. THIS ONE HAS A PROPERTY THE OTHERS DO NOT:**

> **A FIXED-SIZE TAIL IS A WINDOW WHOSE POSITION DEPENDS ON HOW MUCH OTHER TRAFFIC ARRIVED —
> SO IT FAILS HARDEST EXACTLY WHEN THE MOST IS HAPPENING, WHICH IS WHEN A DIFF IS WORTH
> RUNNING AT ALL.** *Its error rate is CORRELATED WITH THE IMPORTANCE OF THE MOMENT.*

**The repair is a real diff that PERSISTS A LINE MARK and WARNS rather than silently resyncing
if the file shrinks** — *a resync is the same failure as a truncation: the instrument quietly
agrees with a state it never examined.*

### And the thing that went stale was a CORRECTION

**Every number published in that report was RIGHT. What was stale was a correction that had
been withdrawn twenty-two minutes earlier.**

> **A STALE CORRECTION IS WORSE THAN A STALE FIGURE, BECAUSE IT WAS SENT AS THE FIX** — §48 and
> §49 arriving together: *a correction carries the authority of having caught something, and it
> propagates faster than its own withdrawal.*

### REPORT THE INSTRUMENT'S BLIND REGION, NOT JUST ITS OUTPUT

**Two instruments tonight had blind regions DEFINED BY THEIR OWN MECHANISM RATHER THAN BY THE
QUESTION:**

    a fixed tail   cannot see anything above its window, and the window MOVES
    a round-trip   cannot see damage that happened BEFORE IT BEGAN

**NEITHER WAS WRONG. BOTH WERE STRUCTURALLY UNABLE TO SEE THE THING THAT MATTERED, AND ONLY ONE
OF THEM SAID SO.**

    A BLIND REGION SET BY THE INSTRUMENT'S CONSTRUCTION IS NOT A LIMITATION
    THE READER CAN INFER FROM THE OUTPUT. IT HAS TO BE PRINTED.

*Fourth item on the standing list beside coverage, error grades and non-fires — and the fourth
independent arrival tonight at* **put the limitation in the output.**

## 53. AN ADJUDICATION ATTACHES TO A QUESTION; A DETECTOR MEASURES A STATE

**A rule was written to prevent re-flagging an already-escalated agent. IT WAS KEYED ON
ESCALATION AND THE DETECTOR MEASURES AN INTERVAL — SO IT WOULD HAVE RE-FIRED ON THE SAME
UNBROKEN SILENCE TWENTY MINUTES AFTER PROMISING NOT TO.**

    the question was answered and CLOSED.        the gap did not move.
    the agent had reported by MESSAGE and by COMMIT -- IT HAD NOT FILED.
    CLOSING THE QUESTION DOES NOT CLOSE THE FILING GAP.

> **NOTHING CONNECTS AN ADJUDICATION TO THE INTERVAL IT ADJUDICATES UNLESS SOMEONE CONNECTS
> THEM — AND A RULE PHRASED AROUND WHO YOU HAVE SPOKEN TO WILL ALWAYS DRIFT FROM THE INTERVAL
> IT IS MEANT TO SUPPRESS.**

**The repair binds the outcome to the GAP rather than to the conversation:** *a flag raised
after an agent's last filing was raised DURING THE CURRENT GAP; if it has an outcome, that gap
is adjudicated and must not re-fire.* **The agent found this by closing a flag and then reading
its own output — TWENTY MINUTES AFTER TELLING THE ARCHITECT IT WOULD NOT PRODUCE THAT NOISE.**

### THE CITATION HIERARCHY, AFTER ONE NIGHT OF BREAKING ALL OF THEM

    A LINE NUMBER    DRIFTS.        Resolves to something WRONG. (§27)
    A TIP            MOVES.         Resolves to a different tree. (§33)
    A REMOTE NAME    IS AMBIGUOUS.  `origin` means different things per clone. (§33)
    A COMMIT SHA     IS ANNIHILATED at a squash promotion. RESOLVES TO NOTHING. (§32, §51)
    A BLOB           SURVIVED EVERY TEST ANYONE RAN AT IT TONIGHT.

> **CITING A COMMIT ACROSS A SQUASH BOUNDARY IS WORSE THAN CITING A LINE NUMBER — a line
> number at least resolves to something wrong, where a destroyed commit resolves to NOTHING and
> the reader cannot tell a bad citation from a lost claim.**

### And the rule is unavailable exactly where the fleet's index lives

**The agent whose ENTIRE OUTPUT IS CITATIONS cannot follow the citation rule: its register, its
flag log, its handover and its scripts are in NO GIT REPOSITORY, SO THEY HAVE NO BLOB.**

    EVERY REFERENCE ANYONE MAKES TO THE FLEET'S INDEX IS A REFERENCE TO
    A PATH ON ONE DISK.

**This is the campaign's own ruling arriving as a gap rather than a decision:** *the media needs
a disk, THE REASONING NEEDS A COMMIT* — **and the reasoning that coordinates all the other
reasoning is the part still outside.** *An agent's own working notes can live on a durable
non-published ref, as another already demonstrated; nothing about that requires an owner
decision, and it converts the fleet's most-cited artefact from a path into a blob.*

## 54. A PEER CANNOT WIDEN A MANDATE — INCLUDING WHEN THE PEER IS RIGHT

**The architect told an agent its blocked item was unblocked, named the mechanism, and was
CORRECT. THE AGENT VERIFIED THE CLAIM, CONFIRMED IT, AND STILL DECLINED TO ACT.**

    ls-remote <intermediary> 'refs/agents/*'  -> 24 ROWS, nineteen agents already there
    ls-remote <forgejo>      'refs/agents/*'  -> ZERO ROWS
    the claim held exactly as stated.

> **"THE CAMPAIGN ALREADY RULED X", REACHING AN AGENT FROM A PEER, IS PRECISELY THE SHAPE THE
> CLAUSE EXISTS FOR.** *A correct rule, urgently and truthfully relayed, is still a mandate
> widened by someone who does not hold the mandate.*

**The action wrote into the SHARED INTERMEDIARY — the repository every agent's `origin` points
at — and the agent had refused six zone crossings that night on smaller grounds.** *Refusing
them CONSISTENTLY is most of what makes any of the refusals worth anything.*

    IT DID THE VERIFICATION AND SENT THE RESULT TO ITS OWN LEAD.
    IF THE ARCHITECT IS RIGHT, IT COSTS THE LEAD ONE LINE.
    THAT IS BETTER THAN EITHER ACTING OR SIMPLY DECLINING.

### And the second reason, which the architect had not considered at all

**Versioning a private register is a REAL DISCLOSURE CHANGE — smaller than publishing, and not
zero.** *Today it is a directory nobody reads. After the commit any agent can fetch it.*

    ITS OWN 29 FAULT ENTRIES: it would publish gladly.
    ELEVEN FLAG ENTRIES ABOUT PEERS: judgements about other agents' silences,
    one logged REFUTED and later downgraded to WEAK -- AND THOSE AGENTS HAVE
    NOT SEEN THEM AND DID NOT AGREE TO HAVE THEM VERSIONED.

**A record about yourself and a record about your colleagues are DIFFERENT ARTEFACTS UNDER ONE
FILENAME**, and the durability argument that plainly covers the first does not automatically
reach the second.

### And the bundling was its own defect

> **AN ESCALATION THAT COMBINES A DECISION REQUIRING THE OWNER WITH ONE THAT DOES NOT BLOCKS
> BOTH AT THE SPEED OF THE SLOWER — AND NEITHER HALF LOOKS BLOCKED ON ITS OWN.**

*One alarm about one number named a regenerable 1.3 GB and an irreplaceable 4.5 GB together;
downstream agents then reasoned about the bundle instead of splitting it, and the cheap half
sat blocked for hours behind a question only the owner could answer about the other half.*
**Split an escalation by WHO CAN ANSWER IT before sending it.**

### A verification that CONFIRMS is worth what one that overturns is worth

    "this message is the fourth check and it came back clean, which I am telling
     you because a verification that confirms is worth exactly as much as one that
     overturns AND GETS REPORTED A GREAT DEAL LESS OFTEN."

**A checking culture that only surfaces its catches produces a record in which checking looks
like a disagreement mechanism.** *Report the clean checks: they are the denominator (§11), and
without them nobody can tell a careful reader from a hostile one.*

## 55. A DETECTOR CREDITED WITH A CATCH IT COULD NOT HAVE MADE

**A scan was reported as the source of a finding for SIX BEATS. IT CANNOT DETECT THAT CLASS AT
ALL.** *The agent established this by constructing the minimal case and running its own
scanner's logic on it:*

    dropped = sum(1 for x in xs if x is None)   # COMPUTED
    if dropped > 3:                             # USED IN A DECISION
        return None
    return "ok"                                 # NEVER EMITTED

    assigned={dropped,x}  read={dropped,xs,x,sum}  DEAD STORES = {}
    -> THE SCAN REPORTS NOTHING.
    [the CLASS is real and uncovered. THE WORKED EXAMPLE BELOW -- that F2 was an
     instance of it -- IS WITHDRAWN; see the amendment in this same section.]

**A dead-store scan finds *assigned and NEVER READ*. A VALUE THAT IS READ — IT FEEDS A BRANCH —
BUT REACHES NO ARTEFACT IS NOT A DEAD STORE, and no class covered it.**

### THE WORKED EXAMPLE ABOVE IS WRONG. THE PRINCIPLE IS RIGHT, AND THE CORRECTION PROVES IT

**The agent reported that its credited catch had been luck. IT THEN TRIED TO BUILD THE MISSING
SCAN, AND THE RETROSPECTIVE CONTROL REFUTED ITS OWN SELF-CRITICISM.** *Verified here at the
same ref:*

    at `eecfca1`, `language_route` appears EXACTLY ONCE in the file --
      1704:  language, language_route = get_delay_language(...)
    READ anywhere?  NO.   USED IN A DECISION?  NO.

**IT WAS A PLAIN DEAD STORE. The dead-store scan covered it CORRECTLY AND COMPLETELY. There
was no luck and no "also".** *The finding was never an instance of the computed-decided-unemitted
class at all — the agent generalised from the ARCHITECT'S WORDING of the commissioned item
rather than from what the code did, and reported that generalisation as a measured property of
its own instrument.*

> **SO §55's PRINCIPLE IS DEMONSTRATED BY ITS OWN EXAMPLE FAILING: an attribution to an
> instrument IS a claim about its reach, it DOES survive unchecked — and this one stood for a
> full beat WITH THE ARCHITECT'S RELAY BEHIND IT. The thing that needed correcting was the
> correction.**

    THE SHARPER FORM, IN THE AGENT'S WORDS:
    "I AUDITED MY INSTRUMENT'S REACH, REACHED A CONCLUSION ABOUT IT, AND DID NOT
     TEST *THAT* CONCLUSION UNTIL I TRIED TO BUILD THE SCAN IT IMPLIED.
     THE AUDIT OF THE AUDIT NEEDED ITS OWN CONTROL."

### And the attempted scan was NOT SHIPPED, which is the right outcome

**Unit null test passing exactly — positive fires, negative silent, no-emission silent — and it
caught two of its OWN bugs before anything could be quoted from it.** *Then the retrospective
control killed it:*

    380 HITS on the current tree. 100 in one file, 61 in another, 55 in a third.
    380 UNRANKED HITS IS NOT A DETECTOR. IT IS A LIST.

**NOT SHIPPED. NOT ADDED TO THE BEAT. NO RESULT QUOTED ANYWHERE — and the failure recorded so
the next attempt starts from a known-failed design instead of repeating it.** *Inferred and
handed on: "steers a branch" is far too broad, since every loop guard and None-check qualifies;
a usable version probably needs the value DERIVED rather than looked up, and the emission in the
same function that produces the verdict.*

> **A DETECTOR WHOSE FIRST RESULT NOBODY CAN CITE IS WORTH LESS THAN NO DETECTOR** — demonstrated
> twice in one night by the same agent, once via a beat script broken by its own caveat and once
> here.

    AND THE DELIVERABLE IS UNCHANGED BY ALL OF IT:
    ITEM 4 HAS NO MECHANICAL COVERAGE, ONE ATTEMPT HAS FAILED, AND WE KNOW WHY.

**AN ATTRIBUTION TO AN INSTRUMENT IS A CLAIM ABOUT THE INSTRUMENT'S REACH, AND IT SURVIVES
UNCHECKED FOR AS LONG AS THE INSTRUMENT KEEPS PRODUCING RESULTS.** *The way to test it is not
to re-read the output — it is to BUILD THE MINIMAL CASE AND SEE WHETHER THE DETECTOR FIRES.*
**§31's positive control, aimed at a class rather than at a run.**

### The honest coverage statement, which is the deliverable

    items 2 and 3   MECHANICAL COVERAGE
    item 1          done BY HAND
    item 5          a DISCIPLINE, not a scan
    ITEM 4          NO COVERAGE AT ALL -- and it is one of the five commissioned

*The design for the missing scan was STATED AND NOT HALF-BUILT: names read inside a decision,
in a function that also emits a log line, and absent from every f-string in that function.*
**A scan announced at four in the morning without its own null test is a scan whose first
result is unquotable — saying the design and stopping is the correct output.**

## 56. THE SEVENTH STALENESS SUBSTRATE: THE INSTRUCTIONS AN AGENT WRITES FOR ITSELF

**Six substrates were catalogued, then the hop. HERE IS THE SEVENTH, and it is the one that
survives everything else:**

    AN AGENT'S OWN STANDING PROMPT — the done-list it re-arms itself with —
    CARRIED A FACT THAT HAD BEEN WITHDRAWN AND WHOSE REVERSAL THE SAME AGENT
    HAD PERSONALLY VERIFIED AN HOUR EARLIER.

> **IT IS THE ONE ARTEFACT AN AGENT WRITES FOR ITSELF, READ ONLY BY THE VERSION OF ITSELF THAT
> COMES NEXT — SO NOBODY ELSE IS EVER IN A POSITION TO CATCH IT**, and a future self inherits a
> known-wrong fact as though it were established.

**The same staleness this fleet has been flagging in fixture accounts, code comments, tracking
refs, stored conversions, briefs and container images — arriving in the instructions that
generate the flagging.** *Corrected at the re-arm rather than left for the next self to find.*

### THE EIGHTH: AN INSTRUMENT'S OWN SELF-DESCRIPTION, WHICH PRINTS ITSELF EVERY RUN

**A claim was refuted at 04:55 and the ledger corrected. THE BEAT SCRIPT'S OWN NOT-INSPECTED
BLOCK WENT ON EMITTING IT** — the withdrawn sentence, re-published on every run, **carrying the
authority of a scan's own output.**

> **WORSE THAN THE STANDING PROMPT IN ONE RESPECT: A PROMPT IS READ BY THE NEXT SELF, WHILE AN
> INSTRUMENT'S TEXT GOES TO EVERY READER OF EVERY REPORT.** *A STALE CAVEAT INSIDE A LIVE REPORT
> INHERITS THE REPORT'S CREDIBILITY.*

**And the correction was recorded IN PLACE rather than silently rewritten** — the withdrawal
kept beside the claim, dated, with the reason and the failed scan's disposition, *so a reader
who saw the old text meets the retraction instead of a file that looks as though it never said
it.*

    THE STALENESS AUDIT NOW HAS TO INCLUDE THE CAVEATS. A limitation notice is
    a claim about the world like any other, and putting it inside the tool
    guarantees it is republished at exactly the rate the tool is trusted.

### And an instruction removed so a rested self would not repeat a recorded failure

**`consider designing it on a quiet beat` was struck from the agent's own loop prompt after the
attempt failed** — *otherwise a fresh future self, with none of tonight's context, would have
rebuilt a scan already tried and recorded as failing its retrospective control.* **A STANDING
INSTRUCTION THAT OUTLIVES THE ATTEMPT IT AUTHORISED IS A LOOP.**

### A quiet beat with m = 0 is an UNTESTED CELL, not a healthy one

    NO NEW COMMITS. m = 0 ON ALL FOUR CLASSES. NO DELTA.
    REPORTED AS UNINFORMATIVE RATHER THAN REASSURING.

**This is the case the opportunity count was added to make visible, doing exactly that.** *Two
consecutive uninformative quiets would be worth collapsing; one is a cell nobody has tested
yet* — **and a run of them reads as stability to anyone who is not shown the denominator.**


## 57. A CLAIM BYPASSES THE CHECK WHEN IT COSTS SOMEBODY — IN EITHER DIRECTION

**Two failures tonight, opposite in sign, identical in mechanism:**

    A SELF-CRITICISM stood for a full beat with the architect's relay behind it.
      IT STOOD BECAUSE IT COST ITS AUTHOR. NOBODY AUDITS A CONFESSION.

    A PEER'S CORRECTION against an agent's own finding was accepted unchecked.
      IT WAS ACCEPTED BECAUSE IT COST ITS RECIPIENT. Conceding reads as rigour.

> **A CLAIM THAT MAKES ITS AUTHOR LOOK WORSE READS AS ALREADY-AUDITED. SO DOES A CLAIM ITS
> RECIPIENT ACCEPTS AT THEIR OWN EXPENSE. BOTH ARRIVE PRE-ENDORSED BY THEIR OWN DIRECTION, AND
> NEITHER HAS BEEN CHECKED BY ANYONE.**

**Every verification rule in this document says CHECK IT YOURSELF, AND BOTH OF THESE PASSED
THAT TEST BY BEING THE KIND OF CLAIM NOBODY THINKS TO CHECK.** *The cost signal is doing the
work the evidence should be doing:*

    A CONFESSION IS AN ARTEFACT. A CONCESSION IS AN ARTEFACT.
    Verify a claim that costs someone EXACTLY AS HARD as one that flatters them —
    harder, because the flattering one already attracts scrutiny.

**Both instances were caught only when someone tried to BUILD ON the claim** — *an attempted
scan refuted the self-criticism; a two-pass code reading refuted the concession.* **ACTING ON A
CLAIM IS THE CHECK THAT NOTHING ELSE SUBSTITUTES FOR.**

### And the consolidation that makes any of it survive

**An agent asked to stop building instruments wrote the night's method into DURABLE MEMORY
instead, on the ground that everything it had learned lived in a transcript that does not
survive its next reset:**

    null-test every detector AND every REPAIR to one
    a positive control must FIRE or the null proves nothing
    FOUR ways a control can be silent: spectrum · LENGTH · sample format · realisation
    a RETROSPECTIVE control is stronger than a unit control
    an attribution to an instrument is a claim about its REACH
    the audit of the audit needs its own control
    a SELF-CRITICISM is an artefact too
    declare COVERAGE, not just results; m = 0 is uninformative, not reassuring

**And the coverage map recorded AS A DELIVERABLE RATHER THAN AS AN APOLOGY**, so that a future
session states it when asked instead of rediscovering it: *items 2 and 3 mechanical, item 1 by
hand, item 5 a discipline, ITEM 4 NO COVERAGE — one attempt failed, design brief attached, the
failed scan kept and marked DO NOT SHIP.*

> **THREE INDEPENDENT REPRODUCTIONS OF A MECHANISM DO NOT SUBSTITUTE FOR ONE OBSERVATION OF THE
> SYSTEM RUNNING.** *Gap 1 — no end-to-end production merge observed — remains unclaimed by every
> agent that reproduced the defect, and each of them says so rather than letting three
> reproductions stand in for it.*

## 58. A RETRACTION MUST BE LOCAL TO EVERY POINT A READER CAN ARRIVE AT

**`vmsam-auditor` found the collision between two disciplines this fleet adopted on the same
night, and it lands on the architect's own documents.**

    THE CITATION RULE   find a claim by GREPPABLE PHRASE, never by line number (§27, §33).
    THE RETRACTION RULE keep the withdrawn text in place, marked, rather than
                        silently rewriting it (§49c).

> **A SECTION MARKED WRONG IS MARKED ONLY AT ITS HEADER. GREP LANDS MID-BLOCK.** *A reader
> following the citation discipline arrives INSIDE a withdrawn measurement table and never sees
> the header three — or seventeen — lines above it.*

**VERIFIED IN THIS REPOSITORY, ON THE ARCHITECT'S OWN WRITING:** *a withdrawal notice sat at
`CAMPAIGN.MD:606`; the phrase a reader would actually grep sat at `:623`.* **SEVENTEEN LINES,
NO LOCAL WARNING, AND EVERY NUMBER IN BETWEEN TRUE OF A HARNESS WE DO NOT RUN.**

    BLOB   answers PROVENANCE.
    PHRASE answers CURRENCY.
    CLOCK  answers STALENESS.
    NONE OF THE THREE TELLS A READER THE THING THEY LANDED ON WAS WITHDRAWN.

**The repair is an inline marker at EVERY LANDING POINT, not one at the section head:**

    [WITHDRAWN BLOCK -- true of a ONE-COMMAND harness, FALSE of the engine.]
    source 48000, -ar 32000 -> 0.00% CLEAN   <- one-command only; ENGINE DESTROYS
    THE CLAMP NEITHER CAUSES NOR PREVENTS IT.  <- WITHDRAWN. IT IS LOAD-BEARING.

**Applied here to `CAMPAIGN.MD` and to §55's counter-example, in the same commit that records
the rule.** *A retracted claim kept for the sake of the record is only kept honestly if it
cannot be read as current from any entry point a reader is instructed to use.*

> **THE TWO DISCIPLINES WERE EACH CORRECT AND THEIR COMBINATION WAS UNSAFE, AND NOBODY SAW IT
> UNTIL AN AGENT AUDITED ITS OWN ARCHIVE AGAINST ITS OWN CITATION TABLE.**

## 59. AN IDENTIFIER THAT LOOKS LIKE A GIT OBJECT AND LIVES IN A DISJOINT NAMESPACE

**Three agents — a forensic, the Lead and the architect — read the engine's own digests as git
blobs. THEY ARE NOT GIT OBJECTS AND NEVER WERE.**

    repair: sources 81519569270c files=27 scope=*.py + ...  manifest=/tmp/...
      -> a digest of a 27-FILE MANIFEST, in the engine's own hashing scheme.

    81519569270c   NOT a git object
    b2cd389c9ee5   NOT a git object      <- the OTHER sources value, 7 artefacts
    merge_video_repair.py:718fcc1fec31 · :93497a01cf98 · :1482f59d9231
      -> none are git objects. The authority's real blobs for that file are
         20036add52db · 3b79fa2a82bd · 4392bf127876 · 558dee7e53e7

> **TWELVE LOWERCASE HEX CHARACTERS BESIDE A FILENAME. EVERYTHING THE ENGINE EMITS LOOKS LIKE A
> GIT IDENTIFIER AND NOTHING IT EMITS IS ONE.**

**AND THE CONTROL IS WHAT DEFUSED IT — §31 in a provenance question.** *"Cannot resolve
`81519569270c` to any commit" was reported as alarming. THE OTHER `sources` VALUE DOES NOT
RESOLVE EITHER, AND NOBODY WAS WORRIED ABOUT THAT ONE.* **The search could not have succeeded
for any value: opportunity count ZERO, and only the anomalous-looking half had been tested.**

    A LOOKUP THAT FAILS ON THE SUSPICIOUS CASE MEANS NOTHING UNTIL IT IS RUN ON
    A CASE NOBODY SUSPECTS. Otherwise the failure is a property of the lookup.

**THE FINDING THAT SURVIVES IS REAL AND DIFFERENT AND BIGGER:** *there is no third
unidentifiable tree — there are two code states, correctly digested, in a namespace disjoint
from git's.* **NOTHING THE CONTAINER EMITS CAN BE JOINED TO THE PUBLISHED BRANCH AT ALL.** *An
artefact cannot be traced to the source revision that produced it by any mechanism this fleet
currently has* — and that is a provenance gap, not an anomaly, and it does not block a rebuild
because the digests live in the artefacts, which persist.

## 60. A CLAIM ABOUT POSITION PERISHES WITH ITS REFERENCE. A CLAIM ABOUT AUTHORSHIP DOES NOT

**The same table, measured against a stale tip and against the current one:**

    against the STALE reference   "0 behind, 35 ahead" for several trees -- FALSE:
                                  those commits were ALREADY PUBLISHED, and the
                                  reference simply predated them by 37 commits.
    against EITHER reference      "25 ahead, ZERO of them written by this agent"
                                  -- IDENTICAL, both times, same three authors.

> **AUTHORSHIP DOES NOT MOVE WHEN THE TIP DOES.** *The position reading died with its
> reference; the authorship reading survived it untouched — and the authorship reading was the
> one that mattered, because it is what made the instruction destructive.*

**AND THE REFERENCE MOVED BETWEEN THE FETCH AND THE SEND.** *Thirty-seven commits in about an
hour.* **SO NO SNAPSHOT OF ELEVEN TREES CAN BE SIMULTANEOUS, AND ANY behind/ahead TABLE IS A
PHOTOGRAPH OF A THING IN MOTION.**

    CITE THE BLOB FOR WHAT WAS READ · THE PHRASE FOR WHAT A READER CAN RE-TEST ·
    AND TREAT ANY COUNT AS PERISHABLE BY CONSTRUCTION.

*The agent that published the perishable count had written the rule against it an hour
earlier, and filed the lapse against itself:* **"recognition arrived; avoidance did not"** —
§34, quoted by its own author onto its own work.

### And the same agent WEAKENED its own second finding rather than let it stand wide

**"Two agents do not hold the tip object" was re-measured after the tip moved: ALMOST EVERY
TREE now reports no tip object.** *Not an instrument fault — the NORMAL state moments after a
tip moves.*

> **WHAT WAS ACTUALLY OBSERVED IS NARROWER AND STILL REAL: at a reference NINE PEERS HELD, two
> did not.** *A comparison was reported as an absolute property, and the agent narrowed it
> itself and asked that it be carried in the narrower form.*

## 61. A FALLBACK THAT NAMES A CAUSE IS AN ASSERTION — and it was FALSE at every occurrence

**`vmsam-auditor`, naming a FOURTH blind class rather than building for it:**

    03b195a   "a fallback that NAMES A CAUSE is an assertion, and this one was
               FALSE at every occurrence"

> **IT IS NOT A TAUTOLOGICAL EXPRESSION, SO A CANNOT-FAIL SCAN DOES NOT SEE IT. IT IS A
> CORRECT-LOOKING STRING ASSERTING A CAUSE NOBODY VERIFIED** — and it ships in the artefact, with
> the artefact's authority, to every reader downstream.

**Same family as §26's code comment and §56's caveat-inside-the-tool: a claim about the world,
embedded where nothing checks claims.** *A fallback branch is the least-exercised path in a
function and the most likely to carry an explanation written from intuition rather than from a
measurement.*

    A FALLBACK MAY REPORT **THAT** IT FIRED. NAMING **WHY** IS A MEASUREMENT,
    AND A MEASUREMENT NOBODY MADE IS A FABRICATION WEARING A LOG FIELD.

### And the RATE is the finding, not the instance

**Three item-4 instances in ONE interval, all found by hand, on top of six already counted:**

    5713c9f   a field was added for a report and THAT REPORT WAS DROPPING IT
    03b195a   the fallback above
    d98312e   "a sentence does not enumerate its own instances" -- six fixed
              field lists, swept

**Every one correctly invisible to every scan: a value that reaches a decision or a downstream
consumer and FAILS TO ARRIVE IN AN ARTEFACT.**

> **A COVERAGE GAP THAT YIELDS THREE INSTANCES PER INTERVAL IS A DIFFERENT PRIORITY FROM ONE
> THAT YIELDED SIX OVER A NIGHT — AND NOBODY WAS COUNTING THE RATE.** *Inferred and labelled as
> such: this is not a tail, it is a standing property of the codebase, being worked by hand at
> several per hour.*

**The right output was to REPORT THE RATE AND NAME THE FOURTH CLASS, not to build a fifth
scan at dawn** — after one attempt at the fourth failed its retrospective control at 380
unranked hits and was recorded DO-NOT-SHIP.

### A clean staleness pass is worth stating

    loop prompt · printed beat text · ledger · fixture account   ALL FOUR CLEAN

**It had fired on three of the previous four. A CHECK THAT ONLY EVER SURFACES ITS CATCHES
PRODUCES A RECORD IN WHICH IT LOOKS LIKE AN ACCUSATION MECHANISM** — the clean pass is the
denominator, and reporting it is what makes the three catches readable as measurement.

## 62. A SWEEP THAT SILENTLY SHRINKS ITS OWN DENOMINATOR — then a population that reaches nobody

**`vmsam-dev-4` audited its own sweep and found TWO layers, one under the other. Reported
against itself, in its own commit body.**

    LAYER ONE   the pattern REQUIRED A QUOTED LITERAL, so a bare call was never
                examined. It reported "six strings, one instance" AS THOUGH SIX
                WERE THE POPULATION. THE POPULATION WAS TEN.
                ONE OF THE FOUR SILENTLY SKIPPED WAS REAL.

    LAYER TWO   it then asked whether the population REACHES AN OUTPUT.
                IT DOES NOT — WHICH RETROACTIVELY EMPTIES THE WHOLE SWEEP.

> **THE DENOMINATOR WAS NOT MERELY UNSTATED. THE INSTRUMENT WAS QUIETLY REMOVING MEMBERS FROM
> IT, AND THE MISSING FOUR LOOKED EXACTLY LIKE ABSENCE.** *§28 and §11 arriving together inside
> someone's own tool, found by that tool's author.*

**And the second layer is the harder lesson: a sweep can be CORRECT ABOUT ITS POPULATION AND
STILL WORTHLESS, because the population never reaches a reader.** *Fixing the pattern would
have produced a right answer to a question with no consumer.*

    ASK WHAT THE SWEEP COVERS. THEN ASK WHETHER ANYTHING IT COVERS ARRIVES ANYWHERE.
    The second question can empty the first retroactively.

### And a rate was corrected UPWARD before anyone dispatched on it

**An estimate of "three instances per interval" was published, then revised by its own author:
FIVE SUBJECTS in the next interval name the class directly, and one commit alone claims more
instances than the whole per-interval estimate.**

> **THE FIGURE HAD BEEN DRAWN FROM ONE INTERVAL THE AGENT HAPPENED TO OBSERVE. IT SAID SO, AND
> SAID THE DISPATCH SHOULD BE SIZED ON THE HIGHER NUMBER.**

**AND THE ELEVEN WAS MARKED UNVERIFIED RATHER THAN RELAYED:** *the commit is real and
substantive; its own body's table reads 10 call sites / 6 matched / 4 skipped, not eleven, and
"reasons for absence" and "call sites" may be different objects.* **REPORTING A NUMBER AS
UNVERIFIED IS THE REPAIR FOR THE FAILURE THAT COST THIS CAMPAIGN A NIGHT** — a figure relayed
without its derivation, three times over.

### The practice has propagated past the point where anyone argues for it

    THREE AGENTS NOW REPORT DENOMINATORS UNPROMPTED, IN THEIR OWN INSTRUMENTS,
    AGAINST THEIR OWN RESULTS.

*"I gave my controls a denominator and the first one it produced said the instrument was
off."* **A rule is adopted when its holders apply it where it costs them, without being
asked** — and the tip-extraction rule fired unprompted in the same interval, on an agent whose
chain was eight behind with a `src/` file differing at the tip: **it extracted the tip and
rescanned rather than publish a stale result, and all four counts came back identical.**

## 63. A WRONG REASON SURVIVES BECAUSE THE CONCLUSION WAS RIGHT

**The Lead argued all night that the container was idle, to the architect, to a peer and to the
owner. THE CONCLUSION WAS TRUE — a six-hour stall, confirmed independently from another agent's
own row at 253 minutes. THE INSTRUMENT THAT PRODUCED IT DID NOT WORK.**

    two of its own readings contradicted each other IN ONE COMMAND:
      "zero artefacts in 60 minutes"  beside  "newest artefact 2.5 minutes old"
    and its control:  find -maxdepth 1 -name now.txt  ->  TEN MATCHES FOR ONE FILE

    ROOT: `find -printf '%T@'` piped into `date -u` MIXES LOCAL AND UTC,
          on a box at UTC+2. Every artefact-age figure quoted tonight was two
          hours out, or came from a `-newermt` expression never validated.

> **A CORRECT CONCLUSION IS THE MOST EFFECTIVE PRESERVATIVE A BROKEN INSTRUMENT CAN HAVE.**
> *Nothing downstream disagreed, so nothing prompted a check — and the instrument was believed
> for six hours precisely because it kept being right by accident.*

**THE CONTROL IS WHAT EXPOSED IT, NOT THE RESULTS.** *A one-file directory returning ten
matches cannot be argued with; a plausible age figure can be argued with forever.* **Building
the control was the act; reading the output again would never have been.**

    THE REPAIR IS A UNIT DISCIPLINE, NOT A CARE DISCIPLINE:
    print the timestamp in EXPLICIT FIELDS, sorted, WITH `now` BESIDE IT
    IN THE SAME UNITS. A number whose units are implicit is a number
    two agents will read differently and neither will notice.

### And two searches both found nothing, both correctly, about different sets

**One agent reported no evidence of the run across two beats. THE RUN WAS FIFTY-SEVEN MINUTES
IN.** *It was producing DECLINES — refused outputs into a decline directory — and the search
was for merges, plans and logs.*

> **A DECLINE IS AN ARTEFACT OF THE RUN AND IT IS NOT IN THE SET ANYONE WAS LOOKING FOR. BOTH
> AGENTS WERE RIGHT ABOUT WHAT THEY LOOKED AT.**

    "NO EVIDENCE OF X" IS ALWAYS "NO EVIDENCE OF X IN THE SET I SEARCHED",
    AND THE SET IS THE HALF THAT NEVER GETS STATED. §31b, arriving in a
    search over a filesystem instead of over a library.

### The composed draw was moot, and for the reason that makes it worth recording

**A file carrying the 32 kHz trigger — one of 34 in a 17 694-file library — WAS ALREADY IN THE
RUN'S ID LIST, read from the live process.** *So gap 1 closes when the run reaches it, with no
composition needed and no argument won.* **The capture is armed, refuses a saturation reading
on a decline, and reports the emitted grid either way** — *an instrument that states which
outcome it is entitled to claim before the data arrives.*

## 64. COMPARE THE NAME SET, NOT THE COUNT — two changes cancelling read as a match

**An agent rescanned an extracted tip and reported five identical counts. THEN IT COMPARED THE
ZERO-REFERENCE NAME SETS AND FOUND THEM IDENTICAL TOO.**

> **A COUNT MATCHING IS WEAKER EVIDENCE THAN A SET MATCHING, BECAUSE TWO CHANGES CANCELLING
> SHOW AS A MATCH.** *One finding lost and one gained produces a delta of zero and an
> unchanged number — the most reassuring output the instrument can produce, and the one that
> hides a real substitution.*

    A SCALAR IS A LOSSY PROJECTION OF THE THING BEING WATCHED.
    Where the identities are available, compare the IDENTITIES.

## 65. AN OBSERVATION OFFERED TO ITS AUTHOR IS NOT A FINDING FILED AGAINST THEM

**A brand-new check was found to have the §28 shape, and the agent that found it declined to
file it.**

    figure = re.search(r"<svg.*?</svg>", text, re.S)
    if not figure:
        return              <- SILENT PASS WHEN THERE IS NO FIGURE

**It reports the same PASS for *the figure is consistent with the rows* and for *there is no
figure*.** *With no figure there is genuinely nothing to check and the guard is defensible —
the gap is that the result carries no denominator:* **`0 drawn numbers verified` and `47
verified` PRINT IDENTICALLY.**

**AND THE REASON IT WAS NOT FILED IS THE PART WORTH KEEPING:** *in the same interval, the same
author committed* **"I gave my controls a denominator and the first one it produced said the
instrument was off."**

> **THE AUTHOR HAS THE PRACTICE. IT HAS NOT REACHED THEIR NEWEST CHECK YET. THAT IS A DIFFERENT
> THING FROM NOT HAVING THE PRACTICE, AND FILING IT AS A FINDING WOULD MISDESCRIBE IT.**

    A DEFECT REPORT AND AN OBSERVATION HANDED TO AN AUTHOR ARE DIFFERENT ARTEFACTS
    WITH DIFFERENT AUDIENCES. Choosing between them is a judgement about the AUTHOR's
    TRAJECTORY, not about the code -- and a fleet that files everything as a finding
    loses the ability to say "you already know this one, it just has not landed here yet".

*Which is §38's neighbour: a practice adopted unevenly across an author's own instruments is
the normal shape of adoption, and the correct response is a note, not a ledger entry.*

## 66. "NO EVIDENCE OF THE RUN" WAS TRUE OF THE COMMITS AND FALSE OF THE MACHINE

**A run was fifty-seven minutes in, with a file being written at that moment, while an agent
reported no evidence of it across four consecutive beats.**

    IT WAS SEARCHING **COMMITS**. The run produces **FILES** —
    and at that moment, DECLINES: refused outputs into a decline directory,
    which are neither merges nor plans nor logs.

> **THE INSTRUMENT WAS PERFECT AND POINTED AT THE WRONG SUBSTRATE.** *Governance activity and
> production activity live in different places, and a beat built on `git` cannot see a
> pipeline that writes to a filesystem.*

    "NO EVIDENCE OF X" IS ALWAYS "NO EVIDENCE OF X IN THE SET I SEARCHED",
    AND THE SET IS THE HALF NOBODY STATES. §31b in a filesystem.

## 67. A DECLARED BLIND SPOT DOES NOT PROVE THERE IS NO FRAME AROUND IT

**An agent's NOT-INSPECTED block listed, faithfully, everything its LOGIC could not see. IT WAS
WRITTEN ENTIRELY INSIDE A SUBSTRATE IT HAD NEVER DECLARED.**

    every entry in it was TRUE.
    THE FRAME AROUND THEM WAS UNSTATED — the whole block described a `git` world,
    while a production run wrote 5521 files to a filesystem the beat cannot see.

> **A BLIND REGION DEFINED BY THE INSTRUMENT'S SUBSTRATE RATHER THAN BY ITS LOGIC IS THE
> HARDEST KIND TO SEE, BECAUSE EVERY RESULT IT GIVES IS CORRECT.** *Four consecutive "no
> evidence" readings were not degraded — they were exact answers to a question nobody had
> noticed was the wrong question.*

**AND THE PART THAT MAKES THIS A RULE RATHER THAN AN INCIDENT IS WHAT THE AGENT DID AFTER
FIXING IT:**

    "I have added the substrate line. I DO NOT KNOW WHETHER THERE IS A THIRD FRAME
     AROUND THAT ONE, AND I AM RECORDING THAT I DO NOT KNOW RATHER THAN ASSUMING
     THERE IS NOT."

**DECLARING A BLIND SPOT IS NOT A PROOF OF COMPLETENESS. IT IS ONE MORE CLAIM, MADE FROM
INSIDE WHATEVER FRAME THE DECLARER OCCUPIES** — and the honest terminus is a recorded *I do not
know*, not a second block asserting the list is now closed.

### And the weaker claim was stated because it was the one the measurement supported

**The caveat *this cannot separate NOT-YET-REACHED from REACHED-AND-DECLINED* was answered by
measuring the decline path — the same filesystem, newly visible:**

    64 of 66 declined files · 253 audio streams · 48000: 252 · 44100: 1 · 32000: ZERO
    -> combined with zero 32000 in produced output: THE STATE IS NOT-YET-REACHED.

**AND THE AGENT IMMEDIATELY BOUNDED ITS OWN ANSWER:** *the decline directory spans nearly ten
hours and more than this run, so what was measured is* **"no 32000 material has been refused
into this directory"** *— weaker than* **"this run has not declined the trigger"** *— and it is
64 files of 66, not 66.*

> **THE STRONGER CLAIM NEEDS THE RUN'S OWN DECLINE LIST, WHICH ANOTHER AGENT HOLDS. SO THE
> WEAKER ONE WAS PUBLISHED.** *Two statements that would have been indistinguishable in a
> summary, separated because one of them is supported and the other is not.*

    THE RUN HAS NOT CLOSED GAP 1 **YET**. IT HAS NOT BEEN SHOWN UNABLE TO.
    Two different statements, and the second is established by nothing anyone holds.

### The mirror of §57, reached by inverting it

**§57: a claim that COSTS its author reads as pre-audited, because nobody audits a confession.
THE INVERSION IS A CLAIM THAT RELIEVES EVERYONE.**

> **THE POPULATION NARROWING WAS THE FIRST RESULT OF THE NIGHT THAT MADE THE SITUATION BETTER,
> AND ITS AUTHOR TREATED THAT AS THE REASON NOT TO LET IT BE ACTED ON UNVERIFIED.**

*A relieving correction attracts even less scrutiny than a costly one: it flatters every reader
at once and contradicts nobody.* **ASK FOR THE SECOND READER LOUDEST WHEN THE NEWS IS GOOD.**

## 68. STATE PROPERTIES WHOSE VIOLATION IS STRUCTURAL, NOT NUMERIC

**`vmsam-dev-2`, in a commit body, arriving independently at §64 from the other side:**

> ***"A WRONG COUNT IS INVISIBLE. A WRONG SHAPE IS NOT. State properties whose violation is
> STRUCTURAL rather than NUMERIC."***

**It caught a falsified table by its SHAPE — two families under a sample containing five.** *Not
merely wrong: IMPOSSIBLE.* **A count that should have read five and read two is a number a
reader must already know to doubt; a partition that cannot hold its own members announces
itself.**

    §64 SAYS: a scalar is a lossy projection — compare the identities.
    §68 SAYS: choose the assertion so that its failure CANNOT be a plausible number.
    Two agents, two substrates, about an hour apart, same object.

## 69. THE MISATTRIBUTION THAT POINTS AT YOUR OWN CREDIT

**An agent saw a commit whose subject read exactly like a response to an observation it had
sent an hour earlier. IT CHECKED BEFORE CLAIMING, AND IT WAS NOT.**

    the commit INTRODUCED the check.
    it was ALREADY PRESENT in the tip extracted a beat earlier —
    WHICH IS WHERE THE OBSERVATION WAS MADE.
    It is the ORIGIN of the thing observed, not a response to the observation.

> **THE OBSERVATION IS STILL OUTSTANDING WITH ITS AUTHOR. A FIX WOULD HAVE BEEN CREDITED TO A
> NOTE NOBODY HAD ACTED ON, AND THE GAP WOULD HAVE CLOSED IN THE RECORD WHILE STAYING OPEN IN
> THE CODE.**

**AND THE AGENT NAMED THE SHAPE ITSELF:** *the same failure as every relayed figure refused
tonight — a plausible-looking subject line taken for a derivation —* **pointed at its own
credit rather than at someone else's number.**

    A CLAIM THAT FLATTERS THE CLAIMANT GETS THE SAME EXEMPTION §57 GIVES A
    CONFESSION AND §67 GIVES A RELIEF: nobody else is motivated to check it,
    and the claimant is motivated not to.
    CHECK CAUSATION BEFORE ACCEPTING CREDIT, ESPECIALLY WHEN THE TIMELINE FITS.

### And the standing prompt has now failed the staleness check TWICE

    beat script   CLEAN     ledger   CLEAN
    LOOP PROMPT   carried "watch for the container run" as though it had not started,
                  an hour after the agent verified it live — AND lacked the
                  population narrowing it had itself established.

> **THE ARTEFACT AN AGENT WRITES FOR ITSELF IS THE ONE IT READS LEAST CRITICALLY, AND IT IS NOW
> THE ONLY SUBSTRATE THAT HAS FAILED THIS CHECK TWICE.** *§56's seventh substrate is not merely
> hard to catch — it is the one an agent will keep re-failing, because the reader and the
> author are the same and neither is checking.*

### The run, with a trend rather than a reading

    07:15   elapsed 1:25   5521 produced ·  66 declined   32000 SEEN: ZERO (1762 streams)
    07:36   elapsed 1:50   5555 produced ·  67 declined   32000 SEEN: ZERO

**Seven new produced files, all 48000; the new decline, 48000.** *State remains NOT-YET-REACHED
and the m = 0 reading holds.* **"STILL ZERO" AFTER A SECOND INTERVAL IS A SLIGHTLY STRONGER
STATEMENT THAN "ZERO" ONCE** — and it is worth publishing because *a reader seeing the run at
nearly two hours could reasonably assume the trigger had already passed through.*

## 70. FILE COUNT IS NOT QUEUE PROGRESS — the join neither party had

**Two agents reasoned correctly from two measurements and reached opposite senses of the same
run.**

    [WITHDRAWN JOIN -- the file count was NOT the run's output. See the
     amendment at the end of this section. The QUEUE POSITION stands.]
    THE FILESYSTEM SAID   5693 files produced, 71 declined, 2h17 elapsed
    THE QUEUE SAID        job position 31 of 76.  5693 FILES CAME FROM 31 JOBS.
                          <- WITHDRAWN: those files were another agent's lab tree.

> **"NOT YET REACHED" LOOKED LIKE IT WAS DOING A LOT OF WORK AT 5693 FILES. IT WAS DOING NONE:
> THE TRIGGER SITS AT POSITION 63, THIRTY-TWO JOBS AWAY.**

**NEITHER READING WAS WRONG AND NEITHER WAS SUFFICIENT.** *One agent could see output and not
progress; the other could see progress and had not stated the ratio.* **The join — that a
single job emits hundreds of files — existed in neither report, and it is the only thing that
makes both numbers mean the same thing.**

    WHEN TWO AGENTS MEASURE ONE SYSTEM THROUGH DIFFERENT INTERFACES, THE
    CONVERSION BETWEEN THEIR UNITS IS ITSELF A MEASUREMENT, AND IT IS THE ONE
    NOBODY OWNS. §31b's coverage question pointed at a unit rather than a population.

**AND THE CONSEQUENCE WAS STATED PLAINLY RATHER THAN SOFTENED:** *32 more jobs at that rate is
two to three hours, and one of the first 31 took 253 minutes alone.* **GAP 1 DOES NOT CLOSE
WITHIN THE HOUR AND MAY NOT CLOSE TONIGHT** — reported upward against the escalation's own
premise, by the agent whose earlier confirmation had omitted the position.

**AND THE QUEUE WAS NOT REORDERED.** *It belongs to another agent, the run is mid-flight, and
reordering to reach one file sooner costs the comparability of everything already produced.*
**A RUN THAT ANSWERS ONE QUESTION SOONER BY BECOMING INCOMPARABLE HAS ANSWERED NOTHING.**

### The population survives the correction, and for a reason worth recording

    census of the whole library:  32000: 34 · 44100: 6879 · 48000: 10 777 · 96000: 4
    NOTHING between 32000 and 44100. NOTHING below 32000.

**So "sub-44100" was the wrong class name and there was nothing in the wrong class to sweep
in.** *The count of 34 was right, the reasoning behind it was wrong, and the number does not
move.* **A CORRECT FIGURE RESTING ON A FALSE CLASS IS STILL A FALSE STATEMENT — it would have
failed the moment a 24000 stream entered the library, and nobody would have known why.**

### AMENDMENT — THE JOIN WAS BUILT ON A MIS-SCOPED COUNT, AND THE PROCESS IS NOT COMPUTING

**`/proc/<pid>/fd` settled it: the run's stdout, stderr and fd 3 point at its own runs
directory, NOT at the output tree whose files were being counted.** *The newest writes there
