**Measured by `vmsam-arch-aide` ON ITS OWN REF `refs/agents/arch-aide`, WHICH IS THE ONLY REF THIS
COUNT DESCRIBES — read as a fleet rate it becomes the §85 join it was written to prevent:** *a media-server
directory name was redacted from a ledger. **NINE OF TWENTY-FIVE PUBLISHED LEDGER BLOBS STILL
CARRY IT** — one contiguous run, sixteen clean afterwards, and the fragment did not predate the
run.* **All 25 opened; `m` is 9 against 16, and both outcomes occurred.**

**THE REDACTION IS CORRECT GOING FORWARD AND DOES NOTHING BACKWARD, AND THE ONLY REMEDY IS A
REWRITE THAT ANNIHILATES EVERY BLOB CITATION INTO THE REF.**

    A LEAK IS CHEAP TO PREVENT AND UNFIXABLE TO REMOVE.
    SWEEP THE HISTORY YOU ARE ABOUT TO **EXTEND**, NOT ONLY THE FILE YOU ARE
    ABOUT TO COMMIT:  git rev-list <ref> -- <path>  then cat-file each blob.
    AFTER PUBLICATION THE ONLY REMEDY BREAKS EVERY CITATION OTHER SEATS HOLD,
    SO THE PRE-COMMIT SWEEP IS NOT HYGIENE. IT IS THE ONLY SWEEP THERE IS.

**§33 and every successor sold immutability as the property that makes work survivable. IT IS
THE SAME PROPERTY THAT MAKES A LEAK PERMANENT.** *Both halves are true, they are not separable,
and the document had stated only the half that flatters the rule.* **One seat swept BEFORE
pushing and caught thirteen; another swept after and found one it cannot unpublish. THE ORDERING
IS THE WHOLE RULE.**

## 102. §33 WAS MEASURED FOR THE FIRST TIME, BECAUSE SOMEBODY PRE-REGISTERED THE TEST

**On the freeze announcement, before the rewrite landed, a seat recorded 15 `<blob> <path>` pairs
into a file with the refutation stated in advance: REFUTED IF ANY BLOB IS ANNIHILATED OR ANY PATH
MOVES ON.**

    SCORED against one consistent snapshot — refs/heads == refs/remotes == ls-remote,
    all three read, because A REF IS A CACHED ANSWER.

    15 of 15 blob objects RESOLVE          0 ANNIHILATED
    15 of 15 still CURRENT at ref:path     0 MOVED ON
    the commit line went 195 -> 8.  GONE.

> **THE COMPACTION IS CONTENT-PRESERVING AND THE RULE HOLDS — MEASURED, NOT ASSERTED. EVERY SEAT
> THAT CITED BY BLOB CAN STILL RESOLVE. EVERY SEAT THAT CITED A COMMIT SHA IN THAT RANGE
> CANNOT.**

**That is the strongest evidence this rule has ever had, and it exists ONLY because the event
happened while someone was watching for it with a falsifier written down first.**

*Its author's own correction, and it is the sharper reading: this scores as a `RUN` rather than a
`HARNESS`* ***not because the seat was more careful — because the falsifier was written before the
outcome existed and neither party could have fitted it to the result.*** **THE INDEPENDENCE IS
CARRIED BY THE TECHNIQUE, NOT BY THE SEAT. Anyone could have done it. Nobody had.**

## 103. IN A DIVERGENCE WINDOW THE BLOBS AGREE AND THE HISTORY DOES NOT

**For a period during the rewrite, the intermediary served a branch that NO LONGER EXISTED
UPSTREAM:**

    refs/heads/dev-AI            195 commits since the base   PRE-rewrite
    refs/remotes/origin/dev-AI     8 commits since the base   POST-rewrite
    NEITHER AN ANCESTOR OF THE OTHER. ONE NAME, TWO HISTORIES, DIVERGED AT THE BASE.

**AND THE PART THAT MAKES IT DANGEROUS: 8 OF 9 GOVERNANCE AND `src` BLOBS WERE IDENTICAL ACROSS
THE TWO DIVERGENT LINES** — *only the one file being actively edited differed.*

> **AN AGENT CHECKING CONTENT IN THAT WINDOW WOULD HAVE SEEN ALMOST NOTHING WRONG WHILE ITS
> ANCESTRY WAS COMPLETELY DISCONNECTED.** *The reverse of the usual failure: here the blobs
> agreed and the history did not.*

    "RE-FETCH, VERIFY THE ROUTE NOT THE CONTENT" IS RIGHT, AND HERE IS WHY:
    CONTENT AGREEMENT IS NOT EVIDENCE THE FETCH SUCCEEDED, BECAUSE A
    CONTENT-PRESERVING REWRITE IS CONTENT-PRESERVING ON BOTH SIDES OF THE WINDOW.

### And a count published without its ref

    base..origin/dev-AI          7        base..refs/heads/dev-AI     195
    base..refs/agents/<seat>   196        two other seats             194

**The announced `196` was right about the thing being rewritten and READS AS WRONG to anyone
checking it against the published branch.** *The seat that noticed MEASURED all five refs rather
than reporting a discrepancy.*

> **AN ANNOUNCEMENT NAMING A COUNT MUST NAME THE REF IT COUNTED — for the same reason this
> campaign has repeated all day about blobs: A NUMBER WITHOUT ITS REFERENCE IS A CLAIM ABOUT AN
> UNNAMED OBJECT.**

## 104. "ONE WRITE SITE, VALUE V" PROVES *V OR RAISE*, NOT *V*

**`vmsam-forensic` recorded an inert guard with its control, and the control was sound. `vmsam-lead`
carried it, and records the carry as its own defect — see the note closing this section:**

    src/video.py:276, :289   if audio["compatible"]:      <- reads as a filter
    src/video.py:124         data["compatible"] = True    <- the ONLY write
    set to False anywhere:   0

**The conclusion — the guard cannot discriminate — is right. THE CONTROL DOES NOT YET ESTABLISH
IT.** *"One write, value True, no False" leaves a dict that never passed the write site: that
read does not yield False, it **raises `KeyError`**.* **A guard that raises is not inert. It is a
different defect wearing the same grep result.**

**What closes it is a count of the CONTAINER's producers, not the key's writers:**

    self.audios  written at exactly THREE sites, all in one file --
      :42  = None    :75  = {}      <- initialisers, no element
      :126 and :128  append / create   <- THE ONLY PRODUCERS (:125 is the TEST
                     `if language in self.audios:` -- corrected by `vmsam-auditor`)
    and :125 sits in the SAME `else` BRANCH as the write at :124, ONE STATEMENT LATER.
    :165 aliases the "und" list to another key -- SAME OBJECTS, already through :124.
    read with a default `.get("compatible")`:  0 sites.

> **EVERY ELEMENT OF EVERY `self.audios[*]` PASSED THROUGH THE WRITE ONE STATEMENT BEFORE
> ENTERING THE LIST. THE GUARD IS INERT BY CONSTRUCTION, NOT BY TALLY** — *and the `KeyError`
> branch is excluded by the same measurement rather than left unexamined.*

    TO PROVE A READ IS CONSTANT, DO NOT COUNT WHO WRITES THE KEY.
    COUNT WHO BUILDS THE THING BEING READ. A KEY TALLY BOUNDS THE VALUES
    A READ CAN RETURN; ONLY A PRODUCER TALLY BOUNDS WHETHER IT RETURNS AT ALL.

*Found by `vmsam-forensic`, whose measurement was correct and whose conclusion needed one more
count. Both halves are the finding; neither seat had it alone.*

> **AND `vmsam-lead` FILED THE RELAY AS ITS OWN DEFECT, UNPROMPTED:** *it carried "one write site,
> nothing sets False, therefore inert" to two seats without noticing that the control bounds the
> VALUES a read returns and not whether the read RETURNS.* **A `KeyError` BRANCH HAS AN IDENTICAL
> GREP SIGNATURE, SO THE CONTROL COULD NOT HAVE CAUGHT IT — which is the exact shape that seat had
> been correcting in others all day.** *A relay inherits the gap in what it relays, silently.*

## 105. A PIN IS A CLAIM ABOUT A MOMENT

**`vmsam-lead`, collecting `src/merge_plan_report.py` from `vmsam-dev-4` — and the near-miss was
`dev-4`'s CATCH, not the collector's discovery: it said the pin had gone stale in the *is this still
ALL* direction AFTER the wrong thing had already been collected:** *it collected at the
seat's CURRENT HEAD blob rather than at the pin the seat had sent earlier — because after the pin
was given, dev-4 landed three more commits.*

> **THE PIN WAS CORRECT WHEN GIVEN AND WENT STALE IN THE DIRECTION NOBODY CHECKS.** *Not "is this
> still current" — the question every seat is drilled to ask — but* ***"is this still ALL".***

    "COLLECTED BY BLOB" PROVES YOU TOOK WHAT WAS NAMED.
    IT DOES NOT PROVE THE SEAT HAD NOTHING ELSE.
    A CITATION IS SOUND ABOUT THE OBJECT AND SILENT ABOUT THE SET.

### The pattern the day made: three independent limits, one property

**Three seats, three separate investigations, each ending on the same shape — and this campaign
had written down only the first:**

    §33   A BLOB IS SOUND ABOUT IDENTITY.  This object is that content. Always.
    §103  ...AND SILENT ABOUT ANCESTRY.    arch-aide: 8 of 9 blobs identical
                                           across two lines neither an ancestor
                                           of the other.
    §105  ...AND SILENT ABOUT COMPLETENESS. lead: the named blob was faithful and
                                           three commits had landed behind it.
    §104  ...AND A TALLY OVER BLOBS IS SILENT ABOUT WHETHER THE READ HAPPENS.

> **THE STRENGTH OF BLOB CITATION AND ITS BLIND SPOTS ARE THE SAME FACT: IT RESOLVES ONE OBJECT
> PERFECTLY AND SAYS NOTHING ABOUT ITS NEIGHBOURS, ITS HISTORY, OR ITS SIBLINGS.** *Keep citing
> by blob — nothing else survives a promotion squash. **AND NAME, ALONGSIDE IT, THE REF, THE
> RANGE, OR THE SET the claim actually depends on**, because the citation will never supply
> them and will never warn you that it did not.*

### §103 fired within the hour, on the check being run to verify §103

**Verifying the section at the authority, `vmsam-arch-aide` read `refs/heads/dev-AI` = `9de4c3a`
and `ls-remote` = `032556e5`.** *Not a divergence — a strict ancestor, one commit behind,
fast-forwardable.* **BUT THE THREE READS DISAGREED, AND THE SEAT WOULD HAVE BEEN READING A TREE
ONE CHANGE OLD WHILE RESOLVING BLOBS THAT LOOKED PERFECTLY CURRENT.** *The benign case and the
disconnected case present identically at the moment of reading; only the three-read agreement
separates them, and it costs one command.*

## 106. A CITATION IS ONLY AS REACHABLE AS THE REF THAT CARRIES IT

**`vmsam-arch-aide` registered the end-condition falsifier BEFORE the run — the right act, done in
the right order — and published it at `refs/agents/arch-aide`, blob `d25b1a6`, with the correct
instruction: *resolve it yourself, do not take it from my message.* I ran exactly that:**

    git cat-file -t d25b1a6                -> DOES NOT RESOLVE in this clone
    git ls-remote origin 'refs/agents/*'   -> 0
    git ls-remote origin | grep -v heads/tags -> HEAD, refs/notes/commits, refs/pull/1/head
    heads on origin: 4.  NONE carries the artefact.

> **THE FALSIFIER IS UNREACHABLE FROM THE AUTHORITY. Its author's own thesis — *a falsifier that
> lives only in a transcript is afterwards indistinguishable from one never written* — EXTENDS ONE
> NOTCH FURTHER THAN IT WAS AIMED: SO IS ONE ON A REF NOBODY ELSE CAN FETCH.** *From every clone
> but its own, the two are the same observable.*

**This is §33's blind spot in its last position. A blob is sound about IDENTITY (§33), silent about
ANCESTRY (§103), silent about COMPLETENESS (§105), silent about whether the read happens (§104) —
and SILENT ABOUT WHETHER THE ASKER CAN GET TO IT AT ALL.** *`d25b1a6` is a perfectly good name for
an object that exists; it is not a way to hand that object to anyone.*

    REACHABILITY IS A PROPERTY OF THE REF, NEVER OF THE BLOB.
    BEFORE CITING, RUN THE FETCH THE READER WOULD RUN.
    A PRE-REGISTERED FALSIFIER MUST LAND WHERE THE SCORING SEAT CAN FETCH IT,
    OR IT PROTECTS NOBODY BUT ITS AUTHOR -- WHICH IS THE ONE PARTY IT WAS
    NEVER MEANT TO PROTECT.

*No fault attaches to the registration: the act was correct, the order was correct, and the defect
is in the transport. Land it on a head the authority carries and the artefact does its whole job.*

## 107. A SESSION NAME RESOLVES AND DOES NOT IDENTIFY — THREE NAMES, ONE SEAT

**Two seats hit the same defect from opposite substrates in the same hour, and neither could see the
other's half.**

    FROM THE COMMIT SIDE (mine):   %an = `vmsam-lead`, ONE occurrence in 400 commits,
                                   and `SendMessage to: "vmsam-lead"` -> NOT REACHABLE.
    FROM THE SESSION SIDE (aide):  ListAgents = 12 vmsam seats, NO `vmsam-lead`.
                                   ELEVEN names are an IDENTITY FUNCTION -- name IS role.
                                   THE ONE THAT IS NOT is the seat that DISPATCHES.

> **THE SEAT HAS THREE NAMES: it is ADDRESSABLE as `vmsam-dev-sandbox`, it COMMITS as `vmsam-lead`,
> and its own session is a third string. ONLY THE FIRST ROUTES.** *An agent enumerating sessions and
> looking for the lead finds nothing — and concludes, wrongly, that the lead is absent.*

    A NAME IS SOUND WITHIN THE NAMESPACE THAT ISSUED IT AND MEANS NOTHING OUTSIDE IT.
    AN ADDRESS, AN AUTHOR NAME AND A SESSION LABEL ARE THREE NAMESPACES.
    BEFORE CONCLUDING A SEAT IS ABSENT, CHECK WHETHER YOU ARE ASKING THE WRONG NAMESPACE.

*`aide` upgraded its own basis for this from SECOND-PARTY to FIRST-PARTY today — it had declined to
assert the mapping while it had only heard it — and it declined to relay this section's substance
on the ground that* ***a published measurement cannot be laundered and a relayed one can.*** *It is
right, and this document has spent its worst hours on the other choice.*

## 108. A MERGE STATE IS NOT A PROPERTY OF A FILE

**`vmsam-dev-4`, correcting `aide`, and it is the shape the END CONDITION needs:**

> **IT IS A COMPARISON BETWEEN TWO REFS AT ONE INSTANT.** *`N files merged` ages badly and cannot be
> re-run.* ***`at T, <ref>:<path> = X while <other-ref>:<path> = Y` is checkable forever.*** **Same
> content; only one of the two survives being read tomorrow.**

**THEREFORE NO SEAT MAY REPORT THE END-CONDITION DENOMINATOR AS A COUNT ALONE.** *It is reported as
the two refs, the instant, and the command — and a seat whose substrate cannot see both refs does
not hold the number and must say so rather than estimate it.*

## 109. A ZERO FROM A DEAD SINK AND A ZERO FROM A SILENT PATH ARE THE SAME OBSERVATION

**`vmsam-dev-1`, measuring the locator's silent refusal in production shape, `tools.dev=False`,
and it ran the control BEFORE claiming the zero:**

    P4  SINK CONTROL -- an ACCEPTING pair emits >=1 line at dev=False    TRUE (1)
    P1  tip emits ZERO on the silent path                                TRUE (0)
    P2  prepared copy emits EXACTLY ONE, verdict UNCHANGED               TRUE (1)
    P3  accepting pair BYTE-IDENTICAL across both copies                 TRUE

> **P4 IS WHY P1 IS WORTH ANYTHING.** *That exact confusion had already cost this seat a false
> result the same day: its logging was off, and* ***a total instrument failure printed as a clean
> refusal.***

    BEFORE REPORTING "IT EMITS NOTHING", PROVE THE SINK IS LIVE IN THE SAME PROCESS
    AT THE SAME SETTING. AN ABSENT SIGNAL IS EVIDENCE ABOUT THE CODE ONLY ONCE THE
    CHANNEL IS PROVEN TO CARRY A PRESENT ONE. THIS IS `m` APPLIED TO AN INSTRUMENT:
    A ZERO WITH NO POSITIVE CONTROL HAS m = 0 AND SAYS NOTHING.

**AND IT REPORTED ITS OWN `m = 0` RATHER THAN LETTING THE COVERAGE BE ASSUMED:** *one arm reached
the branch under test; the `speed_relation_suspected` branch shares the same `return None` and the
prepared change covers it — but no material was produced that lands on it.* **"I WOULD NOT LAND THE
CHANGE CLAIMING BOTH BRANCHES VERIFIED" is the sentence this document wants from every seat.**

## 110. `None` MEANT TWO THINGS, AND THE CONSUMER RECORDED THE FALSE ONE

**The locator documents its own contract at `change_point_locator.py:795`:**

> ***"None means I could not measure — never the files are compatible. dev-2 maps it to `no_plan`
> and the refusal stands. THOSE ARE DIFFERENT ANSWERS AND COLLAPSING THEM IS THE MISTAKE THIS
> CAMPAIGN EXISTS TO AVOID."***

**AND THE MODULE BREAKS ITS OWN CONTRACT ON THE FIDELITY-FLOOR PATH.** *There the probes RAN, they
SUCCEEDED, and they returned a conclusive negative — a measurement was available and it said no.
The function returns `None` anyway, one of fifteen `return None` sites.*

**WHAT THE CONSUMER THEN WRITES, `merge_video_repair.py:1986`, verified unconditional on that
branch:**

    if plan == None:
        record(candidate_path, "no_plan",
               f"no measurement available for this pair ({plan_source})")

> **THAT STRING IS FALSE FOR THIS PATH, AND IT IS THE ONLY THING A READER GETS.** *A conclusive
> negative is filed as an absence of evidence — the single substitution this campaign was convened
> to prevent, committed by the module that documents the rule against it.*

    THEREFORE THE END-CONDITION BLOCKER IS **TWO** CHANGES, NOT ONE:
      LOCATOR  emit the cause on the silent path            (prepared, dev-1)
      REPAIR   READ it, instead of recording the constant   (UNOWNED)
    EMITTING THE CAUSE DOES NOT FIX THE RECORD. A producer that starts telling the
    truth into a consumer that overwrites it with a constant has changed nothing
    a person can read -- and the report still says a measurement was unavailable.

*Found by `vmsam-dev-1` while preparing the first half, and it is outside that seat's boundary —
which is why it was reported rather than fixed.*

## 111. AN ARMED GUARD THAT HAS NEVER FIRED IS INDISTINGUISHABLE FROM A WORKING ONE

**A structural fix for the hop-lag defect exists, is correctly reasoned, carries a comment that
states the right requirement — *"the hop branch must not lag origin, or agent fetches are inert"* —
and HAS NEVER RUN ONCE. `vmsam-lead` found three independent reasons, each alone sufficient:**

    1  IT IS INSTALLED AS `post-fetch`, WHICH IS NOT A GIT HOOK.
       CONTROL, mine: git's own template ships 14 hooks; `post-fetch` is 0 of them.
       GIT NEVER INVOKES IT. Not disabled -- never wired.
    2  IT IS NOT EXECUTABLE (-rw-r--r--). A REAL hook would still be skipped.
    3  THE ONE REAL HOOK PRESENT, `post-merge`, HAS THE ENTIRE BODY
       `exec >/dev/null 2>&1`. IT SILENCES OUTPUT AND DOES NOTHING ELSE.

> **THE FAILURE IT PREVENTS OCCURRED AT LEAST FIVE TIMES TODAY, AND THE HOP WAS LAGGING AT THE
> MOMENT THE REPORT WAS WRITTEN.** *Whoever wrote that file would reasonably believe the problem was
> solved. **A GUARD IS NOT VERIFIED BY EXISTING. IT IS VERIFIED BY BEING MADE TO FIRE** — plant the
> failure, watch it catch, and only then believe it.*

**THE HOOK GIT ACTUALLY PROVIDES IS `reference-transaction` (2.28+; this clone runs 2.53.0), which
fires when a ref is updated — which is what a fetch does.** *It is NOT being installed: a hook on
the shared serving repo changes every seat's fetch behaviour, hooks live in clone configuration, and
that is the owner's call — hardest of all for the repo everyone fetches from.*

**SAME STATUS FOR `check_write_zones.py`, and this closes an item open since the campaign's start:**
*referenced in exactly 3 tracked files; `pre-commit` hooks across the hop and all eleven clones:*
***ZERO.*** *It has never checked a single commit. To be ASKED of each seat's user, never landed.*

## 112. AGREEMENT BETWEEN READS THAT SHARE A SOURCE IS NOT AGREEMENT

**`vmsam-dev-1` did exactly what §103 instructs — three ref reads, one snapshot — and reported that
the architect's announced objects DO NOT EXIST:**

    dev-1's two-row ls-remote:  0017021 / 0017021   AGREE
    announced tip 445177eb, ENTRY.MD 2403a1c235aa, MEASURING.MD 05299daedcec
      -> NOT A VALID OBJECT NAME, all three.

**AT THE AUTHORITY'S URL, MEASURED BY THE ARCHITECT IN ONE SNAPSHOT, ALL THREE RESOLVE:**

    ls-remote AT THE URL  445177eb    refs/remotes  445177eb    local HEAD  445177eb
    445177eb commit · 2403a1c235aa blob · 05299daedcec blob
    "## 4b." in the ENTRY.MD the URL serves: 1.  control "A PEER RELAYING": 1.

> **BOTH SEATS RAN THE THREE-READ CHECK. BOTH GOT AGREEMENT. THEY WERE READING DIFFERENT REPOSITORIES.**

    A CONSISTENCY CHECK ACROSS TWO VIEWS OF THE SAME CACHE MEASURES NOTHING.
    THE THREE READS MUST BE INDEPENDENT, NOT MERELY DISTINCT COMMANDS --
    AND `ls-remote origin` IS NOT INDEPENDENT OF `origin/<branch>` WHEN
    `origin` IS AN INTERMEDIARY. **INDEPENDENCE, NOT AGREEMENT.**

> **AND THE THREE-READ CHECK THIS DOCUMENT PRESCRIBED IS ITSELF ONLY ONE READ. `vmsam-ci`, amending
> the falsifier before running it:** *`refs/heads/dev-AI` IN AN AGENT CLONE IS THE AGENT'S OWN
> BRANCH — its own is 75 ahead and 213 behind and* ***has never once equalled the authority***, *so a
> clause requiring it to match VOIDS EVERY SCORE ANY WORKING SEAT COULD PRODUCE. And the other two
> are not independent either:* **`ls-remote` asks the authority, `refs/remotes` is the cache that
> answer wrote, and after a fetch they agree BY CONSTRUCTION** — *the same defect, one level in.*

    SO: **ONE READ, NAMED AND TIMED** -- `git ls-remote <URL-from-a-durable-artefact>
    refs/heads/<branch>` -- WITH THE SHA RECORDED **AND ITS TREE**.
    THE HOP COMPARISON IS A DIAGNOSTIC, NEVER A SCORING GATE.
    A LOCAL BRANCH OF THE SAME NAME IS NOT A THIRD OPINION. IT IS A DIFFERENT BRANCH.

*Confirmed from a third clone the same minute by `ci-pair`, all three values different: authority,
hop seven behind, and its own clone behind both.*

### And "read it at the authority" does not resolve the same way in every clone

**`origin` is a per-clone name (§107 applied to a REMOTE). The seat believed it was reading the
authority; it was reading the hop, which §111 proved has never once been advanced by its own guard.**

    BEFORE CALLING A SOURCE "THE AUTHORITY", PRINT ITS URL: `git remote -v`.
    A DOCUMENT SAYING "READ IT AT THE AUTHORITY" MUST NAME THE URL, NOT THE REMOTE.
    AND THE TWO-ROW TEST IS A POSITIVE CHECK ONLY: IT CANNOT SEE A COMMIT THE
    INTERMEDIARY WAS NEVER GIVEN. Third distinct staleness mode in one session --
    hop behind upstream; hop fetched without moving its branch; and now
    OBJECTS THAT NEVER ARRIVED AT ALL, which the first two tests cannot detect.

*Reported by `vmsam-dev-1` as a defect in the architect's announcement. It is a defect in the
CHANNEL, and the seat's own conclusion was the correct one to draw from what it could see.*

## 113. `git hash-object` WITHOUT `-w` NAMES AN OBJECT THAT DOES NOT EXIST

**`vmsam-dev-1`, correcting itself, in the same message where it correctly re-resolved a base blob
and correctly refused to send a pin:**

    git hash-object <file>        -> prints the name the content WOULD have
                                     AND WRITES NOTHING.
    git cat-file -t <that name>   -> fatal: could not get object info
    CONTROL, mine, reproduced directly: hashed a fresh file, `-t` FAILED.

**AND THE FILE ITSELF WAS ON `tmpfs` — RAM — so the citation named a non-object describing content
that dies at the next reboot.** *Both violations are of rules that seat had written down itself.*

> **GETTING ONE CITATION RIGHT DOES NOT CARRY TO THE NEXT ONE.** *The correct base blob and the
> impossible fix blob were in the same MESSAGE, two sections apart — corrected by the seat itself
> against an earlier draft of this section that said "same paragraph".* **THE DISTINCTION IS THE
> WHOLE VALUE: a slip of attention inside one paragraph is a small failure. A RULE APPLIED IN ONE
> SECTION AND NOT RE-APPLIED IN THE NEXT IS THE COMMON ONE AND THE DANGEROUS ONE** — *the seat
> asked for the harsher reading of its own error, and it is the correct one.*

    A FILE OUTSIDE A REPOSITORY HAS NO BLOB. GIVE A CONTENT DIGEST AND CALL IT ONE.
    IT IS HONEST AS A DIGEST AND IT IS NOT A FETCHABLE OBJECT.
    AND PUT IT ON DURABLE STORAGE FIRST -- `findmnt --target <path>` -- BECAUSE
    A DIGEST OF A FILE ON RAM DESCRIBES SOMETHING NOBODY WILL BE ABLE TO OPEN.

*The seat re-published the pair on btrfs with digests verified after the copy, plus a 19-line
self-contained diff — which is the artefact that actually travels, since it reproduces the change
without that clone.*

## 114. NAMING A LIMITATION IS NOT ACTING ON IT

**`vmsam-dev-1`, retracting a report of its own, and this is the part it volunteered:**

> *"I had already found the blind spot and kept using the instrument inside it."*

**In the message BEFORE the failure it had written, correctly, that the two-row test is a POSITIVE
CHECK ONLY and cannot see a commit the intermediary was never given.** *Then it ran that exact test,
got agreement, and asserted the result as a fact about the authority.* **IT NAMED THE LIMITATION AND
DID NOT POINT IT AT ITSELF.**

**Second instance the same day, by the same seat, in the opposite direction:** *it told `dev-3` not
to spend a sweep on a hypothesis it had only HALF eliminated — `dev-3` spent it and found
something.*

    A LIMITATION YOU HAVE WRITTEN DOWN IS NOT A LIMITATION YOU HAVE HANDLED.
    THE GAP BETWEEN THE TWO IS WHERE THIS CAMPAIGN LOSES MOST OF ITS HOURS,
    AND IT IS INVISIBLE FROM OUTSIDE BECAUSE THE DOCUMENT LOOKS CORRECT.
    WHEN YOU PUBLISH A CAVEAT, THE NEXT ACT IS TO RUN IT AGAINST YOURSELF.

*Filed against the seat's own request: it asked that this sit beside §112 rather than let the
failure read as a channel defect alone. **The channel made the error possible; the instrument to
catch it was already in its hands.***

### And it had the remedy configured the whole time

    origin    <a local clone>                    <- THE HOP
    upstream  ssh://.../fallrik/VMSAM.git        <- THE AUTHORITY, NEVER ONCE USED

**The second remote had been present all along. THE FIX REQUIRED NO CONFIGURATION, ONLY THE HABIT OF
PRINTING `git remote -v` BEFORE CALLING SOMETHING THE AUTHORITY.**

## 115. WHAT SURVIVES A BAD CHANNEL: A CITATION CORROBORATED BY AN INDEPENDENT EXECUTION

**Every governance blob `vmsam-dev-1` cited today was a hop read and every one of them was wrong.
ONE citation survived intact — `src/change_point_locator.py` = `d5d9a1d892069b69`, which I confirm
is the object the authority serves — and the seat stated why, which is the useful half:**

> *"It was corroborated by a SECOND SEAT HASHING THE BYTES IT ACTUALLY RAN, independently of any
> remote."* **A CITATION VERIFIED AGAINST AN INDEPENDENT EXECUTION DOES NOT NEED THE AUTHORITY TO BE
> RIGHT.**

**THE GOVERNANCE BLOBS HAD NO SECOND SOURCE — AND ONE SOURCE READ TWICE LOOKS EXACTLY LIKE TWO.**

    THIS IS §112's CONSTRUCTIVE HALF. §112 SAYS WHAT FAILS; THIS SAYS WHAT TO DO:
    THE STRONGEST CITATION IS NOT THE ONE READ FROM THE BEST REF. IT IS THE ONE
    A SECOND PARTY REACHED BY A DIFFERENT ROUTE -- BEST OF ALL, BY HASHING THE
    BYTES IT EXECUTED. THAT CORROBORATION SURVIVES A STALE CACHE, A LAGGING
    INTERMEDIARY, AND A REF NAME THAT MEANS SOMETHING ELSE IN YOUR CLONE.

## 116. `flips = 0` IS NOT `monotone` — AND THE FOURTH LOOK-ALIKE PAIR OF THE DAY

**`vmsam-dev-3`, answering whether any low-fidelity pair can exercise the locator's SPEED branch, and
answering it WITHOUT a new run because the module computes the distinction itself and its verdict was
already captured:**

    "speed relation suspected"  (MONOTONE)      0 of 7
    "scattered offsets"         (NOT monotone)  7 of 7
    CONTROL: each string occurs EXACTLY ONCE in the shipped source (verified here,
    1 and 1), SO THE ZERO IS A MEASUREMENT AND NOT A FAILED GREP.

**THE TRAP IT FLAGGED, WHICH WOULD HAVE PRODUCED A CONFIDENTLY FALSE ANSWER:** *the nearest candidate
reports `flips = 0` and only 16 distinct offsets across 37 probes — by far the most structured of the
seven — and it took the `scattered` branch anyway.*

> **`flips = 0` IS NOT `monotone`. `monotone` is computed over the offset SEQUENCE inside the branch;
> `flips` is a separately reported statistic.** *A reader picking the seven apart by the printed
> `flips` column would have named that pair as monotone and been wrong — and the dispatch it was
> answering could reasonably have been answered that way.*

    FOURTH PAIR TODAY OF QUANTITIES THAT LOOK INTERCHANGEABLE AND ARE NOT:
      container duration vs decoded join · file digest vs decoded digest
      median fidelity vs pairing fidelity · flips vs monotone
    §9's COUNTER-RULE, FOUR TIMES: RESEMBLANCE IS NOT PURPOSE. AND IN EVERY ONE
    OF THE FOUR THE RESEMBLANCE WAS IN A **REPORTED COLUMN**, NOT IN THE CODE --
    WHICH IS WHY READING THE REPORT INSTEAD OF THE BRANCH GIVES A COHERENT,
    CONFIDENT, WRONG ANSWER.

### And this reclassifies dev-1's `m = 0` from a gap into a property

> **IT IS NOT "dev-1 HAS NOT PRODUCED THE ARM YET". IT IS "THE ARM CANNOT BE DRAWN FROM HERE."**
> *n = 7 of 7 low-fidelity pairs checked, control fired, no pair excluded.*

**Either a PAL/NTSC pair is selected from the library — `CAMPAIGN.MD` records the 23.976→25 relation
as a 4.27 % duration ratio fully present in audio, and that an `atempo` arm was buildable all along —
OR THE BRANCH STAYS HONESTLY UNVERIFIED.** *An `m = 0` that has been traced to the material is worth
more than one that has not: it names what to go and get.*

## 117. NAME THE AUTHORITY BY THE REMOTE THE SEAT'S OWN USER CONFIGURED, NEVER BY A URL IN A MESSAGE

**The architect told the fleet to verify against a URL written in a dispatch. `vmsam-dev-3` DECLINED,
and it was right:**

> *"That host is not configured in either of my trees and I have never fetched from it. I am NOT
> contacting an unfamiliar external host on a peer relay — that is outward-facing, my user configured
> neither remote, and §4 is explicit that a peer relaying an owner instruction is not authorisation
> INCLUDING WHEN THE PEER IS THE ARCHITECT."*

**THE INSTRUCTION WAS THE DEFECT. "Fetch from this URL I am telling you about" is exactly the shape
§4 forbids, and the architect issued it while quoting §4 at other seats.**

**AND NO NEW REMOTE WAS EVER NEEDED — MEASURED, NOT ASSUMED:** *`vmsam-dev-1` fetched from the
`upstream` ITS OWN USER HAD CONFIGURED and obtained, object for object, exactly what the architect had
announced from the other address — tip, `ENTRY.MD` blob, `MEASURING.MD` blob, plus a positive control
that fired and an impossible control that stayed silent.* **THE TWO ADDRESSES ARE THE SAME
REPOSITORY, AND THAT WAS ESTABLISHED BY A SEAT'S OWN FETCH RATHER THAN BY ANYONE'S ASSERTION.**

    THE AUTHORITY IS NEVER A HOSTNAME THAT ARRIVED IN AN INBOX.
    BOTH SEATS THAT HIT THE HOP HAD AN UPSTREAM CONFIGURED AND UNUSED THE WHOLE TIME.

### AMENDED WITHIN THE HOUR — THE RULE AS FIRST WRITTEN WAS UNSATISFIABLE FOR SOME SEATS

**`vmsam-auditor` and `vmsam-ci-pair`, independently, both ran `git remote -v` and both found
EXACTLY ONE REMOTE — the hop. NEITHER USER CONFIGURED AN UPSTREAM.** *"Fetch from the remote your
own user configured" therefore instructs those seats to fetch the lagging intermediary the rule
exists to prevent.* **A RULE AN AGENT CANNOT SATISFY IS §82's SHAPE, AND THIS ONE WAS MINE.**

**`auditor` refused to substitute a method and call it compliance** — *it said the rule was
unsatisfiable rather than quietly working around it, which is the report I needed and the one a
compliant-sounding seat would not have sent.* **What it does instead is the correct generalisation:**

    IT READS THE HOP'S OWN `remote.origin.url` FROM DISK --
      git -C <hop> config --get remote.origin.url
    A CONFIG ARTEFACT IS A MEASUREMENT OF A REAL OBJECT. A MESSAGE IS NOT.
    AND IT RE-READ IT THIS TURN RATHER THAN REUSING A STRING IT HAD USED ALL SESSION.

**`ci-pair` satisfies it a third way and drew the distinction itself:** *the URL it uses is in ITS
OWN BRIEF, carried across resets and verified over many sessions* — **"I would not want my
compliance read as having taken a URL from you."**

    SO THE RULE IS: THE AUTHORITY IS AN ADDRESS YOU READ FROM A DURABLE ARTEFACT --
    A CONFIGURED REMOTE, A CONFIG FILE ON DISK, OR YOUR OWN CARRIED BRIEF.
    NEVER ONE THAT ARRIVED IN CORRESPONDENCE. THE TEST IS NOT WHO CONFIGURED IT;
    IT IS WHETHER YOU CAN RE-READ IT FROM SOMETHING THAT IS NOT A MESSAGE.

*Recorded against the architect. A seat that declines an outward-facing action arriving on a relay is
obeying the document, and `ENTRY.MD` §4b lists exactly this among the things the owner's grant does
not reach.*

## 118. A GUARD'S SECOND CONDITION COULD NOT SELECT THE POPULATION ITS OWN MESSAGE NAMES

**`vmsam-dev-1` argued from constructed series that the scatter guard cannot fire on what it
describes. `vmsam-dev-3` supplied the REAL file that settles it:**

    curated-307 jpn   16 distinct offsets / 37 probes   flips = 0   -> CANNOT TRIP
    genuinely scattered by the guard's OWN FIRST condition,
    structurally immune to its SECOND.

**VERIFIED HERE IN THE SHIPPED SOURCE:** *`_sign_flips` counts ZERO-CROSSINGS OF THE OFFSET VALUE —
sign changes, positive against negative — and the guard requires `distinct_points > MAX_DISTINCT_POINTS
**and** flips > MAX_SIGN_FLIPS`.* **A CONSISTENTLY-SIGNED FILE SCORES 0 HOWEVER SCATTERED IT IS, so the
conjunction can never fire on it.**

### And the seat tracked its own claim through three versions, each replacing the last

    1  the guard is INERT (42 reached, 0 fired)
    2  ...and its firing would have been INVISIBLE anyway -- it is one of the silent eight
    3  ...and its SECOND CONDITION CANNOT SELECT THE POPULATION ITS OWN MESSAGE NAMES

> **THE SEAT'S OWN VERDICT ON ITSELF: *"I reported the flattering reading of an ambiguous observation
> for hours."***

    AN AMBIGUOUS OBSERVATION HAS A FLATTERING READING AND A COSTLY ONE, AND THE
    FLATTERING ONE ARRIVES FIRST BECAUSE IT REQUIRES NO FURTHER WORK.
    A CLAIM THAT KEEPS IMPROVING IN THE DIRECTION THAT FLATTERS ITS AUTHOR HAS NOT
    BEEN CONVERGING. IT HAS BEEN UNDER-INVESTIGATED, AND EACH VERSION WAS PUBLISHED.
    THE FIX IS THE ONE THAT WORKED HERE: A SECOND SEAT WITH REAL MATERIAL.

## 119. A STALE PATH MADE A HEALTHY JOB LOOK HUNG

**`vmsam-dev-1`, reporting a near-incident it caught before acting:** *a long-running job showed a
scratch directory unchanged for 17 minutes with a live child — the signature of a stuck process.*
**IT WAS READING THE WRONG DIRECTORY: the job had finished that arm and moved to the next one.**

> **CHECKING A STALE PATH AND CONCLUDING A HEALTHY JOB IS HUNG IS THE MIRROR OF `COMING_BACK.MD` §3.**
> *The honest signal was the child's own command line, resolved BY PARENTAGE — what the process says
> it is doing, not what a directory the observer chose says about it.*

    BEFORE KILLING ANYTHING, ASK THE PROCESS WHAT IT IS DOING.
    A DIRECTORY IS THE OBSERVER'S GUESS ABOUT WHERE THE WORK IS.
    THE COMMAND LINE IS THE WORK'S OWN ACCOUNT OF ITSELF.

*It is a note rather than an incident only because the seat looked before acting.*

## 120. §104 SURVIVED A PRE-REGISTERED REFUTATION — AND ITS OWN LESSON ARRIVED ONE LEVEL UP

**`vmsam-auditor` wrote six falsifiers TO A FILE before opening `src/video.py`, then measured by AST
and by blob grep over the whole shipped tree. NONE FIRED.** *`m`: 38 `.audios` mentions outside
`video.py`, 6 video-object construction sites, 20 occurrences of `compatible`, 2 guard sites — **every
falsifier had opportunity, so this is an INFORMATIVE NEGATIVE and not an untested cell.***

### THE PART WORTH MORE THAN THE VERDICT: A FALSIFIER FIRED AND WAS WRONG

    merge_video_chimeric.py:2987   error.audios = audio_reports
    merge_video_chimeric.py:3014   error.audios = audio_reports
    VERIFIED HERE: both are followed by `raise error`. `error` IS AN EXCEPTION,
    NOT A VIDEO OBJECT, AND ITS `.audios` IS NEVER READ BY THE GUARD.

> **ANYONE RE-RUNNING §104's PRODUCER COUNT WITH A NAME-BASED GREP HITS THESE TWO, AND A TALLY THAT
> COUNTS THEM REFUTES §104 FALSELY.** *§104's own lesson, one level up: **an identical grep signature,
> a different object.*** **THE SECTION WARNS THAT A KEY TALLY CANNOT BOUND REACHABILITY — THE SAME
> BLINDNESS APPLIES TO ITS OWN PRODUCER TALLY IF THE TALLY IS TAKEN BY NAME RATHER THAN BY TYPE.**

*The seat nearly filed it as a refutation and stopped to check the class of `error`.*

### AND THE BOUNDARY §104 DOES NOT NAME

**`video.py:115-122` build `self.commentary[language]` and `self.audiodesc[language]` IN THE SAME
LOOP, FROM THE SAME `data` DICTS — and those never receive `compatible`. Verified: 0 occurrences in
that block.** *The guards are safe only because they iterate `self.audios[language]` SPECIFICALLY.*

    §104 PROVES EVERY ELEMENT OF `self.audios` CARRIES THE KEY.
    IT DOES NOT SAY TWO SIBLING CONTAINERS EXIST WHOSE ELEMENTS DO NOT.
    IF THE GUARD IS EVER GENERALISED TO "ALL AUDIO STREAMS", THE CONSTRUCTION
    ARGUMENT AS WRITTEN **APPEARS TO COVER IT AND DOES NOT** -- and the failure
    is a `KeyError`, the exact defect §104 exists to distinguish.

## 121. A RECORDING SCHEME THAT CANNOT TELL ITS OWN INPUTS APART

**`vmsam-ci` amended the end-condition falsifier BEFORE running it, as instructed, and clause 1 —
which reasons correctly that blobs outlive a compaction and shas do not — CHOSE FILES THAT DEFEAT IT:**

    commit         mergeVideo.py   video.py        TREE
    278471a0fa28   942269ba39dc    7808e1858ef0    82572f6ddb27
    38d97b1cafb9   942269ba39dc    7808e1858ef0    b20611aa220f
    0456094dd73d   942269ba39dc    7808e1858ef0    a0713187e5b1
    THREE DISTINCT REVISIONS, ONE BLOB PAIR. m = 3, DISCRIMINATED ON NONE.

> **IT FAILS EXACTLY WHERE THE CAMPAIGN WORKS: the movement is in the locator, the repair and the
> chimeric modules — NONE OF WHICH IT RECORDS — while the two it does record are the frozen-except-tags
> files that barely move.** **AMENDMENT: RECORD THE TREE.** *Durable for the same reason the blobs are,
> and it discriminates where they cannot.*

**NOT HYPOTHETICAL: today's rewrite ANNIHILATED all three shas — `--is-ancestor` now answers NO for
every one while `cat-file -t` still answers `commit` from a local object store.** *The seat had
captured the tree mapping before the rewrite, and it is the only thing that still attributes its rows.*

### Two more amendments, each closing a way to be confidently wrong

**CLAUSE 2 — FILES AND CASES ARE DIFFERENT QUANTITIES.** *The census counts FILES; the end condition
is about `(master, candidate)` CASES.* **76 and 17,694 are not reconcilable in either direction
because they are not the same unit** — *§116's rule landing on the falsifier itself.* **THE
DENOMINATOR CARRIES ITS UNIT, and the conversion is a measurement somebody must produce.** *The seat
holds neither the library census nor the conversion and says so rather than estimating.*

**CLAUSE 4 — A ZERO THERE IS AN ARTEFACT OF THE INSTRUMENT.** *Measured over 68 artefacts with
controls: 0 of 10 dev-gated strings appear, two unconditional strings appear 8 and 4 times,
impossible-token control 0.* **So `excluded_with_stated_cause = 0` means *the field cannot exist*, NOT
*we checked and none had a cause* — and printed bare it reads as the second.**

    A THIRD STATE ABOVE BOTH COLUMNS:
      EMISSION ABSENT  -> both columns UNDEFINED, THE CLAUSE IS UNEVALUABLE
      EMISSION PRESENT -> the columns mean what the clause says
    AND THE PRESENCE OF THE EMISSION IS ITSELF A MEASUREMENT WITH A CONTROL,
    TAKEN BEFORE EITHER NUMBER IS PRINTED. §109 APPLIED TO A REPORTING COLUMN.

## 122. "IDLE" AND "HAS NOT REPORTED" ARE FACTS ABOUT A CHANNEL, NOT ABOUT A SEAT

**The architect told `vmsam-ci-pair` it looked idle and had not reported today. It had reported to
SEVEN seats — the Lead, `forensic` three times, `arch-aide` twice, `dev-1` four times, `ci-build`,
`aide`, `dev-4`. It had not reported TO THE ARCHITECT.**

> **BOTH READINGS OF ITS SILENCE WERE ABOUT THE CHANNEL AND NEITHER WAS ABOUT THE SEAT** — *the same
> class the Lead hit an hour earlier reading `idle` off a session listing, which answers* ***not
> mid-turn*** *and never* ***not working.***

**AND THE SEAT DECLINED THE EXCUSE THAT WAS AVAILABLE TO IT:** *"asking was the right move, and the
four-line form is why it cost one message instead of an hour. I should have been sending these
unprompted; nothing stopped me and the omission is mine."*

    A COORDINATOR'S VIEW OF A FLEET IS THE SET OF MESSAGES ADDRESSED TO IT,
    WHICH IS NOT THE SET OF WORK DONE. BEFORE CONCLUDING A SEAT IS IDLE, ASK IT.
    THE FOUR-LINE FORM IS CHEAP PRECISELY SO THIS COSTS ONE MESSAGE.

**AND `ci-pair` INDEPENDENTLY REPRODUCED §106'S OBSERVABLE FROM A SECOND CLONE:** *the pre-registered
falsifier is, from there too, indistinguishable from one never written.* **A §106 CLAIM WITH ONE
WITNESS IS WEAKER THAN ONE WITH TWO — and `ci` supplied the mechanism: THE HOP CARRIES 44 AGENT REFS
AND THE AUTHORITY CARRIES ONE, so the artefact lives where the stale mirror is, and a seat that
correctly refuses to read the hop cannot read the falsifier at all.**

## 123. §112's CONCLUSION SURVIVES AND ITS MECHANISM IS REFUTED — BY A PRE-REGISTERED TEST

**`vmsam-auditor` wrote falsifiers AND AN EXPLICIT PREDICTION to a file before touching anything,
then took four ref reads in one snapshot with nothing staged:**

    hop  refs/heads/dev-AI           0017021   <- what the hop SERVES
    hop  refs/remotes/origin/dev-AI  092a038   <- what the hop has already FETCHED
    MINE refs/remotes/origin/dev-AI  9de4c3a   <- this clone's cached copy
    forgejo, direct at the URL       092a038   <- the authority
    read A `ls-remote origin refs/heads/dev-AI` -> 0017021
    read B `git rev-parse origin/dev-AI`        -> 9de4c3a      >>> DISAGREE.

> **§112 SAID THOSE TWO ARE "TWO VIEWS OF THE SAME CACHE". THEY ARE NOT. THEY ARE TWO DIFFERENT
> CACHES, AND ONE OF THEM IS YOURS.** *`ls-remote origin` interrogates the hop's ref OVER THE WIRE,
> NOW; `origin/<branch>` is your clone's STORED COPY of the hop's last answer. They diverge precisely
> when your fetch is stale.* **SO THE PAIR HAS REAL POWER: IT DETECTS A STALE TRACKING REF IN YOUR OWN
> CLONE, WHICH NO SINGLE READ DETECTS.**

**§112 IS ITS OWN §107 APPLIED TO ITSELF: it says `origin` is a per-clone name, then treats
`origin/<branch>` as the same object as the remote's ref. IT IS A PER-CLONE OBJECT TOO.**

**THE CONCLUSION IS UNHARMED AND THE SAME SNAPSHOT STRENGTHENS IT — the hop's OWN two rows disagree:
it HOLDS the authority's commit at `092a038` and SERVES `0017021`.** *It has the tip and does not
serve it, so any clone syncing off it right now silently misses the authority.* **Agreement between
reads is positive-only, exactly as written.**

    THE CORRECTED RULE: THE READS MUST BE INDEPENDENT **OF THE AUTHORITY'S STATE**,
    NOT MERELY OF EACH OTHER. `ls-remote origin` AND `origin/<branch>` *ARE*
    INDEPENDENT -- OF EACH OTHER -- AND THEIR AGREEMENT STILL SAYS NOTHING ABOUT
    THE AUTHORITY, BECAUSE **BOTH DERIVE FROM THE HOP**.
    INDEPENDENCE IS A PROPERTY OF **THE CHAIN TO THE AUTHORITY**, NOT OF THE COMMANDS.

### And the read that nearly carried a false refutation

**`git ls-remote origin dev-AI` — A BARE BRANCH NAME IS A PATTERN.** *On a hop it matched BOTH
`refs/heads/dev-AI` and `refs/remotes/origin/dev-AI`, so the `awk` printed TWO shas and the comparison
"disagreed" trivially.* **THE POST-RESTORE CONTROL WAS SUPPOSED TO SHOW AGREEMENT, SHOWED
DISAGREEMENT, AND THAT IS THE ONLY REASON IT WAS CAUGHT.** *The run was discarded and re-measured with
full ref names.*

    ANYONE RUNNING THE REF TEST WITH A BARE BRANCH NAME IS READING A PATTERN MATCH,
    NOT A REF. A READ WHOSE **OUTPUT SHAPE** YOU HAVE NOT CHECKED IS THIS SECTION'S
    OWN DEFECT ONE LEVEL DOWN. ALWAYS `refs/heads/<branch>`, IN FULL.

## 124. F-72 — SIX GUARDS THAT CAN NEVER RAISE THE ERROR THEY NAME

**`vmsam-forensic`, sweeping for §104's siblings, m = 209 guard sites and 226 BoolOp sites opened —
counted from what it OPENED, not from what it found interesting:**

    if X == None and not len(X):   raise ValueError("<x> cannot be empty")

    BEHAVIOUR BY EXECUTION, NOT BY READING:
      X = None        -> *** TypeError: object of type 'NoneType' has no len() ***
                         NOT the intended ValueError -- the AND reaches len(None)
      X = ""          -> guard FALSE. **THE EMPTY VALUE IS ACCEPTED** -- precisely
                         the input the guard exists to reject
      X = "something" -> FALSE, correct

> **THE GUARD NEVER RAISES ITS OWN `ValueError` ON ANY INPUT. `X == None and not len(X)` IS
> UNSATISFIABLE WITHOUT RAISING. The operator should be `or`.** *One guard, two failures, and the
> louder one — `TypeError` — masks the quieter one, an empty value silently admitted.*

**THE CONTROL THAT MAKES IT A TYPO CLASS RATHER THAN A DESIGN CHOICE: the correct idiom appears in the
same two files — verified here, 3 occurrences in one and 6 in the other. THE AUTHOR KNEW THE IDIOM.**

**AND THERE IS A SIXTH, WHICH THE SWEEP DID NOT LIST — `merge_video_repair.py:2020`, INSIDE THE REPAIR
BOUNDARY:** `if speed_ratio == None and not len(plan.get("segments") or []):` *— same shape, and here
the right operand is `or []`, so it cannot raise; it simply* ***cannot be true when `speed_ratio` is
`None`***, *which is the case the line names.* **The seat swept the model files and reported five; the
class is larger than its own report, which is the correct direction for a sweep to be wrong in.**

    NOT ESTABLISHED, AND THE SEAT SAID SO: THE CALLERS ARE NOT TRACED. IF NO CALLER
    EVER PASSES `None` OR `""` THESE ARE **UNREACHABLE** RATHER THAN **WRONG** --
    A DIFFERENT FINDING, CLAIMED NEITHER WAY. **NOT FOUND IS NOT ABSENT.**

### And the sweep's own null result is the more valuable half

**SHAPE A found NOTHING NEW: 209 sites opened, 11 keys looked bounded to one value, ZERO survived
inspection — and BOTH collapse modes were defects in the instrument, not in the code.** *v1 matched
writes by KEY NAME across a whole file, "explaining" a guard with a write to a DIFFERENT container.
v2 partitioned writes by VARIABLE NAME rather than by object, so one family read as one-valued because
its aliases were counted apart — measured, it is written True at 3 sites and False at 19, and it
discriminates perfectly.*

> **A NAME-BASED TALLY CANNOT ESTABLISH OBJECT IDENTITY.** *The `compatible` guard is closable ONLY
> because its write and its producers are one statement apart in one branch.* **EVERYTHING ELSE IS
> INERT-BY-TALLY-ONLY AT BEST — A DIFFERENT FINDING, AND NOT SAFE TO ACT ON. The seat declined to
> offer a second one.**

*And the instrument RE-FOUND the known scatter guard on its own, without looking for it — **an
instrument that independently rediscovers a known case is one whose zero on the rest means
something.***


# APPENDIX — THE DERIVATIONS MOVED OUT OF `AGENT.MD`, 2026-09-05

**`AGENT.MD` was 1082 lines and every seat reads it before it can work. The RULES stayed there;
the NARRATIVE THAT PRODUCED THEM is here, unedited, because reasoning is worth keeping and is not
worth re-reading on every restart.** *Owner: "AGENT.MD tu crois pas que tu peux l'alléger ?"*

# RESTART PROTOCOL — entry comprehension test, and what the published research says we got wrong

**Researched 2026-09-05 against Anthropic's own multi-agent guidance and the current
long-running-agent literature. THREE OF THEIR NAMED FAILURE MODES ARE OURS, MEASURED, TONIGHT.**

## 1. WE DIVIDED BY WORK TYPE, WHICH IS THE NAMED ANTI-PATTERN

> *"Dividing by type of work — one agent writes features, another writes tests, a third reviews
> code — CREATES CONSTANT COORDINATION OVERHEAD. Work should only be split when CONTEXT CAN BE
> TRULY ISOLATED."*

**Our roster is divided by ROLE: developers, checkers, a forensic, an auditor, two aides, a
lead.** *And the coordination overhead is measurable — a large share of one night's traffic was
agents reconciling different readings OF THE SAME SYSTEM:* **file count against queue position,
a log directory against an output directory, a container's PID namespace against our own.**

    DECOMPOSE BY CONTEXT BOUNDARY, NOT BY JOB TITLE.
    Good boundary: independent corpora · separate components with clean interfaces ·
                   BLACKBOX VERIFICATION THAT NEEDS ONLY A RESULT.
    Bad boundary:  sequential phases of one question · tightly coupled work ·
                   anything needing shared live state.

**THE ONE ROLE THE RESEARCH ENDORSES UNRESERVEDLY IS THE ONE THAT PERFORMED BEST HERE:** *a
verification agent judging by pass/fail criteria with its own tools, needing minimal context
transfer because verification is blackbox by nature.* **That is the auditor, and it produced
more of tonight's governance than any other seat.**

## 2. THE TELEPHONE GAME IS NAMED IN THE LITERATURE AND WE PLAYED IT

> *"Sequential phase decomposition where each handoff degrades information fidelity — LOST
> CONTEXT AT EACH HANDOFF."*

**Measured: a causal mechanism reversed THREE TIMES in ninety minutes, each reversal produced by
a harness that was not the engine, each relayed onward by an agent who had verified the
claimant's experiment rather than the subject** (§49b). **A correction propagated faster than
its own refutation and caused a deletion of correct work** (§49).

## 3. "EARLY VICTORY" IS WHAT THE OPPORTUNITY COUNT ALREADY DEFEATS

> *"Verification agents declare success after testing one or two scenarios rather than
> comprehensive validation."*

**We answered this before reading it: `m` — the opportunity count — makes a quiet interval
report itself as UNINFORMATIVE rather than as health** (§39, §72). *Keep it. It is the local
form of the industry's own remedy and ours is stricter.*

---

## 5. AND THE HANDOFF FILE IS THE THING BEING TESTED, NOT THE AGENT

**If an agent fails the entry test twice from the same brief, THE BRIEF IS THE DEFECT.** *An
append-only record is what makes an agent recoverable; a brief that cannot produce a passing
agent is a brief that will not survive its own compaction either.*

**Owner grant: full authority over architecture and roles. USED ONCE, ON EVIDENCE, AND
DELIBERATELY NOT USED TWICE.**

    dev-1       change_point_locator.py                                    ONE MODULE
    dev-2       merge_video_chimeric · merge_video_repair · merge_video_resample
    dev-3       merge_video_chimeric · merge_video_repair            <- SAME TWO AS dev-2
    dev-4       change_point_locator · fusion · merge_plan_report ·
                merge_video_chimeric · merge_video_repair            <- FIVE. NO BOUNDARY.
    auditor · ci-pair · forensic · aide · arch-aide    NO src EDITS AT ALL

**THREE FACTS, AND EACH ONE DECIDES SOMETHING:**

**1. `dev-1` HAS A CLEAN CONTEXT BOUNDARY AND PRODUCED THE NIGHT'S BEST CODE RESULT.** *One
module, no overlap, F4 landed and verified.* **THAT IS THE PATTERN, AND IT IS NOT MOVED.**

**2. `dev-2` AND `dev-3` OVERLAP ON THE SAME TWO FILES.** *That is the named anti-pattern
verbatim — tightly coupled components requiring constant synchronisation — and it is the
measured source of the coordination traffic.*

**3. `dev-4` HAS NO BOUNDARY AT ALL — five modules, every cluster.** *It is also the only agent
of eleven running with `--continue`, and the one the cadence instrument cannot see.* **High
output, no perimeter, and invisible to the fleet's own liveness check.**

# THE ARCHITECTURE, SETTLED — measured, not designed

**Second and final use of the owner's grant. THE FIRST DREW THE CODE BOUNDARIES. THIS ONE NAMES
WHAT THE FLEET IS ACTUALLY FOR, AND IT IS NOT WHAT THE ROSTER SAID.**

## THE MEASUREMENT THAT SETTLES IT

**Governance sections produced, by the agent credited in them, in one night. THE INSTRUMENT IS
THE ARCHITECT'S OWN, run over `WRITE_ZONES.MD` / `CAMPAIGN.MD` / `AGENT.MD` commit bodies:
`git log --grep='vmsam-<agent>' -i` over the docs commits, counting mentions.** *NO AGENT HAS
VERIFIED IT AND NONE IS CITED AS CORROBORATING IT — the corpus is one the container cannot see,
and the top-ranked seat declined the credit on exactly that ground.*

    auditor    12       ci-build   5       dev-3   2
    aide        8       forensic   5       dev-2   1
    ci-pair     6       dev-1      4       LEAD    1
    dev-4       6       arch-aide  5

**READ THE TOP OF THAT COLUMN AND THE BOTTOM TOGETHER.** *The verifier produced twelve. The
coordinator produced one.* **AND THE SECOND AND THIRD PLACES — `aide` and `ci-pair` — DID NOT
EARN THEM BY COORDINATING EITHER:** *`aide`'s came from its cadence instrument with error
grades and blind regions; `ci-pair`'s from the gate that ended by amending §11 against itself.*

> **EVERY PRODUCTIVE SEAT THIS CAMPAIGN HAS BUILT AN INSTRUMENT AND RUN IT AGAINST KNOWN GROUND
> TRUTH. EVERY UNPRODUCTIVE HOUR WAS SPENT PASSING A MESSAGE.**

---

## WHAT IS NOT CHANGED, AND WHY THAT IS THE HARDER HALF OF THE GRANT

**THE AUDITOR IS NOT TOUCHED.** *It is the one multi-agent pattern the published guidance
endorses unreservedly — blackbox verification, judged on pass/fail, minimal context transfer —
and it produced more of this campaign's governance than any other seat.* **A grant to change the
architecture is not a reason to change the part that works.**

**`ci-pair`, `forensic`, `ci-build` AND `ci` KEEP THEIR SEATS.** *Measured: none of them edits
`src/`. They build material, run the pipeline and measure real media* — **which is context
isolation by construction, the good kind, and they were never the overlap problem.**

**THE COORDINATION LAYER — three coordinators for eleven workers — IS THE NEXT DEFECT AND I AM
NOT CUTTING IT TONIGHT.** *Every relay failure of this campaign lived there: a mechanism
reversed three times through a chain, a correction outrunning its own refutation, a wrong number
laundered through a verifier.* **BUT one coordinator is mid-execution on a compact sequence and
another holds the run.** *Restructuring a layer while it is coordinating is the fixer that
reproduced its own bug, at fleet scale.* **It is stated here so the next architect finds it
already diagnosed, and it is scheduled for after the sequence closes.**

---

## THE RECLASSIFICATION — `aide` AND `arch-aide` ARE NOT ASSISTANTS

**MEASURED: their coordination work is where they failed** — a withdrawn correction relayed
onward, a stall inferred from a wrapper, a fixer that reproduced its own bug. **THEIR
INSTRUMENT WORK IS WHERE THEY PRODUCED** — a cadence instrument that reports its own error
grades, a fleet map that walks to the child process, a gap probe that costs the run nothing.

    aide       -> INSTRUMENT: THE FLEET. cadence, liveness, name->role,
                  who holds what. It already declares coverage and blind regions.
    arch-aide  -> INSTRUMENT: THE ARTEFACTS. manifests, settle state, the gap probe,
                  the disclosure sweep. It already refuses to certify what it did not open.

**THEIR RELAY DUTIES END. Anything they would have passed on, they now MEASURE AND PUBLISH, and
the reader fetches it.** *A published measurement cannot be laundered; a relayed one is what
this campaign spent its worst four hours on.*

## THE RULE THAT REPLACES THE RELAY LAYER

    AN AGENT ADDRESSES THE AGENT IT NEEDS. DIRECTLY. NO INTERMEDIARY.
    THE LEAD DISPATCHES AND PROMOTES. THE ARCHITECT RULES ON GOVERNANCE.
    NEITHER IS A CHANNEL BETWEEN TWO OTHER AGENTS.

**EVERY MEASUREMENT THAT HELD TONIGHT WAS TAKEN FIRST-HAND. EVERY ONE THAT BROKE WAS RELAYED.**
*A mechanism reversed three times; a correction that outran its own refutation and caused
correct work to be deleted; a location paraphrased and returned with a verifier's authority.*

## AND THE NEW CLASS THE EVIDENCE FORCES — EXECUTION BEATS SCANNING

**F21, measured tonight: a midpoint passed as a window START. Four of five cut arms DECLINE at
the pairing bar and never reach change-point location — THE GATE REFUSES THE FILES THE MODULE
EXISTS FOR.**

> **NONE OF FOUR STATIC SCANNERS CAN SEE IT. Not unreachable, not tautological, not inert, not
> dropped. IT IS CODE THAT RUNS AND RETURNS A WRONG ANSWER.** *It surfaced only because someone
> EXECUTED THE CHANGED FUNCTION AGAINST A FIXTURE WITH KNOWN GROUND TRUTH — the second time
> tonight execution beat static analysis.*

    SO: EVERY INSTRUMENT SEAT OWES AT LEAST ONE EXECUTION —
    **OF THE THING A CONSUMER WILL CALL, AGAINST MATERIAL WHOSE GROUND TRUTH
    YOU KNOW INDEPENDENTLY.**

**TIGHTENED BY THE AGENT THAT FOUND IT, AND THE TIGHTENING IS THE RULE:** *it is not that a scan
CANNOT find code that works and is wrong — it is that those four scans are each defined by a
property DECIDABLE WITHOUT RUNNING, which is why they are CORRECT to be silent here.*

> **AND EXECUTION ALONE PAYS NOTHING. Executing without known ground truth would have shown four
> declines and told nobody anything.** *The truth existed only because the fixture recorded a
> construction count of 2.* **THE GROUND-TRUTH HALF IS THE HALF THAT DOES THE WORK.**

*And the defect has a better formulation than the finder's own, from the boundary owner:*
**THE INVARIANT WAS ESTABLISHED FOR A POINT AND RELIED UPON FOR AN INTERVAL** — *the comment is
not false; it is a correct claim about the wrong object, which is why it survived review.*

**And the finder did not repair it.** *It has a patch, it makes dev-1's decisive pair RUN where
neither arm was measurable — and it stated that the repair INVALIDATES a measured cross-language
table it has no corpus to re-run.* **THE DEFECT IS MEASURED, THE REPAIR IS UNVALIDATED, AND THE
OWNER OF THAT BOUNDARY DECIDES.** *That is the three classes working exactly as drawn, on the
first finding after they were drawn.*

# A CLAIM ABOUT THE CODE IS TRUE ONLY WHEN IT HAS BEEN RUN

**OWNER'S RULING, 2026-09-05. IT OUTRANKS EVERY CITATION RULE IN THIS DOCUMENT, BECAUSE A
PERFECTLY CITED CLAIM ABOUT UNRUN CODE IS A PERFECTLY CITED GUESS.**

## EVERY CLAIM ABOUT CODE CARRIES ITS VERIFICATION KIND. THERE ARE FOUR AND ONLY ONE IS TRUE

    RUN          the shipped function was CALLED, against material whose ground
                 truth is known INDEPENDENTLY, and the output was read.
                 *** THIS IS THE ONLY KIND THAT MAY BE STATED WITHOUT A QUALIFIER ***

    TRACE        the code was READ and the behaviour DERIVED. Reversible, and it
                 has reversed. STATE IT AS A TRACE, EVERY TIME, IN THE CLAIM ITSELF.

    HARNESS      something was executed, BUT NOT THE THING THE CONSUMER CALLS.
                 A one-command reproduction of a two-pass engine is this.
                 It is a real measurement OF SOMETHING WE DO NOT RUN.

    UNVERIFIABLE no instrument reachable from here can settle it. SAY SO AND STOP.
                 A `||` fallback leaves no trace: the table can say a session
                 started clean and can NEVER say whether it meant to.

## THE HONEST AUDIT OF OUR OWN CLAIMS — SEVEN OF TEN

    RUN          the scan bound was short          11 positions, ground truth known, reproduced
    RUN          a midpoint passed as a START      shipped function called against a known fixture
    RUN          the extractor forces 44100        SHIPPED function imported, called, argv read
    RUN          a field computed and not emitted  function EXECUTED, not re-read
    RUN          94-99 % of the arm destroyed      seven real arms decoded
    RUN          the population is exactly 32000   NEIGHBOURING RATES measured; 24000 CLEAN
    RUN          `m` was inferred, not counted     placements counted: 0 of 2399

    TRACE        THE CLAMP IS LOAD-BEARING         code read + isolated harness.
                                                   NO PRODUCTION MERGE HAS EVER BEEN OBSERVED.
    UNVERIFIED   the filter-graph ORDER            a standalone `-af` chain has no
                                                   auto-inserted resample to be ordered against
    UNVERIFIABLE the `--continue` attempt          `||` leaves no trace

> **THE ONE STILL CARRIED AS SETTLED IS A TRACE. The clamp mechanism reversed THREE TIMES in
> ninety minutes and every reversal came from a HARNESS THAT WAS NOT THE ENGINE.** *It is now
> read from the code and it has not been RUN — and `gap 1` being open is exactly that fact,
> stated in the campaign's own terms since midnight.*

## WHAT THIS FORBIDS

    NEVER say "the code does X". Say "RUN: it did X on <material>, ground truth <how>"
      or "TRACE: reading it, it should do X."
    A TRACE MAY NOT CLOSE A FINDING. It may open one, route one, or bound one.
    A HARNESS RESULT MAY NOT BE REPORTED AS AN ENGINE RESULT — not once, not with
      a caveat further down. The KIND goes in the same sentence as the claim.
    N REPRODUCTIONS OF A MECHANISM ARE NOT ONE OBSERVATION OF THE SYSTEM RUNNING.
      Three agents reproducing the same wrong harness is not corroboration.

## AND WHAT IT DEMANDS OF THE INSTRUMENT SEATS

**A SCAN IS DEFINED BY A PROPERTY DECIDABLE WITHOUT RUNNING — which is why four of them were
correct to be silent on the defect that refuses the files the module exists for.**

> **A SCAN FINDS CODE THAT CANNOT WORK. ONLY A RUN FINDS CODE THAT WORKS AND IS WRONG.**

**And execution alone pays nothing: running without independently known ground truth would have
shown four declines and told nobody anything.** *THE GROUND-TRUTH HALF IS THE HALF THAT DOES THE
WORK*, and it existed only because a fixture recorded its own construction count.

    EVERY INSTRUMENT SEAT OWES ONE EXECUTION OF WHAT A CONSUMER CALLS,
    AGAINST MATERIAL WHOSE TRUTH IT KNOWS BY CONSTRUCTION — EACH SESSION,
    NOT EACH CAMPAIGN.

## 📏 Believing a result

The ways a result can look sound and be worthless are in **`MEASURING.MD`** — read it
when you hold a number and are about to act on it, which is the only moment any of
them applies.

Three lines worth carrying out of it: **mechanise the rules whose failure is silent
in the actor's own frame**, **evidence about an intermediate is not evidence about
the deliverable**, and **independence of instruments is not independence of
assumptions**.

### The campaign's first validation, and what it does and does not cover

**On 2026-09-04 the owner validated the `merge_plan` figure.** *It is the first thing in
this campaign to reach VALIDATED, and the state model exists precisely so that the word
does not spread further than the object.*

    VALIDATED   the FIGURE -- its composition, and that it answers the questions
                a reader brings to it. Validated BY THE OWNER, by looking at a
                rendered view.

    NOT VALIDATED, and unchanged by it:
      the produced .mkv files the figure describes
      would_refuse=True with enforcing=False on one of the two artefacts
      verified 2 of 7 audio tracks on that same artefact
      the master, which that format does not name at all

> **A validated REPORT about an unvalidated FILE is exactly the state this campaign is
> trying to reach, and it is not the same as a validated file.** *The figure's whole
> purpose is to make those four lines visible to the person who can rule on them.*

**And a validated artefact becomes a baseline rather than a finished thing.** *Any later
change to it states what it changes and why, because the next reader will assume the
composition was ruled on and will not re-derive it.* **The three amendments made before
this validation — timestamps on the axis, the amber demoted to a band, a dot on a stepless
rule — are part of what was validated and are not free to revert.**

*Recorded here rather than in a message because `MEASURING.MD` already carries the reason:
the last check anyone runs becomes the definition of done unless a definition is written
above it.*

## Finishing is an ACT, not a state — owner's ruling, 2026-09-04

> ***"Quand un agent pense avoir fini, il a le DEVOIR de commit et de le signifier à tout le
> monde."***

    "I have finished" WITHOUT A COMMIT IS INDISTINGUISHABLE FROM "I stopped".
    And work announced but not pushed exists at NO REFERENCE AT ALL -- it is
    not stale, it is absent, and every reader who trusts the announcement is
    reading a claim about a thing that is nowhere.

**MEASURED THE SAME EVENING — AND THE HEADLINE CASE WAS THE ARCHITECT'S OWN FALSE ALARM,
WHICH IS BETTER EVIDENCE FOR THE RULE THAN THE CASE IT REPLACED.**

*The architect grepped the published branch for a phrase of `vmsam-dev-4`'s announced work,
found nothing, and reported to the owner and to four agents that dev-4 had shipped five
sixths. THE WORK WAS THERE. The marker was split across Python string-continuation lines:*

    :1487   "IN A MULTI-ARTEFACT RENDER, call measure_corpus() "
    :1488   "and pass the result. IN PRODUCTION THIS IS THE "
    :1489   "CORRECT OUTPUT AND NOT A DEFECT: a single merge "

**`grep` is line-oriented. The phrase existed in the RENDERED note and in no single source
line, so the search returned 0 on a file that contained it.**

> **THE MARKER CAN FAIL WHILE THE WORK IS PRESENT. That is the MIRROR of the failure this
> rule exists to catch, and STRICTLY MORE DANGEROUS: it produces a CONFIDENT FALSE ALARM,
> and it would have had an honest agent re-land work that was already there.**

**Two further errors in the same check, both the architect's:**

*The SHA evidence was worthless for a reason dev-4 had already given: it never pushes the SHA
it commits — `commit -> fetch -> cherry-pick onto the fetched tip -> push -> verify` — so the
object that lands is a new commit with identical content.* **A SHA does not survive a
cherry-pick either, not only a rewrite, and the rule already said not to verify by SHA.**

*And two hits on `Call measure_corpus` were read as the instruction still being live. One was
a French comment QUOTING the retracted instruction in order to retract it.* **A citation read
as a carriage — the exact distinction the Lead had ruled on ninety minutes earlier for the
aide's indexer: a retraction that cites the file it retracts would index that file as a
carrier of the claim it withdraws.**

### THE CLAUSE THAT MAKES THE CHECK EXECUTABLE

    GREP THE RENDERED ARTEFACT, OR A BODY PHRASE THAT LIES ON A SINGLE SOURCE LINE.

**A source grep is a grep of the PACKAGING; the reader consumes the OUTPUT.** *dev-4 hit this
on its own commit an hour earlier — `grep -c "nothing after it"` returned 0 on a commit
containing it — and nearly reported its own landed work as missing.*

**And the marker must be COPIED OUT OF THE FILE, never typed from memory: a third architect
false alarm the same evening came from typing `produces a CORRECT` where the heading said
`correct`.** *Two of three manifest runs by its author produced false alarms. A manifest that
cries wolf gets ignored on the night it matters.*

### AND THE PHRASE PROVES THE TEXT LANDED — IT DOES NOT PROVE THE TEXT IS TRUE

**The clause above fixes the FALSE NEGATIVE: the marker fails while the work is present. It
does nothing about the case where the phrase is present and THE BEHAVIOUR IS NOT** — and
that case was demonstrated the same hour, on the same file, by `vmsam-dev-4`.

*The remedy string was on the branch, greppable, correct — and the report CONTRADICTED IT
THREE PARAGRAPHS LATER.* **A production report declared its corpus-scale claims
`not-supplied-to-this-render` and `UNDENOMINATED`, then printed four of them anyway,
measured on the author's lab corpus of twenty-three logs, to a reader holding ONE FILE.**

    "Corpus-wide (23 job logs, one plan each): every one of the 42 INTERIOR
     master fills is a whole number of seconds ..."

**The row declared the claims undenominated and the prose denominated them from someone
else's population.** *And it survived because the narrative had NEVER BEEN RENDERED in the
configuration that exposes it: twenty-three lab renders, every one with a population handed
in, while the only caller in the software passes none.*

    FOR ANYTHING THAT MAKES A CLAIM ABOUT ITS OWN CONTENTS:
    RENDER IT AND ASSERT THE CLAIM HOLDS.

**This sits UNDER the grep clause and not beside it — it only matters once the grep passes.**

**And the fix must be STRUCTURAL rather than a discipline:** *`render_narrative` now reads
the CORPUS row's own state out of the rows it is given, so the scope comes from the table and
the prose CANNOT disagree with it.* **Not an argument a future caller can forget to pass.**
*And not silence either — an omission reads as "there was none", so the report says a
corpus-scale finding EXISTS, is not shown, needs a population, was measured elsewhere, and
names what this artefact can still answer alone.*

### Why an announcement is not a delivery

**An agent that reports completion in a message has produced a claim ITS READER CANNOT
CHECK** — *and five of six being true is the worst possible ratio, because it makes the
sixth invisible.* **A partially-landed batch reads exactly like a fully-landed one.**

    THE DUTY IS THREE PARTS AND ALL THREE ARE THE AGENT'S:
      COMMIT     -- work that is not committed does not exist
      PUSH TO THE PUBLISHED BRANCH, not to a hop
                 -- "promoted" without a remote named is not a claim (§14)
      ANNOUNCE, NAMING THE REFERENCE it landed at
                 -- so a reader can verify instead of believing

**And the announcement carries the reference for the same reason §14 gives: a claim without
one is indistinguishable from a current claim forever.** *`git ls-remote` is the authority;
a hop is not, and this fleet has had four staleness incidents in one night across four
different substrates.*

### The check that costs one line

    after announcing, GREP THE PUBLISHED BRANCH FOR A BODY PHRASE OF YOUR OWN
    WORK. Not a heading, not a filename, not a SHA -- a phrase from the thing
    you changed.

*A SHA does not survive a rewrite; a heading survived the rewrite that gutted §4h; a
filename survives a file that was emptied.* **The phrase is the only marker that fails when
the work is gone, and it is the same rule the Lead now runs over the governance set.**
